Abstract: The convergence of the Metaverse and Large Language Model (LLM)-based AI agent is catalyzing a shift toward autonomous, immersive, and personalized pedagogical frameworks in medical education. This paper presents a novel agentic AI-driven immersive simulation specifically designed for High Dose Rate (HDR) vaginal cylinder (VC) brachytherapy in cancer care. By integrating Virtual Reality (VR) and mobile computing, the system establishes a high-fidelity, risk-free environment that allows trainees to master complex procedural skills without the facility or safety constraints posed by physical anatomy or live radioactive sources. A core contribution of this work is the seamless integration of a knowledge-aware assistant leveraging Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to ground agent interactions in authoritative clinical guidelines. This architecture also enables an interactive agent to provide natural language interfaces and hands-free, real-time guidance during intricate medical maneuvers. We validate the proposed system through a prototype deployment comprising a Meta Quest 3 interface linked to a local GPU-accelerated AI backend, demonstrating a feasible architecture for HDR brachytherapy simulation. Experimental results indicate that the system maintains suitable end-to-end latency and high context precision, answer completeness, and relevance in the RAG-enhanced pedagogical support.

</details>


### [29] [Janus: An Algorithm-Evaluator Co-Evolution Framework for LLM-Driven Discovery under Expensive Evaluation Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08189)
*Ximeng Liu,Qianlong Wang,Yingming Mao,Annan Li,Yatao Li,Shizhen Zhao,Jianmin Wu,Dawei Yin,Dou Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Janus是一个利用LLM协同进化目标程序和可执行代理评估器的框架，用于解决科学和工程设计中评估成本高昂的问题。通过利用LLM的领域知识生成任务特定的评估器程序并用真实结果校准，同时采用推广对齐目标和区域条件化评估器组合来缓解分布偏移。在五个科学和工程设计任务中，Janus以59.1%更少的真实评估达到了基线最终改进的99%，实现了更高的最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的程序发现依赖快速评估反馈，但许多科学和工程任务需要高保真模拟、硬件执行或物理实验，每次评估成本极高。虽然廉价的替代评估器可以降低成本，但固定替代评估器容易受到搜索引起的分布偏移影响，且难以从稀疏且有搜索偏置的标签中可靠拟合。因此需要一种能够适应分布变化且能有效利用稀缺标签的替代评估方法。

Method: Janus框架包含以下核心机制：(1) 利用LLM中的领域知识生成任务特定的评估器程序，并用真实结果进行校准以解决标签稀缺问题；(2) 目标程序与评估器协同进化以缓解分布偏移；(3) 使用推广对齐目标选择评估器，维护区域条件化的评估器组合并进行在线信用更新；(4) 代理预测仅用于候选排序优先，真实验证仍是将候选加入目标程序种群或更新当前最优解的必要条件。

Result: 在五个科学和工程设计任务上，Janus相比仅进化目标程序的匹配基线，在真实评估预算下获得了更大的最优改进曲线下面积和更高的最终性能。平均而言，Janus以59.1%更少的真实评估达到基线最终改进的99%。进化后的代理评估器在排名候选程序方面比其种子版本更准确。

Conclusion: Janus将评估器引导的LLM程序发现从支持廉价、可扩展反馈的任务拓展到真实评估稀缺且昂贵的科学领域，证明了协同进化的代理评估器能够有效降低真实评估成本同时保持甚至提升最终发现质量。

Abstract: LLM-driven program discovery relies on rapid evaluator feedback, but many scientific and engineering tasks require high-fidelity simulations, hardware execution, or physical experiments, making each evaluation expensive. Cheap surrogate evaluators can reduce this cost, yet fixed surrogates are vulnerable to search-induced distribution shift and are difficult to fit reliably from sparse, search-biased labels. We introduce Janus, a framework that uses LLMs to co-evolve target programs and executable proxy evaluators. To address label scarcity, Janus leverages domain knowledge encoded in LLMs to generate task-specific evaluator programs and calibrates them using real outcomes. To mitigate distribution shift, Janus evolves evaluators alongside target programs, selects them using a promotion-aligned objective, and maintains region-conditioned portfolios with online credit updates. Because proxy predictions remain fallible, Janus uses them only to prioritize candidates and requires real validation before candidates can enter the target-program population or update the incumbent. Across five scientific and engineering design tasks, Janus achieves a larger area under the best-so-far improvement curve over the real-evaluation budget and higher final performance than a matched baseline that evolves only target programs. On average, Janus reaches 99/% of the baseline's final improvement with 59.1/% fewer real evaluations. Evolved proxy evaluators also rank promising candidates more accurately than their seed versions. Together, these results extend evaluator-guided LLM discovery from tasks with cheap, scalable feedback to scientific domains where trustworthy evaluation is scarce and expensive.

</details>


### [30] [Persuasive and Compliant Tendencies Predict Group Decision-Making in Humans and Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08199)
*Wenwen He,Wenke Huang,Wei Yang Bryan Lim,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DecisionQE框架，用于量化LLM在群体决策中的说服性倾向和顺从性倾向，并通过狼人杀游戏作为交互测试平台，发现说服性倾向并不能显著改善群体结果，而顺从性倾向在合作场景中更稳定地占优；同时揭示顺从性的双面效应——在正直角色中促进合作、在对抗角色中增强隐瞒能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地参与与其他LLM及人类的群体决策，其影响力究竟源于说服性表达还是顺从性迎合仍不清楚，缺乏系统度量模型内在行为倾向的方法。理解这一问题对于评估LLM在群体交互中的安全性和社会影响至关重要。

Method: 提出了DecisionQE框架，一种基于问卷的方法，在多决策场景中分别度量每个模型的说服倾向和顺从倾向；随后以狼人杀（Werewolf）游戏作为交互测试平台，在信息不对称条件下研究这些行为倾向如何影响社会影响力与群体结果。

Result: 实验发现：更强的说服性倾向并未显著提升群体结果；而顺从性倾向较高的模型在合作方面展现出更稳定的优势。进一步揭示了顺从性的双面效应：在正直角色中有助于合作，但在对抗角色中反而提升了其隐瞒能力。

Conclusion: LLM群体交互不仅反映任务结果，还暴露了可量化的内在行为倾向模式。LLM可作为语言中介社会互动中社会学观察的透镜，同时凸显了将行为倾向纳入LLM系统安全评估的必要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly involved in group decision-making with other LLMs and humans. Yet it remains unclear whether their influence is driven by persuasion-oriented expression or compliance-oriented accommodation. We introduce DecisionQE, a questionnaire-based framework for measuring each model's persuasive and compliant tendencies across multiple decision scenarios, and use the Werewolf game as an interactive testbed to study their effects on social influence and group outcomes under asymmetric information. Across experiments, stronger persuasive tendency does not significantly improve group outcomes, whereas compliant-oriented models show more stable advantages in cooperation. We further reveal a dual effect of compliance: it supports cooperation in honest roles but improves concealment in adversarial roles. These findings suggest that LLM group interactions reveal not only task outcomes, but also measurable patterns of intrinsic behavioral tendency. LLMs can therefore serve as a lens for sociological observation of language-mediated interaction, while highlighting the need to incorporate behavioral tendencies into safety evaluation of LLM systems.

</details>


### [31] [Illusion of Alignment: Detecting Hidden Disagreement in Collaborative Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08210)
*Kaiming Liu,Fuwen Luo,Ziyue Wang,Jinrui Ju,Yuxuan Liu,Xuanyu Lei,Yunghwei Lai,Peng Li,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"对齐幻觉（IoA）"概念，指协作对话中参与者表面达成一致但实际目标、假设或执行计划存在分歧的现象。作者构建IoA-Suite数据集和评估协议，通过生成诊断性多项选择题使隐藏分歧可检测，并训练IoA-Prober-8B模型，在真实会议中平均每场发现2.89个未被言明的分歧，将其用于多智能体协作可提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作对话中常出现看似达成一致但实际存在潜在分歧的现象（对齐幻觉），这一现象在真实协作中普遍存在。然而IoA存在悖论：参与者若意识到分歧则会明说，若未意识到则无法表达，导致分歧对参与者和观察者都不可见，因此迫切需要使其可检测的方法。

Method: 1) 提出通过生成诊断性多项选择题来检测隐藏分歧，不同参与者的分歧回答提供隐藏不一致的行为证据；2) 构建IoA-Suite数据集和评估协议，覆盖五种任务类型和六个领域；3) 基于IoA-Suite训练IoA-Prober-8B模型；4) 在18场真实会议中进行用户研究验证，并在多智能体协作场景中评测。

Result: 最佳基线模型在IoA-Suite上仅达49.5% F1，瓶颈在于对话未呈现的私有上下文信息；IoA-Prober-8B在IoA-Suite上达到51.8% F1，在真实会议中每场平均发现2.89个参与者确认从未口头表达过的隐藏分歧，可迁移到真实人类对话；在多智能体协作中，使用IoA-Prober-8B与LLM智能体配对可提升BigCodeBench-Hard和HiddenBench上的下游任务表现。

Conclusion: 对齐幻觉是可检测的，通过诊断性多项选择可提供隐藏分歧的直接行为证据。当前模型检测性能仍有限（受限于对话未呈现的私有上下文），但IoA-Prober-8B已在真实会议和下游任务中展现出实用价值，为协作智能体的对齐质量保障提供了新途径。

Abstract: Collaborative dialogue can end with apparent agreement while participants still differ on goals, assumptions, or execution plans, creating an \textbf{illusion of alignment (IoA)}. A real-user study across 18 meetings confirms that IoA arises routinely in human collaboration. Yet IoA poses a paradox: if participants were aware of such disagreements, they would already be explicit; if not, they cannot articulate them when asked, leaving IoA invisible to both participants and observers. In this work, we make IoA detectable by generating diagnostic multiple-choice questions whose divergent answers across participants provide direct behavioral evidence of hidden disagreement. We construct \textbf{IoA-Suite}, a dataset and evaluation protocol for detecting hidden disagreement, spanning five task types and six domains. We find that even the best model attains only 49.5\% F1, with the bottleneck traced to private context that the dialogue does not surface. We then train \textbf{IoA-Prober-8B} based on IoA-Suite, reaching 51.8\% F1 on IoA-Suite. Across the aforementioned 18 real meetings, it surfaces 2.89 hidden disagreements per meeting that participants confirm they had not voiced, transferring to live human dialogue. Further, in multi-agent collaboration, pairing IoA-Prober-8B with LLM agents improves downstream task performance on BigCodeBench-Hard and HiddenBench.

</details>


### [32] [LatticeMind: A Conflict-Aware Memory Primitive for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08236)
*Heng Zhou,Lian Zhang,Yutao Fan,Tiancheng He,Siki Chen,Hejia Geng,Philip Torr,Zhenfei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LatticeMind是一种冲突感知的结构化记忆系统，在写入时处理多智能体LLM系统中的矛盾信息，通过显式条目状态、廉价符号冲突检查和LLM调停机制，在ConflictBank评估中达到0.97的准确率（对比最强聚合基线的0.61），但规划任务上表现混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统失败的关键往往不在于缺少候选答案，而在于缺乏持久机制来决定当前应信任哪个不兼容的主张。现有方法（多数投票、辩论、基于评判员的选择）只输出结果，未记录哪个主张胜出、哪个存在争议、以及为何后续更新取代先前结果，导致矛盾无法在写入时被结构化处理。

Method: LatticeMind采用三类机制的组合：(1) 维护显式的条目状态（item status）；(2) 应用廉价的符号冲突检查（symbolic conflict checks）；(3) 仅对未解决的语义冲突调用LLM调停（LLM reconciliation）。整个流程在写入时处理矛盾，而非在输出时通过聚合方式解决。

Result: 在去除来源名称提示的标签盲ConflictBank评估上，LatticeMind达到0.97准确率，而最强聚合基线仅为0.61，差异在配对McNemar检验下p<10^-6水平显著。消融实验显示移除检查器或调停器各损失12至14个点。在四个次级规划基准上表现混合：三个超越朴素合并，但未能在奖励迭代搜索的推理任务上取代深思熟虑（deliberation）方法。

Conclusion: LatticeMind证明了在写入时进行冲突感知处理的可行性和有效性，尤其适合信息源众多且矛盾频发的场景。然而，其优势局限于需要持久冲突解决的任务；对于奖励广泛迭代搜索的规划型任务，基于聚合并未取代专门的推理方法，说明写入时冲突管理应与思考型推理机制互补而非替代。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems often fail not for lack of candidate answers, but because they have no persistent mechanism for deciding which incompatible claim should currently be trusted. Majority vote, debate, and judge-based selection choose an output without recording which claim wins, which is contested, or why a later update supersedes it. We present \term{LatticeMind}, a conflict-aware structured memory that handles contradiction at write time. It maintains explicit item status, applies cheap symbolic conflict checks, and invokes LLM reconciliation only for unresolved semantic cases. On a label-blind ConflictBank evaluation that removes source-name hints, LatticeMind reaches 0.97 accuracy versus 0.61 for the strongest aggregation baseline, with the gap significant at $p<10^{-6}$ by paired McNemar test. Ablations show that removing the checker or the reconciler costs 12 to 14 points. On four secondary planning benchmarks the picture is mixed: LatticeMind beats naive merge on three of four, but does not replace deliberation methods on tasks rewarding iterative search.

</details>


### [33] [FemWear: A Specialized Wearable Foundation Model for Women's Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08244)
*Yifan Wang,Chenzhong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FemWear是一个面向女性健康任务的专业可穿戴基础模型，通过参数高效微调（仅训练1.11%的编码器参数）复用预训练多模态可穿戴骨干网络，在月经周期、情绪、睡眠/恢复、自主神经、活动及妊娠相关结果上学习共享纵向表征。在多个队列评估中，部分指标（如周期阶段宏F1提升8.15%）有改善，但在严格的留一参与者审计中表现不一，未建立普遍的临床有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用可穿戴基础模型虽在广泛的传感器流和人群上预训练，但并未针对女性健康任务进行专门设计。女性健康领域需要专门的模型来更好地处理月经周期、妊娠等特有的生理信号模式。

Method: FemWear采用参数高效迁移学习策略：保留预训练多模态可穿戴骨干的patch投影和Transformer编码器，仅训练239,236个参数（占21.54M编码器参数的1.11%），通过低秩残差适配器和因果任务族头实现。模型学习一个共享的纵向表征，覆盖月经、症状、情感、睡眠/恢复、自主神经、活动和妊娠相关结果的统一表示。

Result: 在固定参与者划分（三个种子）上，周期阶段宏F1提升8.15%，抽筋、情绪症状和睡眠问题的平均绝对误差分别降低9.32%、5.80%和9.43%。在更严格的42参与者嵌套留一参与者审计中，24小时发病、72小时发病和抽筋保持正向改善（2.87%、6.35%、2.19%），但阶段、情绪和睡眠为中性或负向，且无终点具有严格正的校正置信区间。容量匹配实验中优于最新日多层感知器，但不优于共享GRU或多门控混合专家基线。训练仅校准可将发病预期校准误差降低84.2-88.2%，且零时间嵌套违规。

Conclusion: FemWear展示了面向女性健康任务的定向迁移和连贯概率输出的能力，为女性健康研究提供了有价值的工具，但并未确立普适的性能优势或临床有效性，仍需进一步验证和改进。

Abstract: General wearable foundation models are pretrained across broad sensor streams and populations, but are not designed around women's-health tasks. We introduce FemWear, a specialized wearable foundation model that parameter-efficiently repurposes a pretrained multimodal wearable backbone. FemWear retains the patch projection and Transformer encoder, training 239,236 parameters (1.11% of a 21.54M-parameter encoder) through low-rank residual adapters and causal task-family heads. It learns one shared longitudinal representation for menstrual, symptom, affective, sleep/recovery, autonomic, activity, and pregnancy-related outcomes. We evaluate six cohorts with 63 comparable primary metrics, including 33 from women's-health cohorts, while retaining the 32-task OpenMHC ability-retention benchmark. On a fixed participant split over three seeds, FemWear improved cycle-phase macro-F1 by 8.15% and reduced mean absolute error for cramps, mood symptoms, and sleep problems by 9.32%, 5.80%, and 9.43%, respectively. In a stricter 42-participant nested leave-one-participant-out audit, 24-hour onset, 72-hour onset, and cramps retained positive changes of 2.87%, 6.35%, and 2.19%; phase, mood, and sleep were neutral or negative, and no endpoint had a strictly positive corrected confidence interval. Capacity-matched experiments outperformed a latest-day multilayer perceptron but not shared-GRU or multi-gate mixture-of-experts baselines. Train-only calibration reduced onset expected calibration error by 84.2--88.2% with zero temporal-nesting violations. FemWear enables targeted transfer and coherent probability outputs for women's-health research, but does not establish universal performance dominance or clinical validity.

</details>


### [34] [Your Prompt Is Not the Only Prompt: How Much Do LLMs Weight Structured-Output Schema Descriptions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08254)
*Sin-Ying Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM结构化输出中schema描述作为第二指令通道的效果，发现schema放置的优劣依赖于具体模型；当提示与schema冲突时schema可覆盖提示指令，且在中途添加推理字段能显著提升schema-only准确率。结论是应把提示和schema视为统一指令表面，并以系统提示为安全默认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化输出（按预定义JSON schema填充）已成为数据标注和信息抽取的默认机制，但schema描述本身引入了一条不同于prompt的第二指令通道。目前缺乏系统性研究：分类标签定义放在系统提示、用户提示还是schema描述中效果更好？不同模型之间是否存在一致的规律？

Method: 使用单一字段分类任务，引入非ce标签（nonce labels），在来自两家供应商的十个模型配置上系统比较标签定义放置在系统提示、用户提示和schema描述三种位置的效果；并进一步测试提示与schema冲突时的行为，以及在标签字段前加入必需的中级推理字段对schema-only性能的影响。

Result: Schema描述并未一贯胜过提示放置；GPT-4.1和GPT-5.4（无推理）的schema放置比系统提示低11-13个百分点。当提示与schema冲突时，错误schema指令导致准确率下降5-45个百分点（Claude Haiku 4.5从52.5%跌至7%，GPT-5.5从100%降至73%）。在标签字段前添加必需推理字段使schema-only准确率提升15-24个百分点（在有提升空间时），在每种测试配置下都超过仅用系统提示的性能；该效果在Claude Sonnet 4.6中等推理模式下仍成立，而仅扩展thinking并未产生可比增益。

Conclusion: Schema影响具有模型依赖性。系统提示仍是定义的标准安全默认，但更大的纪律在于维护单一事实来源、防止提示与schema漂移。更重要的是，schema设计（如字段顺序和推理字段的布置）可能比指令放置位置是更强的杠杆。实践者应将提示和schema视为统一指令表面，并对目标模型同时实证验证放置位置和字段设计。

Abstract: Structured output, where an LLM populates a predefined JSON schema, has become a default mechanism for data labeling and information extraction, but it also introduces a second instruction channel through schema descriptions. We tested whether classification-label definitions are better placed in the system prompt, user prompt, or schema description using a single-field classification task with nonce labels across ten model configurations from two vendors. Schema descriptions did not consistently outperform prompt-based placement; for GPT-4.1 and GPT-5.4 without reasoning, schema placement underperformed system prompts by 11-13 percentage points. Yet schemas are not inert metadata: when prompts and schemas conflicted, incorrect schema instructions caused accuracy drops of 5-45 points, with Claude Haiku 4.5 falling from 52.5% to 7%, indicating that schema instructions can override prompt instructions, and GPT-5.5 falling from 100% to 73%. Further, adding a required intermediate reasoning field before the label field improved schema-only accuracy by 15-24 points when headroom existed, exceeding system-prompt-only performance in every case tested. The effect held even for Claude Sonnet 4.6 at medium reasoning, where extended thinking alone did not produce a comparable gain. This suggests that schema design can affect how effectively models use information encoded in field descriptions. Overall, these results indicate that schema influence is model-dependent. In practice, the system prompt remains a safe default for definitions, but the bigger discipline is maintaining a single source of truth and preventing prompt/schema drift. More importantly, schema design itself may be a stronger lever than instruction placement. Practitioners should treat prompts and schemas as a unified instruction surface and empirically validate both placement and field design for their target model.

</details>


### [35] [OBLIVION: Workflow-Level Operational Skill Unlearning for Deployed Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08264)
*Zhengyang Shan,Xu Qian,Jiayun Xin,Kun Li,Yue Zhang,Minghui Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型智能体部署中的"技能撤销"问题，提出OBLIVION基准与防御框架，通过跨表面相干擦除和冻结工作流修复机制，将攻击成功率从1.0降至0.114，同时保持良性功能不受影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体正成为文件、记忆、注册表和外部工具的操作接口，这种部署转变带来了新的技能撤销问题：即便技能已从显式注册表中移除，智能体仍可能从残留载体（如存档、转录、模式或记忆条目）中重建该技能。现有研究通常关注参数级遗忘，但真正需要解决的是防止部署中的智能体通过原始工具重建已被撤销的技能，这是一个未被充分研究的"操作性技能遗忘"问题。

Method: OBLIVION将每个攻击片段建模为源到汇的工作流，采用三项核心机制：(1) 跨表面相干擦除（Cross-Surface Coherent Erasure），减少残留载体；(2) 在危险汇点附近使用冻结工作流修复（frozen workflow remediation）；(3) 构建受控基准测试集评估防御效果。实验在两个场景中进行：锁定88个攻击片段和技能攻击派生的沙箱环境。

Result: 在锁定的88个攻击片段中，无防御臂的正式攻击成功率为1.0，OBLIVION将其降至0.114，影响加权暴露度降至0.115，同时保持锁定效用1.0和良性拦截率0。在技能攻击派生的沙箱中，OBLIVION将攻击成功率从1.0降至0.2，影响加权暴露度从1.0降至0.213，且所有效用控制指标均得以保留。

Conclusion: 实验结果有力支持了工作流级评估的必要性——防御评估不能仅检查显式技能条目，而应关注整个工作流层面。OBLIVION证明了在保持系统正常功能的同时，可以有效防止被撤销技能的复活。

Abstract: Large language model agents are becoming operational interfaces to files, memories, registries, and external tools. This deployment shift creates a new skill revocation problem: after a skill is removed from an explicit registry, an agent may still reconstruct it from residual carriers such as archives, transcripts, schemas, or memory entries. We study this problem as operational skill unlearning, where the goal is not parameter-level forgetting, but preventing a deployed agent from rebuilding a revoked skill through primitive tools. We introduce OBLIVION, a controlled benchmark and defense harness for revoked-skill resurrection. OBLIVION models each episode as a source-to-sink workflow, applies Cross-Surface Coherent Erasure to reduce residual carriers, and uses frozen workflow remediation near dangerous sinks. On the locked 88 attack episodes, the no-defense arm reaches formal attack success rate 1.0. OBLIVION reduces the rate to 0.114 and impact-weighted exposure to 0.115 while keeping locked utility at 1.0 and benign block rate at 0. In a separate skill-attack-derived sandbox, OBLIVION reduces attack success from 1.0 to 0.2 and impact-weighted exposure from 1.0 to 0.213 while preserving all utility controls. These results support workflow-level evaluation beyond checking explicit skill entries.

</details>

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索了大型语言模型（LLMs）在海事导航领域的应用，构建了包含50个真实AIS导航场景的数据集，并评估了多种LLM架构和规模对COLREGs规则和良好船艺（Good Seamanship）的理解与推理能力。结果表明，未经微调的LLMs（包括大型在线模型）在海事导航任务上仍面临较大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在汽车等领域的态势理解、推理和决策方面已展现强大能力，但海事导航领域涉及成文规则（COLREGs）和不成文的良好船艺实践，尚未被系统探索。作者希望评估LLMs是否能够理解并应用于海事导航这一复杂决策领域。

Method: 作者从AIS数据中构建了一个包含50个多样化真实导航场景的数据集，为每个场景标注了适用的COLREG规则、推荐行动及行动推理。随后测试了多种不同架构和规模的LLM，评估它们对海事导航任务的理解和推理能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，未经微调的LLMs（即便是较大的在线模型）在海事导航任务上仍然难以取得令人满意的表现，说明该领域任务的复杂性和专业性对现有模型构成显著挑战。

Conclusion: 海事导航任务对LLMs而言尚未解决，需要进一步的微调或针对性优化才能提升模型在该专业领域的表现。

Abstract: Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown considerable capability for situational understanding, reasoning, and decision making in different domains, most notable in the automotive sector. Therefore, we explore current state-of-the-art LLMs as a tool for maritime navigation, which includes both codified rules in the Collision Regulations (COLREGs) and uncodified best practices summarized in the concept of ``Good Seamanship''. We construct a dataset consisting of 50 diverse, real-world navigation scenarios from AIS data, label scenarios with applicable COLREG rules, recommended actions, and the reasoning for the action. We explore a variety of different LLM architectures and sizes to determine their understanding of maritime navigation tasks as well as evaluate their reasoning capabilities in this domain. The results obtained indicate that the maritime navigation task remains difficult to solve without fine-tuning, even for larger online models.

</details>


### [37] [Fair on the Surface? Benchmarking Hidden-Output Fairness Gaps in LLM Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08284)
*Chan Aristella Lu,Arya Fayyazi,Junhao Zhang,Saeid Shokoufa,Yue Xing,Zhen Xiang,Kyu Hyung Lee,Mehdi Kamal,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FairGap是首个同时评估LLM推荐系统在可观测输出和隐藏表示两个层级上公平性的基准，通过对照反事实身份探针（性别、年龄、种族）测量输出偏移（OBS）和隐藏表示偏移（IBS），并用表示-输出对齐度（ROA）及象限诊断揭示两者关系。实验发现隐藏-输出解耦现象普遍存在（ROA极少超过0.22），且激活引导在降低IBS高达8倍的同时会恶化OBS，揭示了内部与输出层级公平性之间存在根本性张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐系统的公平性审计仅关注可观察输出，隐含假设稳定的推荐结果意味着稳定的内部处理过程。作者质疑这一假设，认为需要同时审视输出层级与内部表示层级的公平性，以发现纯输出审计无法检测的隐藏不公平模式。

Method: 构建FairGap基准，包含两个层级的公平性度量：可观测输出偏移（OBS）和隐藏表示偏移（IBS），通过受控反事实身份探针在性别、年龄和种族维度上进行测量；提出表示-输出对齐度（ROA）和象限诊断方法，用于识别用户级隐藏-输出不匹配；在六个开源LLM家族、三个领域上展开实验，并应用激活引导技术进行因果验证。

Result: FairGap揭示了普遍存在的隐藏-输出解耦现象：ROA几乎不超过0.22；相当比例的用户群体在输出稳定情况下存在显著的内部表示偏移，这种模式是纯输出审计因设计缺陷无法检测的。此外，激活引导在将IBS降低高达8倍的同时反而恶化了OBS，证明了内部与输出层级公平性之间存在根本性张力。

Conclusion: 研究表明仅依赖输出级公平性审计会系统性遗漏隐藏的内部不公平，且内部公平性与输出公平性并非天然一致，甚至可能相互冲突。现有公平性框架缺乏诊断这种解耦与张力关系的能力，FairGap为此提供了基准和方法论支撑。

Abstract: Fairness audits for LLM-based recommenders have largely focused on observable outputs, implicitly assuming that stable recommendations reflect stable internal processing. We challenge this assumption with FairGap, the first benchmark to jointly evaluate recommendation fairness at two levels: observable output shift (OBS) and hidden representation shift (IBS), measured through controlled counterfactual identity probes across gender, age, and race. Their relationship is summarized via Representation-Output Alignment (ROA), with quadrant diagnostics for identifying user-level hidden-output mismatch. Applied to six open-weight LLM families across three domains, FairGap reveals pervasive hidden-output decoupling: ROA rarely exceeds 0.22, and a non-negligible user population shows stable outputs despite substantial internal shifts, a mode that output-only audits cannot detect by design. Further, activation steering that reduces IBS by up to 8x simultaneously worsens OBS, demonstrating a fundamental tension between internal and output-level fairness that existing frameworks are unequipped to diagnose.

</details>


### [38] [Query-Only Backdoor Attacks on Self-Evolving Skills via Trajectory Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08303)
*Yuyang Luo,Haoran Wang,Kai Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹后门攻击（TBA），一种仅通过查询即可攻击自进化技能系统的新型攻击方法。攻击者通过提交精心构造的查询，诱导智能体在轨迹中执行目标动作并显式陈述激活条件，重复该条件-动作模式使技能进化器将其固化为带后门的可复用规则，从而在不直接操控技能的情况下实现后门注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化技能系统通过从执行轨迹中自动构建和更新技能来替代外部技能获取，被认为可以降低直接技能注入攻击的风险。然而，这种进化管道可能引入新的攻击面——攻击者可以通过智能体交互间接诱导受损轨迹，从而操控技能进化过程。作者旨在证明这一新型威胁的存在。

Method: 提出轨迹后门攻击（TBA）方法：攻击者提交精心构造的查询，引导智能体执行目标动作并在轨迹中显式陈述相应的激活条件；在多样化的触发任务中重复相同的条件-动作模式，同时保持正常查询不变，促使技能进化器将该模式固化为可复用的触发依赖规则。

Result: 在三个基准、两个技能进化系统、四个开源和闭源骨干模型上的实验表明，TBA能够可靠地植入条件后门，同时保持干净任务效用，甚至达到或超越直接技能注入的效果。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了轨迹驱动技能进化系统中存在的关键安全漏洞，表明即使系统设计为信任内部构建而非外部获取，攻击者仍可通过间接方式操控技能进化过程来实现恶意目标。

Abstract: Agentic skills improve large language model (LLM) agents by encoding reusable procedures for complex tasks. However, manually authored skills often adapt poorly to long-horizon tasks and changing environments. To address the limitation, self-evolving skill systems have been developed to automatically construct and update skills from execution trajectories, shifting skill acquisition from external marketplaces to a trusted evolution pipeline. By replacing external skill acquisition with trusted internal construction, self-evolving skill systems reduce exposure to skill injection attacks that rely on direct skill manipulation. However, this skill evolution pipeline may introduce a new attack surface in which an attacker can indirectly steer skill evolution by inducing compromised trajectories through agent interactions. To demonstrate the threat, we propose Trajectory Backdoor Attack (TBA), a query-only attack that steers a trusted skill-evolution pipeline toward producing a backdoored skill. Specifically, we craft attacker-submitted queries to lead the agent to perform the target action and explicitly state the corresponding activation condition in the trajectory. We repeat the same condition-action pattern across diverse triggered tasks, while leaving clean queries unchanged, encouraging the evolver to consolidate the pattern as a reusable trigger-dependent rule into the evolved skill. Experiments on three benchmarks across two skill-evolution systems using four open- and closed-source backbone models demonstrate that TBA reliably implants conditional backdoors while preserving clean-task utility, matching or even surpassing direct skill injection. The results reveal a critical vulnerability in trajectory-driven skill evolution.

</details>


### [39] [StructReward: Efficient Structured Process Rewards for Self-Correcting Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08326)
*Yifan Li,Ruxin Sun,Tongzhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StructReward是一种计算高效的强化学习框架，通过结构化步骤级奖励对齐为多模态推理提供密集强化信号。它将生成的解表示为推理步骤序列，使用轻量级数值、符号和词法匹配规则与过程标注的参考步骤对齐，生成密集过程奖励，并通过门控GRPO目标与最终答案一致性和输出有效性奖励结合。此外还回收策略rollout用于互补监督和反思性自我修正，大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法通常只基于最终答案正确性对整个响应进行二元奖励，丢弃了中间推理步骤中的监督信息。过程奖励模型虽能提供更细粒度反馈，但依赖单独训练的验证器、昂贵的思维链标注或大型语言模型的在线评判。需要一种既提供过程级密集监督又保持计算高效的方法。

Method: StructReward将每个生成的解表示为推理步骤序列，通过与过程标注的参考步骤进行轻量级数值、符号和词法匹配来对齐生成密集过程奖励。采用门控GRPO目标，将过程奖励与最终答案一致性奖励和输出有效性奖励结合。同时回收策略rollout，将其转化为用于响应比较和反思性自我修正的补充监督；并使用强LLM将采样的正确轨迹改写为面向反思的训练实例。

Result: 实验结果表明，结构化过程监督与rollout回收为自我改进的多模态推理提供了高效路径，且由于奖励计算在线完成且无需额外的学习验证器或外部LLM评判器，StructReward大幅降低了多模态强化学习的计算开销。

Conclusion: StructReward展示了通过结构化步骤级过程监督和策略回滚回收，可以在不依赖昂贵验证器和外部LLM评判的情况下，实现计算高效的多模态推理自我改进。这为多模态强化学习中的密集奖励设计与计算效率权衡提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as an effective approach for improving multimodal reasoning. However, most existing methods evaluate an entire response using a binary reward based only on final-answer correctness, thereby discarding the supervision available in intermediate reasoning steps. Process reward models offer finer-grained feedback, but they typically rely on separately trained verifiers, costly chain-of-thought annotations, or online judging by large language models (LLMs). In this work, we introduce StructReward, a compute-efficient framework that provides dense reinforcement signals through structured step-level reward alignment. StructReward represents each generated solution as a sequence of reasoning steps and aligns them with process-labeled reference steps using lightweight numerical, symbolic, and lexical matching rules. The aligned labels are aggregated into a dense process reward and combined with final-answer consistency and output-validity rewards through a gated Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) objective. We further recycle policy rollouts into complementary supervision for response comparison and reflective self-correction, rather than discarding them after policy updates. Separately, we use a strong LLM to rewrite sampled correct trajectories into reflection-oriented training instances, further strengthening the policy's ability to evaluate and refine its reasoning. Since reward computation is performed online without an additional learned verifier or external LLM judge, StructReward substantially reduces the computational overhead of multimodal reinforcement learning. Experimental results show that structured process supervision and rollout recycling provide an efficient path toward self-improving multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [40] [LLMVisor: A Real-Time Latency Attribution Model for Multi-Tenant LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08382)
*Shuowei Jin,Xueshen Liu,Jiaxin Shan,Le Xu,Tieying Zhang,Liguang Xie,Z. Morley Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMVisor提出了一种基于roofline模型的逐请求延迟归因方法，用于多租户GPU集群中的LLM推理引擎，通过简洁的分段线性函数将批处理延迟分解为可加性的逐请求份额，在微秒级开销下实现高精度归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM推理转向多租户GPU集群，协同批处理虽提升了吞吐量，但模糊了逐租户的资源使用情况，限制了细粒度的控制和计费能力。要实现推理引擎的分数共享，需要在调度循环内运行一个实时、逐请求的归因原语，既要准确又要足够轻量。

Method: LLMVisor采用roofline引导的延迟归因模型，通过简洁的分段线性函数捕捉内存受限和计算受限两个阶段，特征与FLOPs和内存I/O流量成正比。该模型将批处理延迟分解为可加性的逐请求份额，并能在微秒级时间尺度上高效运行。

Result: 在A100/H100 GPU上，针对Llama 3.1-8B和Qwen 2.5-14B/32B模型，在不同张量并行度和工作负载混合下进行评估。相比基于token计数的基线，LLMVisor在预填充阶段p90和p99的相对误差分别降低最多2.5倍和3.3倍，在解码阶段分别降低3.5倍和4.4倍，同时实现了接近完美的R-squared，即使在批处理变化和序列差异的情况下依然保持高精度。

Conclusion: LLMVisor通过roofline引导的分段线性归因模型，为多租户LLM推理环境提供了准确且轻量的逐请求延迟归因方案，在不牺牲精度的情况下满足调度循环内的实时性要求，为推理引擎的分数共享和精确计费提供了关键基础能力。

Abstract: As LLM inference shifts to multi-tenant GPU clusters, co-batching improves throughput but obscures per-tenant usage and limits control. Enabling fractional sharing of the inference engine requires a real-time, per-request attribution primitive that is accurate and light enough to run inside the scheduling loop. We present LLMVisor, a roofline-guided latency attribution model that captures the memory-bound and compute-bound phases via a concise piecewise-linear form over features proportional to FLOPs and memory I/O traffic. LLMVisor decomposes batch latency into additive, per-request shares and runs efficiently at microsecond scale. We evaluate LLMVisor across Llama 3.1-8B and Qwen 2.5-14B/32B on A100/H100 GPUs under varying tensor parallelism and workload mixes. Compared to a token-count baseline, LLMVisor attains near-perfect R-squared and reduces relative error by up to 2.5x and 3.3x at p90 and p99, respectively, for prefill, and by up to 3.5x and 4.4x for decode, despite batching variability and sequence divergence.

</details>


### [41] [TRACE-Memory: Public-Conditioned Retrieval and Utility-Aware Evidence Admission for Personalized Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08446)
*Jing Wang,Zhu Wang,Yifan Guo,Yulong Yang,Yunji Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TRACE-Memory两阶段框架，通过"选择性个性化"策略，仅在公共信息不足且用户历史记忆确实能带来增量效用时才将记忆注入生成上下文，实现了超越随机/词汇检索并接近前沿大语言模型流水线的个性化生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化生成系统通过请求-记忆相关性检索用户历史并注入模型上下文，但存在三个关键缺陷：相关记忆可能涉及错误的偏好维度（噪声干扰）、可能重复公开信息（冗余无益）、或支撑不足（信息量不够）。因此需要选择性使用个人记忆，仅当它比纯公共响应用户提供额外效用时才启用，而非默认总是注入。

Method: 提出TRACE-Memory两阶段选择性个性化框架。阶段一：查询生成器生成缺失的用户特定信息查询，基于覆盖度优先的候选池进行检索；阶段二：证据准入策略从候选池中选取紧凑的、可追溯来源的证据单元子集（或空集），依据响应级别的增量效用进行判定。训练采用渐进式策略：结构化SFT初始化、降维空间的分阶段GRPO预热、以及嵌套多采样联合GRPO，联合训练查询生成与证据准入策略。

Result: 在来自Goodreads、Amazon Reviews和Reddit的4,500个受控与自然任务上，TRACE-Memory一致优于随机和词汇记忆使用方案，在语义检索基础上进一步提升，且随着本地生成器容量增大可匹敌前沿大语言模型记忆流水线。表明证据准入能根据公共上下文的充分性进行条件化取舍，支持选择性而非默认式个性化。

Conclusion: 个人记忆应在能产生实际效用时才被使用。TRACE-Memory通过两阶段"查询-准入"框架和渐进式强化学习训练，验证了基于增量效用的选择性个性化是可行且有效的方向，为个性化生成系统提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Personalized generation systems retrieve user history by request--memory relevance and inject it into the model context. Yet relevant history may concern the wrong preference aspect, duplicate public information, or provide insufficient support. We argue that personal memory should be used only when it adds utility beyond a public-only response. We propose TRACE-Memory, a two-stage framework for selective personalization. Stage 1 queries for user-specific information missing from the request and public context, then retrieves a coverage-oriented candidate pool. Stage 2 admits a compact subset of source-traceable evidence units, or the empty set, according to response-level incremental utility. We progressively train the query-generation and evidence-admission policies through structured SFT initialization, reduced-space stage-wise GRPO warm-up, and nested multi-sample Joint GRPO. Across 4,500 Controlled and Natural tasks from Goodreads, Amazon Reviews, and Reddit, TRACE-Memory consistently outperforms random and lexical memory use, improves over semantic retrieval, remains competitive with frontier-LLM memory pipelines as local generator capacity increases, and conditions evidence admission on public-context sufficiency, supporting selective rather than default personalization.

</details>


### [42] [What Keeps Agent Skills from Being Reusable? Evidence from 138K SKILL.md Files](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08453)
*Chi Zhang,Yimin Liu,Xinze Chen,Ping Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了20,556个仓库中138,133个公开SKILL.md文件，基于官方规范和最佳实践构建两层缺陷分类体系，发现91.8%的技能至少存在一个缺陷，主要问题为打包层面的普通缺陷而非恶意攻击；并通过路由压力测试及修复实验验证了质量保证生成工作流的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agent Skills标准将SKILL.md文件定义为指令与支持资源的结合体，使LLM代理能跨会话复用流程。然而许多被共享为可复用组件的公开技能实际上源自单一任务、仓库或对话，存在广泛的"可复用性宣称与实际质量"之间的差距，但缺乏系统的量化评估。

Method: 收集138,133个来自20,556个仓库的公共SKILL.md文件；构建基于官方规范和最佳实践的两层缺陷分类体系；使用宽松与严格阈值进行稳健性估计；对20,000个技能执行确定性路由压力测试验证功能影响；按平台和来源（如AI标记）分层分析缺陷率差异；开展轻量级执行与修复实验，验证结合规范感知提示、轻量级lint、自动修复和安全门控的质量保证生成工作流。

Result: 91.8%的技能至少含有一个缺陷，在宽松与严格阈值下估计稳定（88.8%-94.6%）。主要失败类型为普通打包问题（弱路由元数据、臃肿或不可操作的正文、资源组织不良）而非恶意攻击。路由压力测试显示具有有效路由元数据的技能从启动描述中检索更可靠。不同平台和来源缺陷率不同：规范感知的技能缺陷更少，AI标记的技能在安全性和可移植性方面问题更多。

Conclusion: Agent Skills领域中缺陷是普遍而非个例，但可修复性高。结合规范感知提示、轻量级lint、自动修复与安全门控的质量保证生成工作流是可行的改进路径。

Abstract: Under the current standard, Agent Skills are SKILL.md files that combine instructions with supporting resources, enabling Large Language Model (LLM) agents to reuse procedures beyond a single conversation. Yet many public skills appear to originate from a single task, repository, or conversation, even when they are shared as reusable components. We analyze this gap across 138,133 public SKILL.md files from 20,556 repositories using a two-tier defect taxonomy grounded in the official specification and best-practice guidance. We find that 91.8% of skills contain at least one detected defect, with stable estimates across lenient and strict thresholds (88.8-94.6%). The dominant failures are ordinary packaging problems rather than exotic attacks: weak routing metadata, bloated or non-actionable bodies, and poor resource organization. A deterministic routing stress test over 20,000 skills shows the functional impact: skills with valid routing metadata are retrieved more reliably from startup descriptions than skills with routing defects. Defect rates vary by platform and provenance: specification-aware skills contain fewer defects, while AI-marked skills show more safety and portability problems. Lightweight enforcement and repair experiments support a quality-assured generation workflow combining spec-aware prompting, lightweight linting, automated repair, and safety gating.

</details>


### [43] [Hierarchical Self-Improvement: A Framework for Task-Specific Evolvable Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08466)
*Tailin Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出分层自我改进（HSI）框架，让冻结的LLM在三个层级作用域上工作——任务harness H执行任务、evolver重写H、meta-evolver在冻结外部锚定下重写evolver的策略代码。通过思维开关设计隔离harness演化的贡献，实验表明在BALROG基准上对中等难度任务有显著一致提升，且具有良好的泛化性，但在超出骨干模型能力范围的任务上无法改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM代理通常通过人工修改提示、工具或工作流来改进，而围绕模型的可执行脚手架（harness）在部署后被视为固定产物。作者希望研究一种替代方案，即让harness具备任务特异性并可持续演化，从而在不改变冻结模型本身的情况下提升代理性能。

Method: HSI采用三个层级的作用域设计：(1) 任务harness H执行具体任务；(2) evolver根据环境反馈重写H；(3) meta-evolver在冻结外部锚定下重写evolver自身的策略代码。通过thinking-on/off设计，任务执行阶段禁用推理、自我修改阶段启用推理，从而隔离harness演化的贡献。理论上有两个边界：反馈保真度（需要信息量充足的奖励信号）和骨干能力（harness重设计不能弥补冻结模型的能力缺陷）。

Result: 以DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Preview为冻结骨干在BALROG基准上，HSI在中等难度任务上相对初始harness取得一致提升（BabyAI +39.3、Crafter +33.0、TextWorld +25.0、MiniHack +15.0，均为原始% Progress），并在BabaIsAI子套件上具有强被保留泛化能力（BreakStop最优测试0.98、GoTo 1.00，来自20%未见切分）。在超出骨干能力的NLE任务上，harness演化无改进。

Conclusion: 任务特定harness演化是在固定LLM代理上实现改进的一个可行维度，但其效果受反馈保真度和骨干模型能力双重边界约束。该工作展示了在清晰的经验限制下，通过harness的持续自我改进来优化冻结LLM代理的潜力。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents are often improved by modifying prompts, tools, or workflows manually, while the executable scaffold surrounding the model---the \emph{harness}---is typically treated as a fixed artifact after deployment. This work studies an alternative where the harness is \emph{task-specific and continuously evolvable}: each task family maintains its own harness, which is hot-swapped across iterations through a fixed task-injection seam and rewritten using environment feedback. We introduce \textbf{Hierarchical Self-Improvement (HSI)}, a framework in which a single frozen LLM $M$ operates across three hierarchical scopes: a task harness $H$ that executes tasks, an evolver that rewrites $H$, and a meta-evolver that rewrites the evolver's strategy code under a frozen outer anchor. A thinking-on/off design isolates the contribution of harness evolution by disabling reasoning during task execution while enabling it during self-modification. HSI is bounded by two factors: a \emph{feedback-fidelity bound}, since evolution requires informative reward signals to guide selection, and a \emph{backbone capability bound}, since harness redesign cannot overcome limitations of the frozen model. On BALROG with DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Preview as the frozen backbone, HSI achieves consistent gains over the initial harness on moderate-difficulty tasks ($+39.3$ on BabyAI, $+33.0$ on Crafter, $+25.0$ on TextWorld, and $+15.0$ on MiniHack, all in raw \% Progress), while obtaining strong held-out generalization on BabaIsAI sub-suites ($0.98$ best-test on BreakStop and $1.00$ on GoTo from a $20\%$ unseen split). On tasks beyond the backbone's capability (NLE), harness evolution provides no improvement. These results demonstrate task-specific harness evolution as a viable axis for improving frozen LLM agents under clear empirical limits. Code is available at https://github.com/TailinZhou/hsi.

</details>


### [44] [LLM within MCP Matters: Measuring Inefficient Resource Utilization Driven by LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08467)
*Minhan Cho,Soyoung Park,Kihyeon Jeong,Byeongkyu Jeon,Daejin Choi,Jinyoung Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过54,000次试验、24个LLM（Claude/Gemini/GPT）在生产法律信息MCP服务器上的系统性研究，发现客户端LLM经常忽略嵌入在系统提示中的参考数据（如标识符查找表），而偏向使用搜索工具。当移除搜索工具时，23/24个模型能可靠读取嵌入数据（命中率≥98%）；但当搜索工具存在时，9个模型命中率降至15%以下，表明这是行为偏好问题而非能力缺失。三因素干预虽有组合效果但交互作用显著，单个干预可能适得其反，作者主张MCP宿主应用应提供显式机制将服务器指令置于工具选择之前。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP服务器常在系统提示中嵌入参考数据（如标识符查找表），使LLM能在不调用搜索工具的情况下直接响应查询。但该设计是否有效取决于客户端LLM是否真正消费这些指令嵌入数据，目前缺乏大规模系统性验证。

Method: 在24个LLM（9个Claude、6个Gemini、9个GPT）上进行54,000次试验，使用生产级法律信息MCP服务器。设置对照条件（移除搜索工具）以区分能力缺失与行为偏好，并采用2^3阶乘分析测试三种指令级干预措施及其交互效应。

Result: 移除搜索工具后，23/24个模型读嵌入数据命中率≥98%；但搜索工具存在时，9个模型命中率跌破15%。三因素组合干预可使20/24个模型恢复至86%以上，但单独干预对特定模型族可能产生反效果。

Conclusion: 服务器级提示工程只是临时解决方案而非根本修复。作者建议MCP宿主应用应提供显式机制，将服务器指令置于客户端LLM的工具选择决策之前，从根本上解决嵌入数据被忽略的问题。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) standardizes how servers expose data and tools to Large Language Models (LLMs). A common server design embeds frequently used reference data, such as identifier lookup tables, directly in the server instructions: the system-prompt text a server hands to the host application. When a query concerns an entry of the embedded table, the model can act on it immediately instead of re-discovering the same information through a search tool. We test whether client LLMs actually consume such instruction-embedded data, reporting a 54,000-trial study across 24 LLMs (9 Claude, 6 Gemini, 9 GPT) on a production legal-information MCP server. A diagnostic condition that removes the competing search tool shows that failures are dominated by behavioral preference rather than missing capability. With search unavailable, 23 of 24 models read the embedded data reliably (hit ratio at least 98%); with a search tool merely present, 9 models drop below 15%. A 2^3 factorial analysis of three instruction-level interventions reveals strong interaction effects: combining all three restores at least 86% for 20 of 24 models, but individual interventions can backfire for specific model families. Per-server prompt engineering is therefore a workaround rather than a fix; we argue that MCP host applications should provide an explicit mechanism that places server instructions ahead of tool selection in the client LLM's deliberation.

</details>


### [45] [Yesterday's Shield, Today's Spear: A Self-Evolving Safety Guardrail in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08471)
*Cong Ming,Jingyi Chen,Bin Liu,Qi Chu,Tao Gong,Nenghai Yu,Yingfei Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SESG（自进化安全护栏）系统，一个在生产环境中运行的多智能体框架，通过监控实时流量、自动生成定向训练数据并循环迭代，使1.7B规模的LLM安全护栏在16-24小时内适应新威胁，相比手动流程（40-90小时）大幅提升效率，在六个月轮次中持续超越静态护栏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署的LLM安全护栏本质上是静态的：训练一次后在发布时冻结，而新的越狱技术和未被覆盖的有害类别会在数天内出现，导致防御永远慢一步。

Method: SESG是一个多智能体系统，包含四个核心组件：1）监控实时流量，识别两类失败——形式上新颖的越狱（jailbreaks novel in form）和内容上新颖的有害类别（harmful categories novel in content）；2）生成智能体（generation agent）为确认的失败合成配对训练数据；3）验证智能体（validation agent）根据部署模型的错误方向重新平衡训练批次，让模型自身的错误引导训练集；4）路由智能体（routing agent）将训练动作匹配到诊断出的差距，并返回下一版本到生产环境。

Result: 在V0到V6共六轮现场进化中，一个1.7B大小的护栏在16-24小时内适应新威胁，仅需约2小时的人工投入（对比手动流程的40-90小时）。在六个新兴威胁上，SESG优于0.6B到9B规模的静态护栏以及自适应基线，同时保持通用筛查能力。自2026年4月起，SESG已成为Sangfor护栏的主要更新管道，两个月内自主关闭了15个新威胁场景中的14个。

Conclusion: SESG证明了通过"自我进化"机制（而非静态训练一次）可以显著缩小LLM安全防御与快速演变威胁之间的时间差，使得安全护栏能够在生产环境中持续自适应更新。论文发布9个测试集（覆盖6个新威胁）供社区使用，并提醒包含可能有害或冒犯性的示例。

Abstract: Deployed LLM safety guardrails are predominantly static: trained once and frozen at release, while new jailbreak techniques and previously un-addressed harmful categories emerge within days, leaving the defense perpetually a step behind. We present SESG (Self-Evolving Safety Guardrails), a multi-agent system running in production. SESG monitors the live traffic behind a deployed guardrail and surfaces two classes of failure: jailbreaks novel in form and harmful categories novel in content. Once a failure is confirmed, a generation agent synthesizes paired training data targeted at it; a validation agent rebalances the batch toward the direction in which the deployed model errs, so that the model's own mistakes steer its training set; and a routing agent matches the training action to the diagnosed gap and returns the next version to production. Over six rounds of live evolution (V0 to V6), a 1.7B guardrail adapts to a new threat in 16-24 hours, with about 2 hours of human effort, versus the 40-90 hours of the manual process it replaces. On six emerging threats, it outperforms static guardrails from 0.6B to 9B and an adaptive baseline while preserving its general screening competence. Since April 2026, SESG has been the primary update pipeline of Sangfor's guardrail, autonomously closing 14 of 15 new threat scenarios in two months. We release 9 test sets for the 6 new threats at https://github.com/Trams1017/SESG. Warning: This paper contains examples that may be harmful or offensive.

</details>


### [46] [HoloAegis: Frozen Representation, Topological Inference: Minimally Parametric Safety Manifolds for Zero-Shot LLM Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08485)
*Tak Ho Alex Li,Kaijie Liu,Lik-Hang Lee,Kin Chung Ho,Ping Shum,Michael K. Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HoloAegis，一种最小参数化拓扑推理框架，通过纯几何推理在冻结的语义表示上实现安全检测，无需微调，实现高精度、亚毫秒延迟和跨语言迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM安全护栏面临根本性矛盾：微调会破坏预训练表示，而生成式判别器推理成本过高。作者质疑主流范式，探索是否可以通过对冻结语义表示的纯几何推理来实现安全检测。

Method: HoloAegis将表示与推理解耦：使用未微调的编码器将文本映射到单位球面，所有决策均为纯几何运算。核心组件包括：(1) 将安全评估形式化为预计算系统拓扑锚库上的Gibbs-Boltzmann自由能计算；(2) 引入双时间尺度指数移动平均检测多轮语义漂移；(3) 提出拓扑边界稳定性猜想，论证稀疏锚质心比全向量空间方法更能稳定决策边界以抵抗高频词法扰动。

Result: 在8个基准测试上，HoloAegis实现了最先进的准确率：AuthenHallu上AUC达1.0000，HarmBench上达0.9802；推理延迟低于毫秒级；零冷启动数据需求；跨语言迁移上中文CHIFRAUD的AUC达0.9758。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过最小参数化的拓扑推理和纯几何方法，可以在不破坏预训练表示且无需昂贵生成式判别器的情况下实现高效、准确且跨语言泛化的LLM安全检测。

Abstract: Current LLM safety guardrails face a fundamental tension: fine-tuning distorts pre-trained representations while generative judges incur prohibitive inference costs. We challenge the prevailing paradigm by asking: can safety be achieved through pure geometric reasoning over frozen semantic representations? We present HoloAegis, a minimally parametric topological inference framework that decouples representation from reasoning. We term our approach minimally parametric because the only free parameters are the anchor count K and the temperature tau, both fixed after construction and requiring no gradient-based training. An un-fine-tuned encoder maps text to a unit sphere, after which all decisions are purely geometric. We formalize safety evaluation as a Gibbs-Boltzmann Free Energy computation over a pre-computed System Topology Anchor Bank, and we introduce Dual Time-Scale Exponential Moving Averages to detect progressive multi-turn semantic drift. Our key theoretical insight is a Topological Boundary Stability Conjecture: we provide theoretical motivation and strong empirical evidence that sparse anchor centroids stabilize the decision boundary against high-frequency lexical perturbations far better than full vector space methods. Evaluated across 8 benchmarks, HoloAegis achieves state-of-the-art accuracy (1.0000 AUC on AuthenHallu, 0.9802 on HarmBench) with sub-millisecond latency, zero cold-start data, and cross-lingual transfer (0.9758 AUC on Chinese CHIFRAUD).

</details>

*Mohanad Odema,Gabrielle De Micheli,Dayin Gou,Nilesh Malpeddi,Prathamesh Vaste,Jacob Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免训练的低秩压缩方法，解决现有SOTA压缩框架中两个关键问题：压缩时校准数据激活的残差误差跨层累积导致的表征错位，以及"压缩后层重要性分布保持不变"这一假设不成立的问题。通过逐层压缩+校准校正和迭代压缩+秩分配校正两种策略，在Llama和Qwen3模型上相较基线提升约1-2.5个准确率点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练低秩压缩框架虽然能有效减少参数并保持任务精度，但存在两个关键局限：(1) 压缩时校准数据激活的残差误差在层间累积，导致压缩时模拟的表征与推理时实际经历的表征之间产生错位；(2) 假设压缩后层重要性分布不变这一前提在实际中不成立。这两个问题共同导致压缩过程与部署模型之间存在对齐偏差。

Method: 提出一种简单且免训练的方法，与现有压缩框架兼容，包含两个核心组件：(1) 逐层压缩与校准校正（Layer-by-Layer Compression with Calibration Correction），用于消除残差误差的跨层累积；(2) 迭代压缩与秩分配校正（Iterative Compression with Rank Allocation Correction），用于修正压缩后层重要性分布变化的问题。该方法基于现有SOTA分解框架实现。

Result: 在Llama和Qwen3模型上，跨多种基准和压缩率进行评测，与逐权重压缩和联合分解基线相比，在零样本任务上取得约1-2.5个准确率点的提升。

Conclusion: 通过针对压缩过程中两个核心对齐问题的定向校正，所提出的免训练方法能够有效提升低秩压缩框架的性能，且无需额外训练成本即可获得一致的精度增益。

Abstract: Training-free low-rank compression frameworks have been gaining prominence for LLM compression given their effectiveness in reducing model parameter count while maintaining task-level accuracy. However, existing SOTA frameworks share two key limitations: (1) residual errors in calibration data activations accumulate across layers during compression, causing misalignment between representations simulated at compression time and those experienced at inference; (2) the assumption that layer importance distribution is preserved post-compression does not hold. Together, these two effects introduce misalignment in the compression process in relation to the deployed model. We study these effects and propose a simple, training-free methodology compatible with existing frameworks to mitigate them, comprising: (1) Layer-by-Layer Compression with Calibration Correction; (2) Iterative Compression with Rank Allocation Correction. Implemented atop an existing SOTA decomposition framework, and evaluated on Llama and Qwen3 models across various benchmarks and compression rates, our approach demonstrates up to ~1-2.5 accuracy point improvements over per-weight and joint decomposition baselines on zero-shot tasks.

</details>


### [48] [Time Present and Time Past: Benchmarking Large Language Models on Temporally Evolving Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08512)
*Mahbub E Sobhani,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi,Fahmid Hasan Chowdhury,Md Adnan Arefeen,Farig Sadeque,Md. Faizul Bari,Swakkhar Shatabda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TIDE基准，一个包含3,050个问答对、覆盖644份孟加拉国官方海关文件的专家验证数据集，用于测试时变文档中的"版本解析"能力。评估9个LLM后发现最佳宏平均准确率仅68.5%，模型在拒绝错误版本（26.7%）上远弱于接受正确版本（59.7%），且倾向于遵循自信的参数化答案而非提供的权威文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间敏感问答数据集仅将时间视为附加标注，没有真正测试"版本解析"这一核心挑战——即在文档被修订、替换或回退的情况下，确定查询日期对应生效的版本。已有事实性知识问答中旧事实被简单覆盖，但法律、税务码和软件文档等演化文档中，修订本身是官方文本，旧版本在有效期内仍保持正确，需要新的评估方法。

Method: 构建TIDE基准：(1) 从1969至2025年孟加拉国政府发布的644份官方海关文书中生成3,050个QA对，覆盖8种任务类型；(2) 文档为深度代码混合文本，布局异构且采用双历法标注日期；(3) 采用统一的评估协议，比较参数化访问、金标上下文和检索访问三种模式下9个最新LLM的表现；(4) 使用三裁判LLM委员会评分，并设置严格的日期门控以区分语义正确与时间正确。

Result: 最佳宏平均准确率仅68.5%；从隐式日期解析版本准确率为59.7%；检测给定版本不适用于查询的准确率仅26.7%。模型更擅长找到正确版本而非拒绝错误版本，且倾向于跟随自信的参数化答案而非提供的权威文本。

Conclusion: TIDE揭示了现有LLM在时变文档版本解析上的显著不足，特别是"拒绝错误版本"这一关键能力严重缺失。当前模型在面对权威文本与参数化记忆冲突时不可靠，实际部署在高风险法律/行政场景前仍需大幅改进。

Abstract: Evolving documents, such as laws, tax codes, and software documentation, are amended, replaced, and sometimes reverted over time, so a question has different correct answers at different dates. In contrast to encyclopedic knowledge, where an old fact is simply overwritten, an amendment is itself an official text that states what it replaces and when it takes effect, and the earlier version stays correct for its validity period. The central challenge is therefore version resolution, that is, identifying the version in force on the queried date. Existing temporal QA datasets treat time only as an annotation, so version resolution stays untested. We present TIDE, an expert-verified benchmark of 3,050 QA pairs over 644 official customs instruments issued between 1969 and 2025 by the Government of Bangladesh, covering eight task types over deeply code-mixed documents that are heterogeneous in layout and dated in two calendars. In addition, we evaluate nine recent LLMs under a single protocol across parametric, gold-context, and retrieval access, scored by a three-judge LLM council with a hard date gate separating correct meaning from correct time. The best macro-averaged accuracy is only 68.5%. Resolving a version from an implicit date reaches 59.7%, and detecting that the supplied version does not govern the query reaches only 26.7%. Models are more likely to find correct versions than to reject incorrect ones, and they tend to follow a confident parametric answer over the supplied authoritative text. All code and data are available at https://github.com/icsetepa44/TIDE

</details>

*Minhan Cho,Jimin Kweon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文独立复现了两种提高LLM推理可靠性的方法（RPC和LCF），并在四个新任务域和多种模型上进行了压力测试。RPC在作者发布的推理路径上能精确复现原始结果，但在新域上对自我一致性的优势不显著；LCF的逻辑有效性方向真实但微弱，其正面效果不显著且在其他模型上可能有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始评估由各方法作者自行完成，从未被独立复现或跨模型、跨域压力测试，且LCF未公开代码。这引发了对这些可靠性改进方法真实有效性的质疑，需要独立验证。

Method: 重新运行RPC已发布的路径聚合方法；重新实现LCF的投影器、对比学习和干预管线（因无公开代码）；将两种方法扩展至文本到SQL、法律提取、谬误识别、先例分级四个新任务域；使用Qwen3-8B及四款7-8B模型进行验证；直接探测LCF的表示特性。

Result: RPC在作者发布的推理路径上精确复现原始网格结果，但在四个新域上其相对自我一致性的优势从未显著（配对比p≥0.28）；在BIRD上预算增长时优势如预测增大，但扩大样本后最大差距从+2.5反转至-0.25。LCF的逻辑有效性方向真实但微弱（最佳子层可分性0.82，低于语义属性对照的0.95），其唯一正面效果不显著（p=0.56），且在另外两个模型中显著降低指标。

Conclusion: 两种方法的效果在独立复现和压力测试下均未能稳健成立：RPC的相对优势在跨域泛化时消失，LCF的干预效果不仅微弱还可能在部分模型上产生负面影响。这强调了独立复现和跨域验证在AI方法评估中的重要性。

Abstract: We independently reproduce two recent methods for making large language model (LLM) reasoning more reliable, and stress-test them across domains and models (RPC across four new task domains with Qwen3-8B, LCF across four 7-8B models). The first, RPC, aggregates token probabilities and self-consistency at inference; the second, LCF, trains projectors that split hidden states into "content" and "logic" and edits the logic part toward a valid region. Validating such reliability claims matters because the original evaluations are run by each method's own authors and were never independently reproduced or stress-tested across models and domains, and LCF shipped no public code. We re-run RPC's published-path aggregation and re-implement LCF's projector, contrastive, and intervention pipeline, then extend both to text-to-SQL, legal extraction, fallacy identification, and precedent grading, and probe LCF's representation directly. RPC reproduces the original grid exactly on the authors' released reasoning paths; on four new domains its edge over self-consistency is never significant (ties or small mixed differences, paired p >= 0.28), and on BIRD, the one domain where we vary the budget, the edge grows with K as predicted but its largest gap (+2.5 accuracy at K=32, p=0.16) reverses to -0.25 when we enlarge the sample to n=200. LCF's logic-validity direction is real but weak (0.82 separability at the single best sub-layer versus 0.95 for a semantic-attribute control); its one positive effect (Qwen3 $Δ$Prob) is not significant (p=0.56), while it significantly reduces $Δ$Prob on two of the other three models.

</details>


### [50] [Unaccountable Delegation, Fading Skills: Mapping the Risks of Workplace AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08601)
*Gabriele La Malfa,Lakmal Meegahapola,Edyta Bogucka,Jie M. Zhang,Michael Luck,Elizabeth Black,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向职场AI智能体的多层风险分类框架，基于文献综述建立"智能体-目标-环境"三层交互模型，将其嵌入结构化提示词后应用于O*NET数据库中2,078项工作任务，生成8,356个按严重程度和部署模式标注的风险场景，并经45名工人和独立LLM法官验证。研究者最终扩展出15类职场AI智能体风险分类体系，发现增强模式并非天然安全（过度依赖会侵蚀技能与监督）、错误智能体行为占比最高且严重风险密集于人机边界、自动化主要带来组织风险而增强主要带来工人风险，且工人对本文分类法的易用性偏好显著优于其他两种分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI风险分类体系聚焦于通用性风险，未能捕捉AI智能体在工作岗位层面引入的特定风险。随着AI智能体在职场中的广泛部署，组织亟需能够对岗位级智能体风险进行分类和评估的系统性框架，以提前预判社会技术风险。

Method: 研究方法包括：(1) 通过对AI智能体的文献综述开发一个多层框架，建模智能体、目标与环境三大核心组件及其交互；(2) 将该框架嵌入结构化提示词，应用于O*NET数据库中2,078项工作任务描述，生成8,356个按严重程度和部署模式（自动化或增强）标注的风险场景；(3) 用45名覆盖10个岗位的工人及独立LLM法官验证风险场景的合理性与任务对齐度；(4) 扩展现有分类法，构建15类职场AI智能体风险分类体系；(5) 通过工人对比实验评估新分类法的易用性。

Result: 研究产生四大发现：(1) 增强模式并非天然安全，过度依赖智能体会逐步侵蚀工人的技能与监督能力；(2) "错误智能体行为"（Erroneous Agent Actions）占风险场景的最大份额且严重风险最集中，许多风险源于人机边界；(3) 自动化主要关联组织层面风险，而增强主要关联工人层面风险；(4) 在与另外两种分类法的对比中，工人认为本文分类法在风险分类任务中更易使用，且在64%的非平局比较中偏好该框架优于新兴的生成式AI风险分类法。

Conclusion: 职场AI智能体风险不仅源于智能体本身，还取决于人类如何与智能体协作以及智能体的部署方式。实现更安全的工作场所需要双管齐下：既要有更安全的智能体，也需要精心设计的人机协作机制。

Abstract: To anticipate socio-technical risks from AI agents, organizations need taxonomies to classify them. However, existing AI risk taxonomies focus on broad risks and do not capture job-specific risks introduced by agents. To address this gap, we make three main contributions. First, we developed a multi-layer framework from a literature review of AI agents. The framework models three core components and their interactions: agents, goals, and environment. Second, we embedded this framework in a structured prompt and applied it to descriptions of 2,078 job tasks from the O*NET database, producing 8,356 risk scenarios labeled by severity and deployment mode (automation or augmentation). We validated these scenarios with 45 workers across 10 job roles and an independent LLM judge, confirming their plausibility and alignment with job tasks. Finally, we extended an existing taxonomy to create a 15-category taxonomy of workplace AI agent risks that covers all our risk scenarios. Our analysis highlights four findings. First, augmentation is not inherently safe because overreliance on agents can gradually erode workers' skills and oversight. Second, Erroneous Agent Actions accounts for the largest share of risk scenarios and has the highest concentration of severe risks. Many arise at the human-agent boundary. Third, automation is associated mainly with organizational risks, while augmentation is associated mainly with risks to workers. Fourth, workers found our taxonomy easier to use for a risk classification task than two other taxonomies and preferred it in 64% of non-tied comparisons with a recent generative AI risk taxonomy. These findings show that workplace AI agent risks do not arise from agents alone; they also depend on how people work with agents and how agents are deployed. Safer workplaces require not only safer agents but also carefully designed human-AI agent collaboration.

</details>


### [51] [ForestBench: A Unified Graph Framework for Evaluating Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08605)
*Guo Chen,Ziwen Li,Reed Li,Yu Lu,Haibo Shi,Bingbing Xu,Junjie Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ForestBench，一个基于统一协作图表示的多智能体系统（MAS）通用评估框架，将异构MAS执行轨迹映射到共享空间，通过与查询相关的参考森林（由已验证成功图组成）进行比较，无需额外LLM推理即可在毫秒级完成评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS评估存在两大问题：基于结果的基准无法捕捉智能体间的协作过程，而LLM-as-Judge评估依赖额外推理且结果随LLM和评分标准波动。异构MAS执行轨迹缺乏统一的比较基础，导致不同方法之间难以进行公平、可复现的评估。

Method: 将原生MAS轨迹映射为统一协作图，与查询特定的参考森林（每个森林由基准提供的已验证成功图集合构成，记录多种完成任务方式）进行比较。从7个公开数据集中筛选844个需要协作的查询，为每个查询预计算10个成功目标条件的参考图，评估6个代表性MAS框架，并通过控制骨干、参考构建和扰动研究测试评估的稳定性和范围。

Result: 框架实例化为ForestBench后，评估了6个代表性MAS框架；构建基准森林后，对单个轨迹的评分仅需毫秒级计算，无需进一步的LLM推理，提供可复用的结构评估基础。

Conclusion: ForestBench为不同MAS方法提供了统一的表示、参考集和指标面板，实现了可重用、高效且模型无关的协作轨迹评估，是结果型基准和LLM-as-Judge之外的第三种、更结构化的评估范式。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on Large Language Models (LLMs) are proliferating rapidly, but their heterogeneous execution traces provide no common basis for evaluation across methods. Outcome-only benchmarks discard collaborations, whereas LLM-as-Judge evaluation requires additional, model-dependent inference and can vary with the LLM and rubric. We introduce a generalizable evaluation framework that maps native MAS traces into a shared space of unified collaboration graphs, enabling different methods to be evaluated under the same representation, reference set, and metric panel. Candidate graphs are compared with a query-specific reference forest. Each forest is a benchmark-provided collection of verified-success graphs: it records diverse ways in which representative MAS methods can complete the task, rather than prescribing a unique optimal process. Instantiating the framework as ForestBench, we filter $844$ collaboration-necessary queries from seven public datasets, precompute ten successful target-conditioned reference graphs per query, and evaluate six representative MAS frameworks. Controlled backbone, reference-construction, and perturbation studies test the stability and scope of evaluation. Once the benchmark forests are built, ForestBench scores a trace in milliseconds without further LLM inference, providing a reusable structural basis for comparing diverse MAS collaboration traces.

</details>


### [52] [Business Arena: Benchmarking LLM Agents in a Realistic Marketplace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08621)
*Yijun Pan,Yukun Lian,Kunyu Shi,Junbo Li,Hongwei Xue,Sicong Xie,Guannan Zhang,Xiaoying Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Business Arena，一个基于真实阿里巴巴采购数据和权威市场数据校准的受控环境，用于评估AI智能体在跨境电商场景中的端到端商业运营能力。评估15个前沿模型后发现在平均最终净资产上存在9倍差异，且即使最佳模型也落后于人类设计的策略，表明商业运营对LLM智能体仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM智能体已能完成复杂工作流，但现有智能体基准很少评估与商业相关的综合能力。经营企业需要从部分信号推断机会、在不确定下投入资本、适应延迟结果并满足合规要求，这种复杂的端到端商业能力缺乏可信的评估框架。

Method: 构建Business Arena受控环境，让AI智能体在长周期内经营跨境商店（从供应商采购并向买家销售）。环境以真实Alibaba.com采购数据为基础，使用权威来源校准市场条件。采用三种互补评估方式：与人类设计策略对比以估计机会空间、技能级指标揭示强弱项、行动级归因追踪盈亏来源。同时通过机制消融排除捷径和模拟器特定漏洞。

Result: 对15个前沿模型进行评估，发现平均最终净资产存在9倍差异；最佳模型仍落后于人类设计策略。技能级分析揭示了不同运营风格（从聚焦利润率的溢价卖家到高周转批发商和客服专家），行动级归因识别出采购、定价和恢复决策对价值创造或毁灭的影响。

Conclusion: Business Arena是迈向真实可信的端到端商业智能体评估基准的第一步。它证明了商业运营对LLM智能体仍具挑战性，并为理解智能体的商业决策能力和运营风格提供了多维度的分析框架。

Abstract: Running a business is a challenging form of intelligent work. Operators must infer opportunities from partial signals, commit capital under uncertainty, adapt to delayed outcomes in a changing market, and satisfy regulatory obligations before trading legally. Frontier LLM agents can increasingly complete complex workflows, yet business-related capabilities are rarely evaluated in existing agent benchmarks. We introduce \textbf{Business Arena}, a controlled environment where an AI agent runs a cross-border shop, buying from suppliers and selling to buyers over a long horizon. We ground the arena in real Alibaba.com sourcing data and market conditions calibrated from authoritative sources. Delayed and coupled consequences make individual business decisions difficult to judge, but their combined outcome is measurable through profit. Because profit alone cannot explain why an agent succeeds or fails, we compare agents with human-designed strategies to estimate available opportunity, use skill-level metrics to reveal underlying strengths and weaknesses, and trace realized gains and losses to the actions that produced them. We use mechanism ablations to establish that strong results reflect genuine business intelligence rather than neglect or simulator-specific shortcuts. We evaluate 15 frontier models and find a ninefold difference in mean final net worth. Even the best model falls behind human-designed strategies, indicating that business operation remains challenging for LLM agents. Skill-level analysis reveals operating styles, from margin-focused premium sellers to high-turnover wholesalers and customer-service specialists, while action-level attribution identifies the sourcing, pricing, and recovery decisions that create or destroy value. Together, Business Arena takes a first step toward a realistic and trustworthy testbed for evaluating end-to-end business agents.

</details>


### [53] [SkillReason: Reasoning-Enhanced Agent Skill Retrieval for Implicit User Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08640)
*Donghong Jiang,Endian Lin,Luoping Cui,Hanqing Liu,Mingjie Liu,Fan Yang,Hong Wang,Zhao Yang,Chuang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillReason-Bench基准（含3,729个查询和61,228个技能，覆盖九个领域）及SkillReason两阶段框架，通过思维链推理作为训练时监督来增强技能检索，在三个基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体依赖可复用技能扩展能力，但从大规模技能库中检索合适技能面临挑战，因为用户请求通常简洁且未明示所需能力和执行步骤。现有基准对此类请求覆盖有限。

Method: SkillReason采用两阶段框架：Stage I利用强教师模型生成能力推理轨迹，通过对比学习、检索分布对齐和语言建模提供显式监督；Stage II采用检索引导的GRPO目标，鼓励模型探索更适合自身能力和更利于检索的推理轨迹。推理时直接编码原始查询，无需自回归思维链生成。

Result: 在SkillReason-Bench、SkillRet和SRA-Bench三个基准上均取得最先进性能，证明推理增强训练能更好地弥合高层任务目标与技能能力之间的语义鸿沟。

Conclusion: 推理增强的训练方法能够有效提升技能检索性能，同时保持查询-only检索的高效性。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on reusable skills to extend their capabilities beyond parametric knowl- edge. However, retrieving the appropriate skill from a large- scale library remains challenging because realistic user re- quests are often concise and underspecified, stating only the task goal while leaving the required capabilities and execu- tion steps implicit. Existing benchmarks provide limited cov- erage of such requests. To address this gap, we introduce SkillReason-Bench, a large-scale cross-domain benchmark containing 3,729 queries and a retrieval corpus of 61,228 skills spanning nine domains. We further propose SkillRea- son, a two-stage framework that uses chain-of-thought rea- soning as training-time supervision for skill retrieval. In Stage I, capability reasoning traces generated by a stronger teacher provide explicit supervision through contrastive learning, re- trieval distribution alignment, and language modeling, en- couraging the retriever to internalize capability reasoning in its query representation. In Stage II, a retrieval-guided GRPO objective encourages the model to explore reasoning trajecto- ries better suited to its own capabilities and more effective for retrieval. At inference, SkillReason directly encodes the orig- inal query without autoregressive CoT generation, preserv- ing efficient query-only retrieval. Extensive experiments on SkillReason-Bench, SkillRet, and SRA-Bench show that Skill- Reason achieves state-of-the-art performance across all three benchmarks, demonstrating that reasoning-enhanced training better bridges the semantic gap between high-level task goals and skill capabilities.

</details>


### [54] [EnergyBridge: Benchmarking Household Energy Management, User Participation, and Grid Flexibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08691)
*Xudong Wu,Zeqing Wu,Jiarui Zhang,Xuhao Fan,Ziang Ding,Yuming Zhuang,Mingqi Yuan,Yilun Du,Hongjie Jia,Yunfei Mu,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnergyBridge是一个连接容量报告、住户授权与物理执行的住宅虚拟电厂基准与智能体框架，通过LLM驱动的用户参与模拟器评估"物理灵活性"向"可靠容量"的转化，并在天津和柏林两个区域场景中实现最高模拟授权率与最可靠的容量承诺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅评估住宅VPP的控制性能，忽略了"事件特定授权"这一关键环节——即住户是否批准灵活用电计划以及实际响应是否兑现。物理灵活性只有在居民授权并交付承诺响应后才成为可信容量，因此需要一个将授权纳入评估的基准框架。

Method: 构建EnergyBridge框架，包含：(1)针对天津和柏林的基于EnergyPlus的区域特定仿真环境；(2)LLM驱动的用户参与模拟器(User Participation Simulator)；(3)连接容量报告、住户授权与物理执行三环节的完整流程；并对照584个由真人角色扮演的、按人物与事件匹配的评判结果进行验证。

Result: LLM用户参与模拟器与人类评判相比保持方法排序一致性，平均绝对接受误差为5.3个百分点；在传统控制器与智能体基线对比中，EnergyBridge在两地均获得最高模拟授权率、最低事件窗口能耗和最可靠的容量承诺。

Conclusion: 将住户授权纳入VPP评估是可行的且必要的，LLM模拟器能以较小误差代替人类角色扮演，支持可复现的、以人为中心的电网灵活性研究。

Abstract: Residential virtual power plants (VPPs) can provide grid flexibility by shifting household demand, but physical flexibility becomes dependable capacity only when residents authorize a plan and the promised response is delivered. Existing benchmarks evaluate control but omit event-specific authorization. We present EnergyBridge, a benchmark and agent framework connecting capacity reporting, household authorization, and physical execution. It combines region-specific EnergyPlus environments for Tianjin and Berlin with an LLM-based User Participation Simulator. Against 584 persona- and event-matched human role-play judgments, the LLM-based User Participation Simulator preserves method ordering with a 5.3-point mean absolute acceptance error. Across conventional controllers and agent baselines, EnergyBridge achieves the highest simulated authorization, lowest event-window energy, and the most reliable capacity commitment in both regions. We release human data and codes for reproducible human-centered grid-flexibility research: https://github.com/Agentic-Intelligence-Lab/EnergyBridge.

</details>


### [55] [PluginEval: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Fine-Grained Error Attribution in Function Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08700)
*Dongjie Xu,Julius,Hanchi Dong,Minghua Tang,Yuxuan Sun,Ziwei Nie,Zicheng Liu,Dujun Qing,Jiajie Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了PluginEval基准，通过两阶段框架系统性地缓解工具路由评估中的三大结构缺陷（幂律分布、缺乏对抗性难负样本、未经验证的LLM标注），实现了可靠的工具路由评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具路由基准存在三个结构性问题：(1)数据分布遵循幂律，导致罕见场景代表性不足；(2)缺乏对抗性困难负样本，难以区分不同模型的性能差异；(3)标注流程依赖未经执行验证的LLM判断，可靠性存疑。这些问题限制了LLM作为自主智能体时工具路由能力的准确评估。

Method: PluginEval采用两阶段框架：(1)将工具路由建模为三个决策序列，分离生成与验证——LLM提出候选调用，确定性验证与真实API执行提供可靠质量信号；(2)按能力、意图、边界三个维度分解每个插件，识别触发与排除场景，在不同难度级别生成查询以填补覆盖缺口，包括针对三种失败模式的对抗性负样本，并返回第一阶段进行标注，形成闭环迭代直至覆盖收敛。评估方面，LLM评判器锚定金标准标注，将失败分类为漏调用、虚假调用或参数错误，生成详细错误画像。

Result: 评估了五个模型家族（含专有模型和开源权重模型），分析了它们在不同难度级别和错误类别上的表现，并通过与人类标注的一致性验证了评判器的可靠性。

Conclusion: PluginEval通过闭环的两阶段构建框架和细粒度的错误分类评估，为工具路由提供了一种更可靠、更全面的基准测试方法，克服了现有基准的结构性局限。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of tool routing is critical as Large Language Models increasingly operate as autonomous agents. Current benchmarks face three structural limitations: data distributions that follow a power law leave rare scenarios underrepresented; the absence of adversarial hard negatives obscures performance differences across models; and annotation pipelines depend on LLM judgments that have not been validated through execution. In this paper, we introduce PluginEval, a benchmark constructed through a two-stage framework that systematically mitigates these limitations. First, we formulate tool routing as a sequence of three decisions and separate generation from verification. LLMs propose candidate calls, while deterministic validation and real API execution provide reliable quality signals. Second, we decompose each plugin by capability, intent, and boundary to identify trigger and exclusion scenarios. We then generate queries at different difficulty levels to fill coverage gaps, including adversarial negatives targeting three failure modes, and return them to the first stage for annotation. This process creates a closed loop that iterates until coverage converges. For evaluation, we move beyond aggregate accuracy. An LLM judge anchored to gold annotations classifies failures as missed calls, spurious calls, or parameter errors, producing a detailed error profile for each model. We evaluate five model families, including proprietary models and models with open weights, analyze their performance across difficulty levels and error categories, and validate the judge through agreement with human annotations.

</details>


### [56] [SymDiag: Explainable Diagnosis for LLM Reasoning via Neuro-Symbolic Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08786)
*Wenyao Cui,Huaping Zhang,Yongyi Huang,Qiuchi Li,Jian Xu,Cheng-Lin Liu,Chunxiao Gao,Juan Wang,Baohua Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SymDiag是一个神经符号框架，将LLM的思维链验证重构为结构化失败诊断，通过将自然语言CoT翻译为符号约束并执行逐步可满足性/蕴含检查，结合Self-Auditor区分翻译误差与推理误差，实现可验证的推理诊断与修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理验证信号缺乏诊断性：答案匹配只观察最终结果，LLM-as-judge提供主观且不可验证的批评，标量奖励（如PRM/RM）无法定位多步推导中的具体失败位置，导致即使最终答案正确也无法发现不忠实的思维链。

Method: SymDiag将自然语言思维链翻译为符号约束，执行逐步可满足性/蕴含检查以定位失败步骤并生成可验证的诊断证据（反例、不一致见证、缺失前提指示）。核心创新是Self-Auditor模块，通过双重符号编码的一致性检查来区分翻译误差与推理误差，从而在部分可观测条件下实现鲁棒诊断。

Result: 在数学、逻辑、科学和通用推理等多个基准上，SymDiag相比仅基于结果验证和LLM评判方法，显著提升了不忠实推理的检测能力，并为多轮推理修复提供了更有效的反馈。

Conclusion: SymDiag为可信且可扩展的推理诊断提供了原则性基础，弥合了神经符号转换噪声与真实推理缺陷之间的鸿沟，为LLM推理验证开辟了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve as data-driven reasoners, yet their chains-of-thought (CoT) can be unfaithful even when final answers are correct. Most existing ``verification'' signals are not diagnostic: answer matching observes only the outcome, LLM-as-judge provides subjective and non-verifiable critiques, and scalar rewards (e.g., PRMs/RMs) offer little insight into where a multi-step derivation fails.We propose \textbf{SymDiag}, a neuro-symbolic framework that \textbf{reframes reasoning verification as structured failure diagnosis}. SymDiag translates natural-language CoT into symbolic constraints and performs step-level satisfiability/entailment checks to (i) localize failing steps and (ii) produce verifiable diagnostic evidence, including counterexamples, inconsistency witnesses, and missing-premise indicators. A central challenge is that apparent ``logic violations'' can be caused either by genuine reasoning defects or by neural-to-symbolic translation noise. SymDiag therefore incorporates a Self-Auditor that disentangles TranslationError from ReasoningError via dual symbolic encodings consistency checks, enabling robust diagnosis under partial observability. Across diverse mathematical, logical, scientific, and general reasoning benchmarks, SymDiag improves detection of unfaithful reasoning and provides substantially more effective feedback for multi-round reasoning repair than outcome-only verification and LLM-based judging, offering a principled foundation for trustworthy and scalable reasoning diagnosis.

</details>


### [57] [Deferred Audio Pruning with Local Audio-Visual Dynamics for Omni-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08794)
*Kyeongyoon Lee,Hongyeob Kim,Youngeun Kim,Sungeun Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A-PACK是一个两阶段的全模态LLM压缩框架，在LLM边界前后分别对视频和音频token进行压缩。它将音频保留并利用局部视听动态压缩视频，然后在LLM内部渐进式剪枝低相关性音频和视觉token及其KV-cache条目。在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B/3B上的四个基准测试中，A-PACK取得了最强平均性能，同时将prefill FLOPs减少最多78%，解码吞吐量提升最多2.21倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全模态（音频、视频、文本）LLM在处理长多模态序列时面临巨大的prefill和KV-cache开销。已有压缩方法主要集中在LLM之前的token减少，而跨LLM边界的模态特定压缩尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出A-PACK两阶段框架：(1) LLM之前阶段——基于分析发现音频比视频具有更高的任务相关信息密度和表征多样性，保留音频，利用局部视听动态（而非逐token匹配）来压缩视频token；(2) LLM内部阶段——随着查询条件化的多模态交互逐步形成，渐进式剪枝低相关性的音频和视觉token及其对应的KV-cache条目。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B/3B上的四个基准测试中，A-PACK在已评估的先前方法中取得了最强的平均性能，同时将prefill FLOPs减少最多78%，解码吞吐量提升最多2.21倍。

Conclusion: A-PACK验证了跨LLM边界的模态特定压缩策略的有效性，证明了两阶段压缩框架能够在保持最强平均性能的同时大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: Omni-modal LLMs jointly process audio, video, and text, but long multimodal sequences incur substantial prefill and KV-cache costs. Existing omni-modal compression methods primarily focus on pre-LLM token reduction, leaving modality-specific compression across the LLM boundary underexplored. We propose A-PACK, a two-stage framework that defers audio pruning until query-conditioned multimodal interactions emerge. Our analysis shows that audio exhibits higher task-relevant information density and representational diversity per token than video. We further find that local audio-visual dynamics provide a more effective cue for visual selection than token-wise matching. We therefore preserve audio and compress video with local dynamics before the LLM, then progressively prune low-relevance audio and visual tokens and their KV-cache entries inside the LLM. Across four benchmarks on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B/3B, A-PACK achieves the strongest average performance among the evaluated prior methods while reducing prefill FLOPs by up to 78% and improving decoding throughput by up to 2.21x.

</details>


### [58] [Findings of the First Teaching Monster Challenge: A Benchmark of Pedagogical Content Knowledge in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08852)
*Yi-Cheng Lin,Yu-Kai Guo,Szu-Chi Chen,Bo-Han Feng,Yun-Man Hsu,Hsiang Hsieh,Yu-Jung Lin,Yue-Ling Wu,Jia-Kai Dong,An-Yu Cheng,Yu-Han Huang,Lok-Lam Ieong,Kuan-Yu Chen,Ming-Douo Tchouang,Shao-Hua Sun,Che Lin,Jian-Jiun Ding,Hung-yi Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Teaching Monster Challenge，这是首个将学习者人设作为显式评估标准的教学视频生成基准，通过LLM评判、众包投票和专家评审三重机制评估AI系统能否将课程内容适配特定学习者（即教育领域的PCK能力）。首次评测发现当前系统内容生成能力强但呈现与适配能力弱，同时暴露了LLM自动评判的局限：能区分低性能尾部但无法正确排序最强系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理已能解决问题、像领域专家一样回答并生成长篇多模态内容，但其是否能根据特定学习者调整教学（即教育学中的学科教学知识PCK）尚未被系统评测。现有基准缺乏将学习者人设作为评估维度的教学视频生成测试，因此需要一个能衡量AI教学适配能力的基准。

Method: 构建Teaching Monster Challenge基准：给每个系统一个主题和一个学习者人设，要求生成完整教学视频；每个视频由LLM评判筛选、众筹配对投票排名、专家小组最终审定。基于首次评测分析LLM评判与人类偏好的差异。

Result: 首次评测表明当前系统在内容处理方面表现良好，但在内容呈现和针对学习者的适配方面远为薄弱。同时发现自动评判的局限：LLM评判能分离出明显的低性能尾部，但对最强系统的排名不准确——最强系统获得几乎相同的评判分数，其排名与人类偏好不一致。

Conclusion: 研究进展不仅需要更好的教学系统，也需要更好的自动评判器。作者发布基准、评分准则和人类评判结果，作为教学系统和自动评判器两方面的测试平台。

Abstract: AI agents can now solve problems, answer like subject experts, and generate long-form multimodal content. However, whether they can adapt a lesson to fit a specified learner, which education calls Pedagogical Content Knowledge (PCK), has not been benchmarked. To measure it, we introduce the Teaching Monster Challenge, the first instructional video generation benchmark to treat the learner persona as an explicit evaluation criterion. Each system is given a topic and a learner persona and must generate a complete instructional video. Every video is screened by an LLM-judge, ranked by crowd pairwise voting, and finalized by an expert panel. The first edition shows that today's systems handle the content well but are far weaker at presenting it and adapting it to the learner. The same process exposes a limit of automatic judging. The LLM-judge separates a clear low-performing tail but ranks the strongest systems poorly. The strongest systems receive nearly identical scores from the judge, so its ranking of them does not match human preference. Progress therefore requires not only better teaching systems but also better automatic judges, and we release the benchmark, rubric, and human judgments as a testbed for both.

</details>


### [59] [AquiLLM: An Architecture for Supporting Tacit Knowledge Capture in Research Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08883)
*Jack Stark,Srinath Saikrishnan,Vikram Seenivasan,Bernie Boscoe,Andrew Lizarraga,Tuan Do*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AquiLLM——一个开源模块化RAG-LLM框架的重大升级，通过本地嵌入与重排序、多模态能力、语义与情景记忆、通用接口及技能支持等改进，帮助科研团队捕获隐性知识，并避免专有商业AI在透明性、可复现性和隐私方面的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RAG和LLM在科学工作流中具有巨大潜力，但专有商业AI系统在透明性、可复现性和隐私方面存在不足，这些是科学实践的核心要求；此外，科研团队需要一种能捕获隐性知识的AI工具。

Method: 在AquiLLM开源框架基础上，提出一系列架构与功能改进：(1)本地嵌入和重排序；(2)多模态能力；(3)OpenAI兼容推理接口；(4)用户界面改进；(5)语义与情景记忆；(6)技能支持。改进基于与天体物理学家和环境研究者的领域专家讨论。

Result: 改进后的AquiLLM更加贴近科学研究实践需求，在透明性、可复现性和隐私方面提供保障，同时通过多模态、记忆和技能功能增强了框架的实用性，具体实验结果摘要中未提供。

Conclusion: AquiLLM的架构与功能升级代表着向更符合科研实践、支持隐性知识捕获的开放AI系统迈出的重要一步，为科研团队提供了专有商业AI的可信替代方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and large language models (LLMs) enable researchers to integrate AI into scientific workflows. However, using proprietary commercial AI systems raises concerns about transparency, reproducibility and privacy, which are essential for scientific practices. To this end, AquiLLM was developed as an open-source modular RAG-LLM framework using open-weight models, designed to support research groups in capturing tacit knowledge. In this work, we present a series of architectural improvements and feature enhancements to AquiLLM, including local embedding and reranking, multimodal capabilities, OpenAI-compatible inference interfaces, user interface improvements, semantic and episodic memory capabilities, and skills support. These enhancements were informed by discussions with domain experts, including astrophysicists and environmental researchers, and represent a step toward AI systems more closely aligned with scientific research practices.

</details>


### [60] [LLM Reasoning for Subjective Tasks: Failure Modes, Mitigation, and Dynamic Reasoning Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08889)
*Juncheng Dong,Ding Tong,Ishan Gupta,Yuyan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究揭示，将LLM作为主观领域验证器时，基于数学推理的RLVR训练方法会引发"推理坍缩"现象，并提出了基于条件长度惩罚的后训练算法和上下文对齐人格的中间训练架构来解决这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统的"正确性"本质上是主观的人类偏好问题，而非客观二值判断。现有RLVR方法主要针对数学等客观任务，本文质疑这种显式推理是否能够泛化到主观、人性化的工业验证标准，并探索其根本性缺陷。

Method: 1) 在四个真实生产环境推荐平台验证任务上进行涵盖专有和开源模型的大规模研究；2) 提出条件长度惩罚的后训练算法（conditional length-penalized post-training），将验证准确性与有界推理长度交织；3) 基于1500个合成人格的实验，提出通过上下文对齐人格进行推理路由的中间训练架构。

Result: 刚性数学推理轨迹会主动降低验证效果；标准RLVR会触发"推理坍缩"（策略放弃深思转向快速启发式猜测）；提出的条件长度惩罚算法能阻止坍缩并恢复性能；推理轨迹的功效与其社会语言框架紧密耦合——仅因采用不同推理人格，验证宏F1波动达0.38。

Conclusion: 主观验证中的大量误差实际上是推理风格不匹配所致。本文提供了可扩展的算法修补方案和长期架构蓝图，用于将推理模型与真实世界的主观约束对齐。

Abstract: Recommendation systems thrive on personalization, where ''correctness'' is rarely a binary truth but a matter of subjective human preference. As Large Language Models (LLMs) are deployed as autonomous verifiers of safety and quality guidelines, they face a distinctive challenge: context-aware preference alignment. Recent gains in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) are indexed mostly on objective, mathematical tasks. Through a large-scale study spanning both proprietary and open-source models on four real-world verification tasks from a production recommender platform, we ask whether explicit reasoning generalizes to subjective, human-centric industry rubrics. We expose a fundamental vulnerability: rigid, math-centric reasoning traces actively degrade verification, and applying standard RLVR triggers a phenomenon we term reasoning collapse, in which the policy abandons deliberation in favor of rapid heuristic guessing. We introduce a conditional length-penalized post-training algorithm that intertwines verification accuracy with bounded reasoning length, halting collapse and recovering performance. Finally, we show that a reasoning trace's efficacy is tightly coupled with its socio-linguistic framing: across 1500 synthesized personas, verification accuracy swings by nearly 0.38 macro-F1 depending solely on the adopted reasoning persona---evidence that much subjective-verification error is really reasoning-style mismatch. This observation motivates a mid-training architecture that routes reasoning through contextually aligned personas. This work offers both a scalable algorithmic patch and a long-term architectural blueprint for aligning reasoning models with real-world subjective constraints.

</details>


### [61] [From Manuals to Maintenance: Fine-Tuning MedGemma for Multi-Modal Imaging System Support in Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08896)
*Bernes Lorier Atabonfack,Zion Kongbi Nfo,Ahmed Tahiru Issah,Tolulope Olusuyi,Clemence Ingabire,Mohammed Hardi Abdul Baaki,Mawuli Deku,Abdulrazaq Zubair,Alyasaa Anas,Raymond Confidence,Maruf Adewole,Udunna C. Anazodo*
