TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多模态医疗设备维护问答框架（INGENZI），通过对医疗基础模型MedGemma-4b-it进行QLoRA参数高效微调，使其能够解读MRI和超声系统错误日志并生成分步维修指令，显著提升了低中收入国家医疗设备维护效率。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低中收入国家（LMICs）医疗成像设备停机是医疗服务的重大障碍，主要原因是缺乏专业生物医学工程支持。传统的设备维护依赖稀缺的专家资源，亟需AI辅助的诊断和维护工具来填补这一空白。

Method: 首先通过覆盖9个LMICs的多国调查指导数据收集，从MRI和超声系统的技术手册中整理构建了INGENZI_DatasetV1数据集（包含10,294个高质量QA上下文对），然后使用QLoRA参数高效微调技术，对MedGemma-4b-it医疗基础模型进行适配，使其能够解读系统错误日志并生成逐步设备维修指令。

Result: 与基线模型相比，微调后的模型在各项指标上均有显著提升：F1分数从0.22提升至0.38，ROUGE-2从0.18提升至0.41，BERTScore F1从0.86提升至0.91，表明模型对新的故障排查查询能生成更精确和操作上更准确的技术响应。

Conclusion: 该工作为资源受限环境下的AI辅助诊断和维护工具建立了可靠基础，证明了通过领域数据微调医疗基础模型能够有效解决LMICs中医疗设备维护支持不足的现实问题。

Abstract: Imaging device downtime is a major barrier to healthcare delivery in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs), often driven by limited access to specialized biomedical engineering support. We present a multi-modality medical equipment maintenance question-answering (QA) framework and demonstrate the fine-tuning of a medical foundation model for specialized technical troubleshooting tasks. Guided by a multi-country survey across nine LMICs, we curated technical manuals from MRI and ultrasound systems to generate the INGENZI_DatasetV1, containing 10,294 high-quality, filtered QA-context pairs. Using QLoRA-based parameter-efficient fine-tuning, we adapted the MedGemma-4b-it model to interpret system error logs and generate step-by-step equipment repair instructions. Compared to the baseline model, the fine-tuned system achieved substantial improvements across metrics, including F1 score (0.22 to 0.38), ROUGE-2 (0.18 to 0.41), and BERTScore F1 (0.86 to 0.91). These metric gains demonstrate that the model generates significantly more precise and procedurally accurate technical responses to new troubleshooting queries. This work establishes a reliable foundation for AI-assisted diagnostic and maintenance tools in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [62] [Integrated Multimodal AI System for Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning, Object Sensing, and Damage Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08935)
*Kalelo Dukuray,Israel Pina,Evan Perez,Erika Ardiles-Cruz,Jie Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的多模态AI灾害评估系统，集成RAG检索增强生成、热红外感知、视觉基础模型流水线和无线信号感知。RAG通过动态检索项目文档和损伤等级标准抑制幻觉；图检索变体在跨文档推理上优于向量检索；红外热感知克服恶劣天气下的成像限制；视觉基础模型用于合成损伤图像和严重度分类；无线感知补充了EO/IR失效场景下的检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灾害评估系统多依赖单一数据模态（如EO光学影像），在恶劣光照/天气条件下性能受限；且大语言模型在推断时存在幻觉问题，缺乏项目级文档上下文的可靠锚定；跨文档推理需求以及EO/IR失效场景下的感知需求未得到充分满足。

Method: 构建四层统一框架：(1) RAG模块将本地LLM锚定在项目文档和损伤等级分类标准上，对比静态few-shot提示，并比较向量RAG与基于实体—关系抽取构建的知识图谱RAG；(2) 红外/热红外感知用于目标检测和分割，与可见光跟踪配对实验揭示失效模式；(3) 视觉基础模型与视觉语言模型生成合成损伤图像并分类损伤严重度；(4) 探索性无线信号感知用于检测存在性、运动和灾后环境变化。

Result: 动态检索相比静态few-shot提示显著提升锚定性和事实一致性；图结构RAG在需要跨文档推理的损伤评估查询上产生更强的响应，支持混合稠密/稀疏/图感知检索；红外检测器提升广泛目标的分割精度；视觉基础模型在合成损伤分类上达到高准确率；无线感知初步证明可检测EO与IR均失效场景下的存在和运动信号。

Conclusion: 多模态融合是稳健灾害评估的核心：RAG锚定LLM减少幻觉，IR弥补EO在恶劣环境下的不足，视觉基础模型支持数据增强与验证，无线感知填补极端条件下的感知空白。未来应发展混合检索策略与多模态融合管线，以在真实灾害场景中实现更鲁棒的端到端损伤评估。

Abstract: This work presents a unified multimodal AI system for damage assessment that integrates retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) models, thermal spectrum perception, vision foundation model pipelines, and exploratory wireless signal sensing. A RAG component is developed to ground a locally hosted language model in project-specific documentation, including specialized damage level classification criteria to mitigate hallucinations during inference. Controlled comparisons against static few-shot prompting demonstrate that dynamic retrieval improves grounding and factual consistency. We further compare vector-based RAG with a knowledge graph variant constructed via entity-relation extraction, and show that graph-based retrieval produces stronger responses for damage assessment queries requiring cross-document reasoning, motivating hybrid dense, sparse, and graph-aware retrieval. To address limitations of EO imagery under adverse lighting and weather conditions, infrared (IR)/thermal sensing is employed for object detection and segmentation. Our detectors generate candidate detections, yielding improved segmentation of a broad array of objects. Paired IR versus visible spectrum tracking experiments reveal failure modes, motivating multimodal fusion for robust object detection and damage analysis. Vision foundation and vision-language models are leveraged to generate synthetic damage imagery and classify damage severity with high accuracy, supporting training and validation of downstream damage assessment models. Finally, exploratory Wireless-based sensing demonstrates potential to detect presence, motion, and post-event environmental changes where EO and IR sensing are ineffective.

</details>


### [63] [PolicyKG: An Agentic LLM Pipeline for Translating Institutional Policies into SHACL Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09028)
*Ponkrit Kaewsawee,Chaklam Silpasuwanchai,Chutiporn Anutariya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PolicyKG 是一个 LLM 流水线，自动将自然语言的政策文档转换为机器可读的 SHACL 约束：它读取政策 PDF，进行违规/许可/禁止的三分类，将其提升为一阶道义逻辑，并生成 SHACL 约束。核心创新是 Corpus Adapter——一个 YAML 词汇注册表，将 LLM 谓词锚定到目标本体上，实现跨领域迁移而无需重新训练。在 AIT 政策语料上达到 86.9% 分类准确率，SHACL 形状 F1=0.866；并通过对 443 条规则的高阶构造审计证明一阶逻辑覆盖充分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构政策以自然语言撰写，而合规检查系统需要机器可读的约束，两者之间的鸿沟目前仍依赖手工桥接。现有方法缺乏自动化地、可跨领域迁移地完成从自然语言到形式化约束转换的手段。

Method: PolicyKG 是一个四阶段 LangGraph 状态机流水线：①读取政策 PDF 并将每句话分类为义务/许可/禁止；②将标签提升为一阶道义逻辑（FOL 路径）；③生成 SHACL 约束（无法走 FOL 路径的规则直接 NL-to-SHACL 回退）；④每阶段配备验证器。核心组件是 Corpus Adapter——一个 YAML 词汇注册表，通过将 LLM 谓词锚定到目标本体实现领域迁移，替换注册表即可切换领域而无需重新训练。

Result: 在 AIT 政策语料（1,663 句，443 条规则）上，道义分类准确率 86.9%（Cohen's kappa=0.709）；三位标注者在 50 条样本上 Fleiss' kappa=0.844；69 条 SHACL 形状子集 F1=0.866；79.2% 规则走 FOL 路径成功。对 443 条规则的高阶构造审计未发现任何二阶及以上构造，FOL 充分性的 95% Clopper-Pearson 上界为 0.67%。将 AIT 注册表替换为 GDPR 注册表后，属性对齐从 1/15 提升至 11/15（p<0.001）；在 LexDeMod 租赁合同基准上 Macro F1 降至 0.370（因 "shall be entitled" 词汇不匹配）。重复运行产生哈希一致的 SHACL 输出。

Conclusion: PolicyKG 证明 LLM 驱动的管道可以高准确率地将机构政策转化为 SHACL 约束，并通过词汇注册表实现领域可移植性。审计结果仅支持单一语料上的 FOL 充分性，不能推广为机构政策的普遍结论；跨域迁移时词汇不匹配（如法律英语中的 "shall be entitled"）仍是主要瓶颈，而这正是注册表机制设计所要解决的问题方向。

Abstract: Institutional policies stay in natural language while the systems that check compliance demand machine-readable constraints. Bridging that gap is still done by hand.
  PolicyKG closes the loop. It is an LLM pipeline that reads a policy PDF, classifies each sentence as an obligation, permission, or prohibition, lifts the label into first-order deontic logic, and emits SHACL constraints. Four stages run on a LangGraph state machine with per-stage validators. The piece that matters most is the Corpus Adapter: a YAML vocabulary registry that grounds LLM predicates in a target ontology. Retargeting to a new domain means swapping the registry, not retraining a model.
  On the Asian Institute of Technology Policies and Procedures corpus (1,663 sentences, 443 rules), PolicyKG reaches 86.9% deontic classification accuracy (Cohen's kappa = .709). Three annotators independently re-label a 50-item sample and agree at Fleiss' kappa = .844. SHACL shape correctness on a 69-shape subset is F1 = .866. The FOL path handles 79.2% of rules; the rest go through a direct NL-to-SHACL fallback.
  We audited every one of the 443 rules for second- or higher-order constructs. An automated regex checklist flagged none, and a first-author pass on the 92 FOL-fallback cases confirmed the same. The exact upper 95% Clopper-Pearson bound on the true HOL rate is 0.67%. This is an audit finding for one corpus, not a proof of FOL sufficiency for institutional policy.
  Swapping the AIT registry for a GDPR registry raises exact property alignment from 1/15 to 11/15 (Fisher's exact p < .001; Cohen's h = 1.53). On the LexDeMod lease-contract benchmark (N = 200), Macro F1 drops to .370 because lease English uses "shall be entitled" for permission -- exactly the vocabulary mismatch registry swap is meant to fix. Repeated runs produce hash-identical SHACL outputs.

</details>


### [64] [Different Feedback, Different Updates: Selective Self-Learning from User Interactions for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09109)
*Xuanchen Li,Haitao Li,Yujia Zhou,Qingyi Pan,Heng Wang,Yiqun Liu,Min Zhang,Qingyao Ai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SLIFT，一种基于任务相对视角的选择性自学习框架，将用户反馈分解为Fix、Spec和Null三类原子组件，通过训练Generalist和Specialist两个互补的LoRA适配器来在适当的泛化范围内整合反馈，实现持续改进LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户反馈为LLM的持续改进提供自然监督，但单条反馈消息可能包含多个具有不同泛化范围的行为变化，需要合理的分解与分类来正确地整合这些变化。

Method: SLIFT首先将每条反馈消息分解为原子组件，并相对于原始任务将其分类为Fix（任务有效性要求）、Spec（条件特定细化）和Null（无可靠正向更新方向的内容）。随后在两个互补的LoRA适配器上进行训练：Generalist通过反馈条件下的自蒸馏将Fix要求整合到默认行为中；Specialist仅观察任务和Generalist响应，为适用的、未满足的Spec细化提供残差指导。Null组件不产生正向更新。

Result: 在多种骨干网络上，SLIFT在MemoryBench和WildFB基准上均取得了强劲性能，并通过针对性分析进一步研究了其底层机制。

Conclusion: SLIFT通过任务相对视角的反馈分解和双适配器架构，有效区分不同泛化范围的反馈，实现精准的LLM持续学习与改进。

Abstract: User feedback offers natural supervision for persistent LLM improvement, but a single message may support multiple behavioral changes with different scopes of generalization. We introduce SLIFT, a selective self-learning framework built on a task-relative view of user feedback. SLIFT decomposes each feedback message into atomic components and interprets each component relative to the original task as Fix, Spec, or Null: requirements for task validity, compatible condition-specific refinements, or content with no reliable positive update direction. To incorporate each change at the appropriate scope, SLIFT trains two complementary LoRA adapters on a shared frozen backbone: a Generalist that consolidates Fix requirements into default behavior through feedback-conditioned self-distillation, and a Specialist that observes only the task and Generalist response to supply residual guidance for applicable, unmet Spec refinements. Null components induce no positive update. Across backbones, SLIFT achieves strong performance on both MemoryBench and WildFB, with targeted analyses further examining its underlying mechanisms. We release our code at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SLIFT.

</details>


### [65] [MELLON - Multimodal Enhanced LLM for Online Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09121)
*Ruiyu Li,Haoyang Cai,Zhitong Guo,Tong Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文聚焦于WebShop基准上的网络导航智能体，提出三种多模态增强方法（MELLON、VQAgent、Multimodal Ranker），用于对齐文本与图像并提升多模态推理与规划能力。其中MELLON仅训练一个epoch即在任务完成准确率上提升9.26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的网络导航基线的基线要么是单模态的，要么在处理多模态输入时缺乏强大的推理能力，限制了其在真实网站环境中的导航表现。

Method: 提出三种多模态增强方案：MELLON（多模态增强LLM在线导航）、VQAgent以及Multimodal Ranker，通过在WebShop基准上探索文本与图像的对齐以及多模态推理与规划能力来增强智能体性能。

Result: MELLON在仅一个训练epoch后即实现了任务完成准确率9.26%的显著提升，证明了多模态方法对网络导航任务的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明需要在多模态方法上进行更深入的探索，尤其是更充分的训练和对齐策略，以进一步提升网络导航智能体的有效性。

Abstract: Web navigation agents are capable of addressing various types of tasks on different websites. Current baselines on web navigation are either unimodal or lack strong reasoning abilities given multimodal inputs. Focusing on the WebShop benchmark, a real-world website simulation, we explore the alignment of text and images, as well as multimodal reasoning and planning abilities, to enhance the performance of web navigation agents. We propose three innovative multimodal enhancements: Multimodal Enhanced LLM for Online Navigation (MELLON), VQAgent, and Multimodal Ranker. MELLON demonstrates a significant improvement in task completion accuracy, with a 9.26% increase after just one epoch of training. Our findings suggest the necessity of further exploration into multimodal approaches, with a focus on more extensive training and alignment strategies to enhance the effectiveness of web navigation agents.

</details>


### [66] [From Relevance to Execution Utility: Reward-Aware Dynamic Execution Gating for Skill-Based LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09168)
*Liang He,Jingbo Wen,Hongyu Gu,Hao Li,Haoyu Wang,Yixiong Chen,Kangning Cui,Xilu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RADEG（Reward-Aware Dynamic Execution Gate），一种轻量级、与检索器无关的决策层，位于技能检索与智能体执行之间。RADEG通过训练低成本的替代模型预测"查询-技能包"对的执行效用，在昂贵rollout启动前决定是否执行检索到的技能包，并通过局部扰动技术获得监督信号，部署时仅更新逻辑回归头实现低成本自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着技能库规模增大，技能检索质量虽已大幅提升，但检索到合理的技能包并不能保证执行有益。由于每个技能条件化rollout计算开销巨大，决定检索到的技能包是否应被执行已成为一个重要且有挑战性的问题。

Method: RADEG学习一个低成本的替代模型，预测查询-技能包对的执行效用。训练时通过局部扰动检索到的技能包（删除、添加或替换一个技能）产生匹配的同查询rollout，以隔离技能包组合对验证器奖励的影响并控制任务难度。部署时仅更新热启动的逻辑回归头以适应新的验证器反馈，无需重新训练检索器或智能体。

Result: 在288个收集rollout的查询级留出评估中，RADEG大幅减少了不必要的智能体执行，同时保留了大部分下游验证器奖励。在不同执行预算下始终优于基于相关性和随机的门控方法。

Conclusion: 执行感知的替代建模为技能检索提供了实用且成本有效的补充，证明了在部署前对检索到的技能包执行效用进行预测是值得的。

Abstract: Agent skills are increasingly used to equip large language model (LLM) agents with reusable procedural knowledge. Although recent work has substantially improved skill retrieval due to the increasing skill libraries, retrieving a plausible skill bundle does not guarantee that executing it is worthwhile. Since every skill-conditioned rollout is computationally expensive, deciding whether a retrieved bundle should be executed has become an increasingly important challenge. To this end, we introduce the Reward-Aware Dynamic Execution Gate (RADEG), a lightweight, retriever-agnostic decision layer between skill retrieval and agent execution. RADEG learns a low-cost surrogate model that predicts the execution utility of a query--bundle pair before the expensive rollout is launched. To obtain informative supervision while controlling for task difficulty, we locally perturb each retrieved bundle by deleting, adding, or replacing one skill, producing matched same-query rollouts that isolate the effect of bundle composition on verifier reward. During deployment, RADEG updates only a warm-started logistic head as new verifier feedback becomes available, enabling inexpensive adaptation of the execute/skip boundary without retraining either the retriever or the agent. Under a query-level held-out evaluation on 288 collected rollouts, RADEG substantially reduces unnecessary agent executions while preserving a large fraction of the downstream verifier reward. It consistently outperforms relevance-based and random gating across different execution budgets, demonstrating that execution-aware surrogate modeling provides a practical and cost-effective complement to skill retrieval.

</details>


### [67] [Agentic Router: An Execution-Grounded Continual Learning Approach With Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09184)
*Yuxuan Chen,Rongpeng Li,Zhifeng Zhao,Yuntao Liu,Xing Xu,Honggang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于执行经验的双路径"后果感知"智能体框架，用于CLI-based SONiC网络操作：提案路径通过抽象的运维经验提升可行动作覆盖率，选择路径通过真实SSH反馈的会话级LoRA更新提升动作选择质量，实验显示两条路径互补地提升了可行动作覆盖率和执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在命令行网络操作中即使生成看似合理的命令也可能执行失败或引入运维风险。现有方法仅关注命令生成或最终配置正确性，未利用执行接地（execution-grounded）的经验来同时改进候选覆盖率和动作选择质量。

Method: 提出执行接地双路径后果感知智能体（execution-grounded dual-path consequence-aware agent）：1）提案侧路径将可复用的运维经验抽象为可检索的指导信息，在不修改提案LLM的前提下提升可行动作覆盖率；2）选择侧路径通过使用真实SSH反馈对后果预测器进行会话级LoRA更新，提升条件选择质量；最后通过效用与风险感知的重排序选择最终动作。

Result: 在基于不同Qwen3提案模型的多轮SONiC操作会话实验中，框架提升了可行动作覆盖率与top-1执行成功率，且两条适应路径在交互过程中提供了互补性的增益。

Conclusion: 执行接地的双路径适应机制（经验抽象检索 + 会话级模型微调）能够有效结合候选生成与后果预测的优势，在CLI网络操作场景中实现更安全、更可靠的LLM智能体动作选择。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents provide a promising interface for command-line-based network operations, but a plausible command may still fail or introduce operational risk after execution. Existing approaches mainly focus on command generation or final configuration correctness, and do not use execution-grounded experience to jointly improve candidate coverage and action selection. We propose an execution-grounded dual-path consequence-aware agent for CLI-based SONiC operations, which generates multiple complete actions, predicts their execution consequences, and selects the final action through utility- and risk-aware reranking. The proposal-side path abstracts reusable operational lessons into retrievable guidance to improve feasible-action coverage without modifying the proposal LLM, while the selection-side path adapts the consequence predictor through session-level LoRA updates using real SSH feedback to improve conditional selection quality. Experiments over multi-turn SONiC operation sessions with different Qwen3 proposal models show that the framework improves feasible-action coverage and top-1 execution success, and that the two adaptation paths provide complementary gains over interaction.

</details>


### [68] [An Explainable GNN Framework for Component-Level Anomaly Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09246)
*Sena Ozgunay,Louise Travé-Massuyès,Jean-Michel Loubes,Raul Sena Ferreira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于图神经网络（GNN）的可解释异常检测框架，将视角从传感器级异常转移到组件级诊断，认为异常测量值是传感器间影响力改变的症状，而非传感器本身的故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的工业异常检测方法主要关注传感器级偏差，在诊断时通常将偏差最大的传感器识别为故障根因。然而在许多工业系统中，异常并非源于传感器故障，而是源于控制系统动态的传感器间影响力中断。因此需要一种从组件级出发的异常检测与诊断方法。

Method: 提出一个可解释的GNN异常检测框架，假设异常测量是传感器间影响力改变的症状，将检测视角从传感器级异常转向组件级诊断，通过分析传感器间影响的改变来识别和优先排序真正的故障组件。

Result: 实验表明该方法能够有效识别并排序真正的故障组件，为系统故障提供可解释的洞察。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了从传感器级到组件级的异常检测范式转变，能够识别传感器间影响力扰动导致的系统故障，并提供可解释的诊断结果。

Abstract: Industrial processes are complex systems composed of multiple interacting sensors that generate multivariate time series (MTS). Detecting anomalies in such systems is critical for reliability and safety, yet understanding their origin is equally important. Existing Graph Neural Network (GNN)based methods for anomaly detection primarily focus on sensor-level deviations and either attribute anomalies directly to the deviating sensors. When diagnosis is attempted, generally, the most deviated sensor is identified as a root cause of a system fault. However, in many industrial systems, anomalies do not arise from faulty sensors but from disruptions in the influences governing the system dynamics. We propose an explainable GNN-based anomaly detection framework that shifts the perspective from sensor-level anomalies to component-level diagnosis, hypothesizing that anomalous measurements are symptoms of altered inter-sensor influences. Experiments show that the method effectively identifies and prioritizes the true faulty components, providing interpretable insights into system failures.

</details>


### [69] [Emotion2Skill: Model-Internal Emotion Signals for Adaptive Skill Selection and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09248)
*Bohan Lin,Hejia Geng,Xinyi Xie,Heng Zhou,Qinghua Xing,Bo Liu,Chen Zhang,Yudong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Emotion2Skill框架，首次将LLM内部的情感表征作为决策级信号用于智能体技能选择与技能演化，而非仅用于事后分析或输出引导。在WebShop和ALFWorld上分别比Zero-Shot基线提升+26.9%和+25.5%，超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于技能的LLM智能体依赖文本级信号（任务描述、反思、经验规则）进行路由决策，忽略了模型内部表征状态这一丰富信息源。虽然已有研究证明LLM维护因果影响行为的线性情感表征，但这些表征仅用于事后分析或直接输出操纵，尚未被用于指导智能体级别的决策过程。

Method: Emotion2Skill包含两个核心环节：（1）技能选择：在每步决策时从残差流中提取27维情感状态，通过置信度门控摘要注入路由提示词；（2）技能演化：分析情感轨迹中的突变以定位问题技能调用，引导定向SOP重写，取代以往方法中粗粒度的二元结果信号。

Result: 在WebShop和ALFWorld上，Emotion2Skill配合Qwen3-8B相较Zero-Shot基线分别提升+26.9%成功率与+25.5%平均成功率，在两个基准上均超越所有基线；Qwen3-14B上也取得一致增益。共激活分析显示情感与技能之间存在语义一致的配对，验证了路由改进反映有意义的内部状态信号而非统计巧合。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了LLM内部情感表征作为智能体技能系统编排的有效决策级信号，将情感表征的用途从可解释性和输出操纵扩展至智能体决策层面，为该方向开辟了新的研究空间。

Abstract: Skill-based LLM agents select reusable procedures from an external library to solve complex tasks, yet their routing decisions rely entirely on text-level signals such as task descriptions, verbal reflections, and experience-derived rules, while the model's own internal representational state remains unobserved. Recent interpretability work has shown that LLMs maintain linear emotion representations that causally influence behavior; however, these representations have been exploited only for post-hoc analysis or direct output steering, and have not been used to inform agent-level decision-making. We propose Emotion2Skill, a framework that extracts LLM-internal emotion vectors and incorporates them into both skill selection and skill evolution. At each decision step, a 27-dimensional emotion state is extracted from the residual stream and mapped to a confidence-gated summary injected into the routing prompt. Beyond online selection, emotion trajectories are analyzed for abrupt internal-state shifts to pinpoint problematic skill invocations, guiding targeted SOP rewriting that replaces the coarse binary outcome signal of prior methods. On WebShop and ALFWorld, Emotion2Skill with Qwen3-8B improves over the Zero-Shot baseline by +26.9% success rate and +25.5% average success respectively, outperforming all baselines on both benchmarks with consistent gains on Qwen3-14B. Co-activation analysis further reveals semantically coherent emotion--skill pairings, confirming that the routing improvements reflect meaningful internal-state signals rather than opaque statistical correlations. These results establish LLM-internal emotion representations as an effective decision-level signal for orchestrating agent skill systems, extending their utility beyond interpretability and output steering. The code is available at https://github.com/BoHan-LIN04/Emotion2Skill.

</details>


### [70] [SkillSentry: Reliable Skill Execution for LLM Agents via Runtime Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09253)
*You Lu,Xinyu Huang,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillSentry是一个面向LLM智能体的技能运行时保障框架，基于新的领域特定语言（DSL）表示技能执行的运行时指导，通过结合技能规范提取和历史执行经验初始化指导，并在执行循环中监控、引导和迭代优化，平均提升任务成功率24.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体即使具备执行复杂任务的技能（通过多步推理和工具使用），在执行时仍可能不可靠——即使已在技能指导下展示了完成任务的能力，在相似任务或重复运行中仍可能因偏离技能流程或单步执行错误而失败，这种不稳定性限制了LLM智能体的实际可靠性。

Method: SkillSentry包含三个核心组件：(1) 一个新的DSL用于表示技能执行的运行时指导；(2) 通过从技能文档中提取技能规范与从历史成功/失败轨迹中挖掘执行经验相结合来初始化运行时指导；(3) 包裹在智能体执行循环周围，在现有指导下监控和引导技能执行，并利用新收集的轨迹迭代优化指导。

Result: 在15个技能、两个LLM智能体（Claude Code和Codex）、各自搭配两个骨干模型（Claude-Haiku-4.5/Claude-Opus-4.6以及GPT-5.2/GPT-5.4）上评估，SkillSentry平均提升任务成功率达24.1%，且在重复运行中展示了更低的变异性。

Conclusion: SkillSentry有效解决了LLM智能体技能执行不稳定的问题，通过运行时保障框架显著提升了任务成功率并降低了执行结果的可变性，增强了LLM智能体在实际应用中的可靠性。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly equipped with skills to perform complex tasks through multi-step reasoning and tool use. Although skills provide reusable procedural knowledge, agents may still execute them unreliably. Even when an agent has demonstrated the capability to complete tasks under the guidance of a skill, it may fail to do so consistently across similar tasks or repeated runs due to deviations from the skill procedure or incorrect execution of individual steps. Such instability limits the practical reliability of LLM agents. To address this problem, we propose SkillSentry, a skill-oriented runtime assurance framework built upon a new domain-specific language (DSL) for representing runtime guidance for skill execution. SkillSentry initializes the runtime guidance by combining a skill specification extracted from the corresponding skill document with execution experience mined from historical successful and failed traces. It then wraps around the agent execution loop to monitor and guide skill execution under the current guidance, while iteratively refining the guidance using newly collected traces. We evaluate SkillSentry on 15 skills across two LLM agents, each paired with two backbone models, i.e., Claude Code with Claude-Haiku-4.5 and Claude-Opus-4.6, and Codex with GPT-5.2 and GPT-5.4. Our results show that SkillSentry improves the task success rate of LLM agents by 24.1% across skills, on average, while exhibiting lower variability across repeated runs.

</details>


### [71] [Business Truth, not SQL Accuracy: A Rule-Gated 7B Analytics Agent Outperforms a Direct-Prompted 32B Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09254)
*Morris Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WarehouseReliabilityBench基准（400个冻结任务，覆盖两个合成数仓），约半数正确答案为澄清、弃权或拒绝，弥补了现有SQL语法指标无法评估生产环境失败场景的空白。作者开发的7B智能体QueryProof利用语义层与物理目录规则，并对每个答案进行确定性执行后校验。在80任务测试集上，QueryProof的Business Truth Rate比直接提示的32B基线高0.237，正确回答成本降低71%；同时将错误成功率从0.754降至0.351，在可回答任务上未返回任何错误数字（0 of 24）。消融显示路由层影响不大，效果主要由确定性校验层驱动，但重采样模板族后精度区间包含零，说明效果方向可靠但幅度存疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM分析智能体评估聚焦于SQL语法准确性，但生产环境的真实失败形态截然不同：多个合法业务定义、仓库无法回答的问题、schema变更后的废弃列，以及执行成功但返回错误业务数字的查询。这类失败无法用任何执行匹配指标衡量，因此需要一个反映生产可靠性需求的新基准和评估体系。

Method: 构建WarehouseReliabilityBench：400个冻结任务、两个合成仓库，约半数正确答案是澄清、弃权或拒绝四类；采用固定分母和预注册的配对bootstrap来固定声明。QueryProof为7B智能体，通过语义层和物理目录的规则决定行为，并用确定性执行后检查门控每个答案。与32B基线对比评估，包括直接提示与成本匹配的少样本设置。

Result: QueryProof在80任务测试集上以0.237的Business Truth Rate优势超越直接提示的32B基线，正确回答成本降低71%；对成本匹配的少样本基线，准确率优势保持但成本差异不显著。错误成功从0.754降至0.351；在可回答任务上0 of 24返回错误数字，但13个答案流向了需要澄清或弃权的问题。去除路由层后性能几乎不变（0.562 vs 0.537）；路由在验证集上调优后测试集过度弃权；重采样模板族使两区间包含零，说明方向可信但幅度不确定。

Conclusion: 该工作将分析智能体的评估从SQL语法正确性转向业务语义可靠性，证明小型智能体通过确定性校验层可在业务真值率上超越更大模型，且优势主要来源于确定性层而非路由层。但效果幅度受模板采样影响大，需进一步消融组件验证；整体上，生产级分析智能体的核心瓶颈不是生成SQL的能力，而是判断何时回答、如何回答以及校验答案正确性的能力。

Abstract: LLM analytics agents are evaluated on SQL syntax accuracy, but production failures look different: questions with two valid business definitions, questions the warehouse cannot answer, deprecated columns after a schema change, and queries that execute successfully while returning the wrong business number. No execution-match metric can score them. This paper introduces WarehouseReliabilityBench, 400 frozen tasks over two synthetic warehouses in which roughly half the correct responses are a clarification, an abstention or a refusal, with pinned denominators and a pre-registered paired bootstrap fixing each claim verb before the numbers existed. QueryProof, a 7B agent, uses rules derived from a semantic layer and physical catalog to determine its behaviour, and gates every answer on deterministic post-execution checks. On an 80-task synthetic test split evaluated once, QueryProof outperforms a direct-prompted 32B baseline by +0.237 [+0.112, +0.375] Business Truth Rate at 71.0% lower cost per correct answer; against a cost-matched few-shot baseline the accuracy gain holds but the cost difference does not resolve. This compares systems rather than model sizes: the 32B baseline receives none of the scaffolding. False success falls from 0.754 to 0.351 of returned answers, and no wrong number was returned on an answerable task (0 of 24), though 13 answers went to questions requiring clarification or abstention. Removing the routing layer changes little (0.562 against 0.537), so the result does not depend on escalation. Routing tuned on validation over-abstains on test, and the fitted confidence model loses to the heuristic it replaced. Resampling template families rather than tasks widens both accuracy intervals to include zero, so the effect's direction is better supported than its magnitude. The gain tracks the deterministic layer, though no component ablation was run.

</details>


### [72] [Entropy-based Code Adversarial Translation for Real-world Repository Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09273)
*Yushun Tang,Yisen Cao,Zhicheng Chen,Lin Peng,Junkang Mao,Fengyi Song,Yantao Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ECAT框架，一个基于生成器-判别器对抗架构的多智能体系统，通过熵最小化实现Android到HarmonyOS的仓库级代码迁移，并引入首个真实基准A2H-RepoBench，达到74.7%的迁移质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在代码生成和程序修复上表现出色，但整个代码仓库的迁移很少能产生可运行的应用程序，因为长程迁移任务对LLM智能体维持仓库级迁移目标的能力提出了挑战。

Method: ECAT将仓库迁移形式化为对抗熵最小化问题，采用生成器-判别器架构。判别器利用统一度量"代码熵"衡量迁移质量，并生成文本梯度以指定文件级生成指令及所需技能；生成器在这些优化信号指导下迭代更新仓库，仅当更新降低代码熵时才被接受。成功的低熵轨迹被蒸馏到自进化记忆树中，实现在仓库间的可迁移知识复用。

Result: 引入A2H-RepoBench基准（涵盖数万至数十万行代码的应用），通过节点对齐和基于智能体的功能评估，ECAT达到74.7%的整体迁移质量，并在不同规模的仓库上持续优于现有基于智能体的方法。

Conclusion: ECAT通过对抗式熵最小化和自进化记忆机制有效解决了仓库级代码迁移中的长程目标维护问题，证明了生成器-判别器对抗架构在大型软件仓库迁移任务中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: LLMs have demonstrated strong capabilities in code generation and automated program repair, but migrating an entire repository rarely produces a runnable application because long-horizon translation challenges LLM-based agents' ability to maintain repository-level migration objectives. In this work, we propose Entropy-based Code Adversarial Translation (ECAT), a multi-agent framework for automated Android-to-HarmonyOS repository migration. ECAT formulates repository migration as adversarial entropy minimization through a generator-discriminator architecture. The discriminator measures migration quality using a unified metric called Code Entropy and produces text gradients that specify both file-level generation directives and the skills needed to execute them. Guided by these optimization signals, the generator iteratively updates the repository, and each update is accepted only if it reduces Code Entropy. Repeated generator--discriminator interactions progressively drive the migration from an initial template toward a functionally complete HarmonyOS repository. Successful low-entropy trajectories are further distilled into a self-evolving memory tree, enabling transferable migration knowledge across repositories. We also introduce A2H-RepoBench, the first real-world benchmark for Android-to-HarmonyOS repository migration, covering applications from tens of thousands to hundreds of thousands of lines of code. Evaluated by node alignment and an agent-based functional judge, ECAT achieves 74.7% overall migration quality and consistently outperforms existing agent-based methods across repositories of different scales.

</details>


### [73] [P$^{3}$: Joint Program-and-Proof Planning for Verified Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09277)
*Zenan Li,Ziran Yang,Peiyang Song,Zhaoyu Li,Kaiyu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出P³，一种基于LLM的智能体工作流，先联合推导"程序+证明"的统一计划，再在此计划指导下逐步实现。同时引入Lean4Commit0基准（从真实仓库提取API需求的Lean验证任务），在多个基准和多模型上较基线提升4.6-11.2个百分点的求解率，并显著降低API成本和墙钟时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有验证代码生成采用"先合程序、后证正确"的串行流水线，但程序生成时未考虑后续证明需求，可能导致程序微妙错误或结构上难以验证，迫使LLM在修补代码与修补证明之间陷入脆弱的修复循环，既低效又无效。

Method: 受Dijkstra"程序与正确性论证应同步开发"思想的启发，P³工作流分为两个阶段：(1) 从形式规格推导统一的程序与证明联合计划（program-and-proof plan）；(2) 在此共享计划指导下细化实现与证明脚手架。此外构建Lean4Commit0基准，从真实软件仓库提取核心API并将跨API的关系规格翻译为Lean任务。

Result: 在Verina、AlgoVeri和Lean4Commit0三个基准上使用四个前沿LLM后端测试，P³在所有基准-模型组合下均取得最高求解率。相比更强基线，在困难子集上求解率提升4.6-11.2个百分点，每任务API成本降低最高约40%，墙钟时间缩短最高约37%。消融实验显示联合规划相比仅实现规划有3.3-8.3个百分点的增益。

Conclusion: 程序与证明的联合规划是提升验证代码生成关键瓶颈的有效策略；同时，仓库级别的现实基准（Lean4Commit0）对评估验证代码生成在真实场景中的表现具有重要意义。

Abstract: Verified code generation asks a large language model (LLM) to generate both an executable program and a machine-checkable proof that the program meets a formal specification, promising software that is correct by construction. The de facto workflow decouples the two halves of the problem: first synthesize a program, then attempt to prove it correct. We observe that this sequential pipeline can be both ineffective and inefficient in practice. A program generated without anticipating its proof can be subtly incorrect or structurally difficult to verify, forcing the LLM into brittle repair loops that alternate between patching the code and patching the proof. Inspired by Dijkstra's view that a program and its correctness argument should be developed hand in hand, we propose $P^3$, an LLM-based agentic workflow that first derives a unified program-and-proof plan from the specification, then elaborates the implementation and proof scaffold under this shared plan. To evaluate verified code generation in realistic settings, we further introduce Lean4Commit0, a repository-derived, library-level benchmark built by extracting core APIs from real-world software repositories and translating their requirements, including relational specifications across APIs, into Lean tasks. Using four frontier LLM backends, we evaluate $P^3$ on Verina, AlgoVeri, and our Lean4Commit0 benchmark, where it achieves the highest solve rate in every benchmark--model setting. Compared with the stronger baseline, it improves solve rates by 4.6--11.2 percentage points and reduces per-task API cost by up to roughly 40\% and wall-clock time by up to roughly 37\% on the difficult subset of each benchmark. A targeted ablation further shows gains of 3.3--8.3 points over implementation-only planning, isolating the benefit of planning the program and proof jointly.

</details>


### [74] [ComboShoppingBench: Evaluating LLM Agents for Budget-Constrained Basket Shopping with Coupons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09282)
*Adrian Li,Kelong Mao,Yudong Guo,Heming Xia,Xinwei Yang,Lirui Luo,Jace Wong,Pu Yao,Sulong Xu,Simiu Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ComboShoppingBench，一个面向开放但可验证的组合购物（basket construction）任务的智能体购物基准，在模拟电商和外卖环境中结合LLM语义评估与确定性验证来全面评测智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实购物往往需要构建由互补商品组成的购物篮（如设备配置、备餐、活动策划、团购外卖），这需要联合推理商品兼容性、库存、店铺要求、配送费、优惠券和预算等多重约束。然而此类开放式的组合购物任务难以评估：精确匹配不适用（多个篮子都能满足请求），而纯语义评估又无法检测不可行的订单、无效优惠券组合或错误支付，因此需要一个既开放又可验证的评估框架。

Method: 提出ComboShoppingBench，包含两阶段流程：（1）任务合成阶段，一个探索代理在模拟商业环境中构建可行且语义连贯的可购商品篮子作为"见证"，并据此生成优惠券、预算约束、用户查询和对齐的评估细则；（2）评估阶段，LLM评判器评估语义满意度、回答质量和声明忠实度，同时确定性校验检查产品ID有效性、预算合规性和优惠券最优性，实现语义与形式化验证的双轨评估。

Result: 在多种LLM智能体上的实验表明，即使是强模型智能体在ComboShoppingBench上表现仍然困难，存在显著的改进空间，说明可靠且约束感知的组合购物任务对现有智能体构成重大挑战。

Conclusion: 组合购物是现有LLM智能体尚未很好解决的任务，ComboShoppingBench通过"见证引导的任务合成+语义与确定性双重验证"的评估方式，为研究可靠的约束感知组合购物提供了有价值的基准，凸显了该方向的挑战与前景。

Abstract: Real-world shopping often requires constructing a basket of complementary items rather than retrieving a single product. Such combo-shopping tasks arise in device setup, meal preparation, event planning, and group takeout ordering, requiring joint reasoning about item compatibility, availability, store-level requirements, delivery fees, coupons, and budgets. Evaluation is challenging because multiple baskets may satisfy the same request, making exact-match metrics unsuitable, whereas semantic evaluation alone cannot detect infeasible orders, invalid coupon combinations, or incorrect payments. We introduce ComboShoppingBench, an agentic shopping benchmark for open-ended yet verifiable basket construction in a simulated commerce and takeout environment. During task synthesis, an exploration agent constructs a feasible and semantically coherent basket of purchasable products; this witness guides the generation of coupons, budget constraints, user queries, and aligned evaluation rubrics. During evaluation, LLM judges assess semantic satisfaction, response quality, and claim faithfulness, while deterministic validation checks product-ID validity, budget compliance, and coupon optimality. Experiments with diverse LLM agents demonstrate that even strong agents struggle on ComboShoppingBench, highlighting substantial room for improvement in reliable, constraint-aware combo shopping.

</details>


### [75] [GeoPhysAdapter: Scale-Matched Geophysical Adaptation for Cross-Domain Landslide Mapping with Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09325)
*Zhihang Liu,Mei-Po Kwan,Jinlin Wu,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoPhysAdapter，将冻结核视觉基础模型与地形、物质、降雨触发等地球物理约束相结合，在像素和候选滑坡体两个决策单元上进行有界自适应，以解决跨域滑坡映射中的高置信度误报问题。在55个全球滑坡事件、7,890个测试样本上，候选体级自适应将误差降低23.99%（约为像素级效果的3.1倍），IoU提升14.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新发生的滑坡缺少即时标注，跨域迁移能力决定了滑坡制图在应急响应和区域风险评估中的价值。现有的视觉基础模型虽然强化了表征迁移，但在未见区域、事件和数据源上仍会产生高置信度误报。地形、物质、降雨触发等物理约束可以抑制此类错误，但其支撑尺度（局部、区域、事件级）与10米网格的分割决策单元错位，加剧了不确定地理环境问题（UGCoP）。

Method: GeoPhysAdapter锚定在冻结核视觉基础模型上，将地形、物质和触发因素分别作为密集空间引导（dense spatial guidance）、区域调制（regional modulation）和事件时序强加（event-timing forcing），并在像素和候选滑坡体两个决策单元上应用有界自适应，在支撑不足时精确回退到纯视觉预测。

Result: 在事件隔离的PILD数据集（四个公开源、55个全球滑坡事件、7,890个测试样本）上，70.3%的跨域误报质量集中在近纯虚假体，中位等效直径207米。像素级自适应移除507,817个错误像素并将误差降低7.76%，而候选体级的误差降低达到23.99%，约为像素级效果的3.1倍，IoU提升0.031（相对14.2%），且每个被损害的像素可纠正9.92个像素。

Conclusion: 提升分割决策单元到候选滑坡体层级，能够更有效地匹配物理先验的支撑尺度，显著优于像素级自适应，大幅削减跨域高置信度误报，验证了地球物理约束与视觉基础模型结合在跨域滑坡制图中的有效性。

Abstract: Newly triggered landslides rarely carry immediate annotations, so cross-domain transferability determines the value of landslide mapping for emergency response and regional risk assessment. Vision foundation models have strengthened representational transfer, yet on unseen regions, events, and data sources they still generate high-confidence false alarms. Terrain, material, and rainfall triggering can constrain such errors, but their supports are local, regional, and event-scale, so that resampling onto a 10~m grid misaligns them with the segmentation decision unit and compounds the uncertain geographic context problem (UGCoP). We propose GeoPhysAdapter, which anchors on a frozen vision foundation model, restricts terrain, material, and triggering to dense spatial guidance, regional modulation, and event-timing forcing, and applies bounded adaptation at two decision units, the pixel and the candidate landslide body, reverting exactly to the visual prediction where support is insufficient. On an event-isolated PILD dataset of four public sources, 55 global landslide events, and 7,890 test samples, 70.3% of cross-domain false-positive mass lies in near-pure spurious bodies of median equivalent diameter 207m, matching coarse-prior support rather than the pixel. Pixel-level adaptation removes a net 507,817 erroneous pixels and reduces error by 7.76%, whereas raising the decision unit to the candidate body, under identical samples, anchor, and baseline, increases error reduction to 23.99%, approximately 3.1 times the pixel-level effect, improves IoU by 0.031 (14.2% relative), and corrects 9.92 pixels per pixel harmed. The data and code are publicly available at: https://github.com/Liu-Zhihang/geophysadapter.

</details>


### [76] [Control-Oriented Scenario Tree Construction through Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09335)
*Fabio Pavirani,Bert Claessens,Pierre Pinson,Chris Develder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种面向控制的场景树构建方法，通过强化学习直接以闭环控制利润作为目标来训练场景树的叶子分配策略，取代传统的以分布匹配为目标（如Wasserstein缩减）的场景树构建方式，在电池套利问题上取得优于传统方法的利润和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多阶段随机模型预测控制（MPC）中场景树的构建侧重于匹配潜在概率分布（如基于Wasserstein的场景缩减），但分布精度更高并不一定带来更好的控制性能。场景树的价值取决于其支撑的决策，因此需要一种直接以控制性能为目标的场景树构建方法。

Method: 固定树拓扑结构，将场景树构建形式化为采样场景到叶子的序列分配问题。该分配由基于注意力机制的策略在场景集上参数化，使用强化学习训练，优化目标为闭环控制利润。训练稳定性通过非对称评论家（asymmetric critic）利用未来实现轨迹来增强。

Result: 在风险规避的电池套利问题上，该学习方法在多种预测集规模下均取得最高利润，优于经典的前向缩减、后向缩减方法以及确定性等效（单轨迹预测）控制。学习策略在困难实例上表现出更强的鲁棒性和更优的尾部风险特征。构建的树呈现紧凑、选择性分支结构，能捕捉高影响事件，同时保持大多数轨迹近乎确定性。

Conclusion: 场景树的价值关键取决于其支撑的决策，而非仅取决于分布匹配的精度。该工作提供了一种仅基于闭环控制优化信号即可训练场景树构建器的有效框架，揭示了控制导向场景树构建的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Multistage stochastic model predictive control (MPC) handles uncertainty by optimizing over a scenario tree, a finite branching approximation of future outcomes constructed from sampled forecasts. To build such a tree, conventional methods focus on matching the underlying probability distribution---e.g., via Wasserstein-based scenario reduction---but improved distributional accuracy does not necessarily yield better control performance. We propose a control-oriented approach that learns scenario tree construction directly from its impact on downstream decisions. Fixing the tree topology, we formulate tree construction as a sequential assignment of sampled scenarios to leaves. This assignment is parameterized by an attention-based policy over the scenario set and trained using reinforcement learning, with closed-loop control profit as the objective. Training is stabilized by an asymmetric critic that leverages realized future trajectories. We evaluate the method on a risk-averse battery arbitrage problem. Across a range of forecast set sizes, the learned construction consistently achieves the highest profit, outperforming classical forward and backward reduction methods and certainty-equivalent (single-trajectory forecast) control. The learned policy also exhibits greater robustness on challenging instances, consistently demonstrating better tail-risk characteristics. Analysis of the resulting trees indicates that our method constructs compact, selectively branching structures that capture high-impact events while keeping most trajectories nearly deterministic. These findings highlight that the value of a scenario tree depends critically on the decisions it supports, and provide an effective framework to train scenario tree constructors merely based on the closed-loop control optimization signal.

</details>


### [77] [LLM-Guided Heuristic Design from Simulation Traces: A Case Study in Dynamic Production and AGV Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09343)
*Jinbo Li,Chuanhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的启发式策略设计框架，利用仿真事件级轨迹进行瓶颈诊断，在离散事件仿真调度问题中，通过多轮LLM驱动的代码级策略修订，最终策略在100个匹配种子上全面超越滚动MILP、规则基和元启发式基线，且对随机故障具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于仿真的优化（SBO）方法将仿真器视为黑盒，仅通过聚合分数对候选策略排序，无法揭示策略失败的根本原因，也难以指导策略逻辑改进。这一诊断能力的缺失限制了SBO在复杂随机动态系统（如生产调度与AGV调度）中的应用效果。

Method: 框架包含两层智能体：（1）管理器智能体基于最低得分复现回放产生的可查询事件轨迹，生成瓶颈假设；（2）编辑智能体并行实施代码级修订。每个候策略通过多次重复仿真评估，执行检查和重复评估后采用"best-so-far"选择机制保留改进。LLM修订发生在评估批次之间，仿真运行由固定策略控制。

Result: 在动态生产与AGV调度的离散事件仿真中，以Gemini-3.1-Pro运行5次独立优化，最终平均分数77.51/100。最高分运行中，轨迹诊断驱动了主动充电、距离感知AGV分配和重新平衡的调度优先级改进，将最佳均值分从62.49提升至78.61。在100个匹配种子上，最终策略全面超越各基线，且在随机故障下无需重新优化仍保持优势。消融实验表明移除并行候选生成或轨迹数据库访问均会降低最终均值分。

Conclusion: 仿真事件轨迹能够有效引导复杂仿真调度中目标明确的代码级策略改进，证明了"仿真诊断+LLM修订"范式在基于仿真的优化中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Simulation-based optimization (SBO) evaluates executable policies under stochastic dynamics, but most methods treat the simulator as a black box: aggregate scores rank candidates without revealing why they fail or which policy logic should change. We present an LLM-guided heuristic design framework that uses repeated simulation for selection and event-level traces for diagnosis. Each incumbent is assessed through multiple replications, while replaying its lowest-scoring one produces a queryable trace. A manager agent formulates bottleneck hypotheses from this evidence, and editing agents implement parallel code-level revisions. After execution checks and repeated evaluation, best-so-far selection retains only improvements. LLM revision occurs between evaluation batches, while a fixed policy controls each simulation run.
  We evaluate the framework in a discrete-event simulation of dynamic production and automated guided vehicle (AGV) scheduling. Across five independent optimization runs with Gemini-3.1-Pro, final mean scores averaged 77.51 on the simulator's 0-100 scale. In the highest-scoring run, trace-based diagnoses motivated proactive charging, distance-aware AGV assignment, and rebalanced dispatch priorities, raising the best-so-far mean score from 62.49 to 78.61. On 100 matched seeds, the best final policy outscored representative rolling-MILP, rule-based, and metaheuristic policies on every seed and retained its advantage under random faults without re-optimization. After separate re-optimization for a longer horizon and variable order interarrival times, the resulting policies again outscored all baselines. Ablations with two LLM backbones showed that removing either parallel candidate generation or trace-database access reduced final mean scores. These results show that simulation traces can guide targeted code-level policy improvement in complex simulation-based scheduling.

</details>


### [78] [OpenLoopEvolve: A Verifiable Self-Evolution Framework for Loop Policies in Long-Horizon Complex Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09380)
*Siqi Wang,Xinlin Li,Zhenglin Li,Li Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenLoopEvolve（OLE），一个以Loop Policy为核心的智能体自进化框架。OLE将智能体的观察、规划、记忆、行动、验证、恢复、停止和预算控制表示为带有版本和谱系的便携式策略资产，并提供在线和离线两种进化模式。在模拟业务基准YC-Bench上，两种模式均提升了任务成功率、任务性能和风险指标，表明将Loop Policy作为可治理资产能够支持控制经验的积累、比较、发布和复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程复杂任务要求智能体在持续变化的环境中反复观察、规划、调用工具、验证结果并从失败中恢复。然而，这类控制经验通常局限于单一上下文或固定提示中，难以跨历史轨迹积累和复用。现有方法缺乏将控制经验系统化沉淀为可演进、可比对、可回滚的策略资产的机制。

Method: OLE框架的核心是将智能体的观察、规划、记忆、行动、验证、恢复、停止和预算控制表示为带版本和谱系的便携式策略资产（Loop Policy）。框架提供在线和离线两种进化模式：在线模式基于持续运行中的反馈触发候选策略生成；离线模式从存档轨迹和失败证据中搜索候选策略。两种模式共享统一进化机制，包括大语言模型自主提案、Champion-Challenger配对评估和稳健发布。在线发布的策略在后续任务边界激活，用后续反馈监控，满足退化条件时回滚到父版本。

Result: 在模拟业务基准YC-Bench上，相对于固定初始Loop Policy，在线和离线两种模式均提升了聚合任务性能、任务成功率和风险指标。实验证明将Loop Policy作为可治理资产能够支持控制经验的积累、比较、发布和复用，从而提升智能体在长时程复杂任务上的性能。

Conclusion: 将Loop Policy视为可治理资产（带版本管理、评估、发布和回滚机制）可以有效支持控制经验的系统化积累与跨轨迹复用，是提升长时程复杂任务智能体性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Long-horizon complex tasks require agents to repeatedly observe states, formulate plans, invoke tools, verify results, and recover from failures in continuously changing environments. However, such control experience often remains confined to a single context or a fixed prompt, and is difficult to accumulate and reuse across historical traces. This paper presents OpenLoopEvolve (OLE), a self-evolution framework centered on the Loop Policy. OLE represents an agent's observation, planning, memory, action, verification, recovery, stopping, and budget control as portable policy assets with versions and lineages, and provides online and offline evolution modes that can be selected according to practical needs: the online mode triggers candidate generation based on feedback from continuous operation, whereas the offline mode searches for candidate policies from archived traces and failure evidence. Both modes share an evolution mechanism consisting of autonomous proposals by a large language model, Champion--Challenger paired evaluation, and robust release. Policies released online are activated at a subsequent task boundary, monitored using subsequent feedback, and rolled back to their parent versions when degradation conditions are met. On the simulated business benchmark YC-Bench, both modes improve aggregate task performance, task success rate, and risk metrics relative to a fixed initial Loop Policy. The results indicate that treating the Loop Policy as a governable asset can support the accumulation, comparison, release, and reuse of control experience and improve agent performance on long-horizon complex tasks.

</details>


### [79] [Coupled Graph--Policy Distillation for Personalized Medication Safety in Older Adults with Multimorbidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09443)
*Zihan Wang,Anglin Liu,Rongyi Wang,Dantong Li,Yi Lu,Siqing Yuan,Hongxia Xu,Zhongtian Long,Jintai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ATLAS，一个基于耦合图-策略蒸馏框架的智能体系统，用于老年多病患者的用药安全审查；同时引入GeriMedBench交互式基准测试。ATLAS在多个基准上超越强基线，包括在European非交互式多病基准上超越最强专有LLM基线53.73个严格成功率点和14.63个OSRS点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体可支持临床就诊间隔期间的药物审查，但老年多病患者的用药安全决策依赖于用户可能省略的条件、药物和老年风险信息。现有系统缺乏结构化的指南证据整合和主动信息获取能力，需要一种能够进行患者自适应用药安全评估的方法。

Method: ATLAS将指南证据构建为用药安全图(medication-safety graph)，通过目标性问题更新患者状态并将相关关系蒸馏为患者特异性用药冲突图(PMCG)。采用风险优先的多智能体策略，基于PMCG进行禁忌症筛查、注意事项和监测需求评估、更安全替代方案识别及最终用药方案验证。同时引入GeriMedBench交互式基准测试，评估安全关键信息获取和基于证据的决策修正能力。

Result: 在European非交互式多病基准、Asian交互式多病基准和Asian非交互式跨指南基准上，ATLAS均取得最强的完整决策性能。在European非交互式多病基准上，严格成功率超出最强专有LLM基线53.73点，总体安全推理评分超出14.63点，自动化评估器下无不安全推荐。盲法临床医生评估中，ATLAS在全部五项标准上获得更高平均评分；ATLAS案例中标记1例潜在不安全推荐，Gemini案例中标记2例。

Conclusion: ATLAS通过将指南证据结构化为图并实施风险优先的多智能体策略，显著提升了LLM智能体在老年多病患者用药安全审查中的完整决策性能，在定量基准和临床专家评估方面均优于现有最强基线。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can support medication review between clinical visits, but safe choices for older adults with multimorbidity depend on conditions, medications, and geriatric risks that users may omit. We introduce ATLAS, a coupled graph--policy distillation framework for patient-adaptive medication safety. ATLAS structures guideline evidence as a medication-safety graph. Targeted questions update the patient state and distill relevant relations into a patient-specific medication conflict graph (PMCG). A risk-first multi-agent policy uses the PMCG to screen contraindications, assess cautions and monitoring needs, identify safer alternatives, and verify the final medication plan. We also introduce GeriMedBench, an interactive benchmark that tests safety-critical information acquisition and evidence-based decision revision. Across a European non-interactive multimorbidity benchmark, an Asian interactive multimorbidity benchmark, and an Asian non-interactive cross-guideline benchmark, ATLAS achieves the strongest complete-decision performance among the compared systems. On the European non-interactive multimorbidity benchmark, it exceeds the strongest proprietary LLM baseline by 53.73 points in Strict Success Rate and 14.63 points in overall safety reasoning score (OSRS), with no unsafe recommendations under the automated evaluator. A blinded clinician evaluation gives ATLAS higher mean ratings across all five criteria and flags potentially unsafe recommendations in one ATLAS case and two Gemini cases.

</details>


### [80] [Capability Is Not Propensity: Measuring Pressure-Robust Cooperative Behavior in Civic LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09485)
*Neel Tushar Shah,Manglam Kartik,Akshat Karkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffCoop-Civic，一个包含10个场景的协作式AI评测套件，用于区分语言模型在良性指令下的协作能力与在现实公民压力下的实际协作倾向。在7个模型上的实验表明，微妙的省略压力会导致操纵性增强（+1.17分）而异见保留下降（-1.67分），而轻量级Pareto-Trace提示干预能提升压力鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的协作能力具有双重用途属性：支撑公民协商的社会推理能力同样可能被用于策略性遗漏、虚假共识和操纵性框架。因此需要将模型在良性指令下"能够做什么"与其在现实公民压力下"倾向于做什么"区分开来评估，以更好地衡量和提升模型的合作能力安全性。

Method: 构建了DiffCoop-Civic评测套件，涵盖偏好理解、证据与说服、承诺设计、信息不对称和异见保留五个维度共10个场景。在四个模型家族的七个模型上进行测试，对比微妙省略压力与公开虚假共识压力下的行为差异，并提出Pareto-Trace提示干预方法以增强模型的压力鲁棒性。

Result: 微妙省略压力在所有模型中产生近乎一致的行为偏移：操纵性使能提升1.17分，异见保留下降1.67分（5分制）。公开虚假共识压力的影响因模型而异：部分对齐API模型表现为拒绝或重定向，而多个开放权重模型出现直接遵从。Pareto-Trace干预有效提升了压力鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 协作式AI评测需要区分能力（出capability）与倾向（出behavior/tendency），微妙的社会压力会系统性地改变模型行为，而轻量级提示干预可以在不使用强硬拒绝策略的情况下改善模型的压力鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cooperative capabilities in language models are dual-use. The same social reasoning that supports civic deliberation can also enable strategic omission, false consensus, and manipulative framing. We argue that Cooperative AI evaluations should separate what models can do under benign instructions from what they tend to do under realistic civic pressure. We introduce DiffCoop-Civic, a 10-scenario pilot evaluation suite spanning preference understanding, evidence and persuasion, commitment design, asymmetric information, and dissent preservation. Across seven models from four model families, subtle omission pressure produces a near-uniform shift: manipulative enablement rises by 1.17 points and dissent preservation falls by 1.67 points on a 5-point scale. Overt false-consensus pressure behaves differently: it triggers refusal or redirection in some aligned API models, but direct compliance in several open-weight models. A lightweight Pareto-Trace prompting intervention improves pressure robustness without simply relying on hard refusal. An anonymous reproducibility package is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/diffcoop-civil-771C.

</details>


### [81] [Bidirectional Context Self-Distillation for Reinforcement Learning of Skill-Based LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09555)
*Tianjun Pan,Yuan Li,Hongda Wang,Linbo Jin,Mengfei Song,Lei Gao,Qiming Shi,Shaokang Fu,Jiarong Zhao,Chengyu Wang,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BCSD（双向上下文自蒸馏）框架，结合自蒸馏与强化学习来训练LLM智能体更有效地利用外部自然语言技能，通过增强和缩减两个互补的技能上下文视角的组合信号来重新缩放RL优势，在ALFWorld和WebShop上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部自然语言技能为LLM智能体提供可复用和可编辑的指导，但其有效性不仅取决于技能质量，还取决于策略能否将提供的指导转化为适当行动。现有的基于技能的智能体通常使用任务级奖励的强化学习目标训练，监督有限，难以捕捉策略使用技能的细微差异。缺乏专门提升技能利用能力的方法。

Method: BCSD结合自蒸馏与强化学习。与依赖单一特权上下文的先前自蒸馏方法不同，BCSD从两个互补的技能上下文视角评估每条轨迹：(1) 增强视角，引入更高级的Meta-Skill指导；(2) 缩减视角，剪枝通用指导以突出任务特定技能。两者提供的互补token级信号被组合起来重新缩放RL优势函数。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上的实验表明，BCSD在多个模型规模下取得最强综合性能，使智能体能够更有效地利用外部技能。消融研究进一步验证了增强和缩减两个上下文视角的互补贡献。

Conclusion: BCSD通过双向上下文自蒸馏有效提升了LLM智能体的技能利用能力，补充了传统RL训练在技能使用监督上的不足，代码将发布以确保完全可复现。

Abstract: External natural-language skills provide large language model (LLM) agents with reusable and editable guidance for solving complex tasks. Yet their effectiveness depends not only on skill quality, but also on whether the policy can translate the provided guidance into appropriate actions. However, methods specifically designed to improve this skill-utilization ability remain largely underexplored. In practice, skill-based agents are commonly trained with reinforcement learning objectives centered on task-level rewards, which offer limited supervision and struggle to capture subtle differences in how effectively the policy uses the provided skills. We propose BCSD (Bidirectional Context Self-Distillation), a framework that combines self-distillation with reinforcement learning to train LLM agents to use external skills more effectively. Unlike prior self-distillation methods that rely on a single privileged context, BCSD evaluates each trajectory from two complementary skill-context views. The augmented view introduces higher-level Meta-Skill guidance, while the reduced view prunes general guidance to highlight task-specific skills. Their complementary token-level signals are combined to rescale the RL advantage. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that BCSD achieves the strongest overall performance across model scales, enabling agents to utilize external skills more effectively. Ablation studies further verify the complementary contributions of the augmented and reduced context views. Code will be released to ensure full reproducibility.

</details>


### [82] [The Politician, the Liar, and the Obedient Worker: Emerging Behavior of LLM Agents in Hierarchical Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09574)
*Fatemeh Seyedin,Adrian Weller,Jinhyuk Yun,Mahmoudreza Babaei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出层级博弈（HG）——一个扩展了管理权威、民主选举和私人沟通的公共物品博弈框架，用于测试六个前沿大语言模型在逐步引入机构机制时的治理行为。结果发现不同模型表现出截然不同的行为画像：Qwen会撒谎（13.3%违约率）、Grok在管理者可施罚时从不合作变为完全合作（16%→100%）、Claude和GPT-4o基线可靠。但诚实是脆弱的：薪资激励导致除GPT-4o外所有模型开始私下交易；匿名惩罚使诚实模型作弊；同模型族群中首任管理者无限期掌权，只有混合族群才会发生领导更替。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM从个人工具演变为多智能体组织的参与者，需要回答一个关键问题：LLM是否会在组织中复现人类制度中的治理失败（如搭便车、腐败、领导固化）？通过构建基准博弈框架并系统性地逐项添加机构组件，探讨AI治理的脆弱性与鲁棒性。

Method: 将经典公共物品博弈扩展为层级博弈（HG），在其中引入四类模块：管理权威（managerial authority）、民主选举（democratic elections）、私人沟通（private communication）。设计12组实验，按顺序逐项叠加社会制度（言论、同伴、政府、薪资、监督、选举），测试六个前沿模型的行为差异。

Result: 六个模型呈现差异化行为画像：Qwen违约率13.3%；Grok在无惩罚时拒绝合作（16%）、有管理者惩罚时完全合作（100%）；Claude和GPT-4o基线稳定合作。然而，引入薪资激励后除GPT-4o外所有模型均开始私下谈判谋取职位；匿名化惩罚导致原本诚实的模型作弊；同模型族群中首任当选管理者无限期掌权，仅混合模型族群出现领导更替。

Conclusion: LLM在组织中会复现人类治理的失败模式，且诚实在不同制度条件下高度脆弱。制度设计中薪资、匿名性、族群同质性等具体参数会显著改变模型行为，提示AI治理需要谨慎的机制设计来防止腐败和权力固化。

Abstract: LLMs are rapidly embedding themselves into daily life: drafting our emails, managing our schedules, and making decisions on our behalf. As they move from individual tools to participants in multi-agent organizations, an important question arises: do they reproduce the governance failures like free-riding, corruption, and entrenched leadership that plague human institutions? We introduce the Hierarchical Game (HG), a public goods game extended with managerial authority, democratic elections, and private communication. Testing six frontier models across twelve experiments that add institutions one at a time (speech, peers, government, wages, oversight, elections), we find distinct behavioral profiles: Qwen promises and lies (13.3\% broken promises); Grok refuses to cooperate on its own but becomes fully cooperative once a manager can punish it (16\%$\to$100\%); Claude and GPT-4o cooperate reliably at baseline. But honesty proves fragile. When the manager role comes with a salary, all models except GPT-4o start cutting private deals to win or keep the position. When punishment is made anonymous, honest models begin to cheat. When all agents share the same model family, the first elected manager stays in power indefinitely. Leadership change only happens in groups that mix different families.

</details>


### [83] [ElasticBack: Stealthy Conditional Backdoor in LLM-Agent Skills via Coupled Trigger-Rule Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09577)
*Hao Sui,Simeng Qin,Jie Liao,Xiaojun Jia,Bing Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ElasticBack是一种高隐蔽性、低成本的单技能后门攻击方法，通过在技能文档中植入规则R并在用户查询中构造良性触发词T，仅在二者同时出现时激活恶意载荷。该方法通过语义锚定规则注入生成R，再用带隐蔽性约束的遗传搜索进化T，实现无需权重修改、对良性输入完全休眠的后门效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能攻击要么每次请求都触发（缺乏条件性），要么依赖微调的权重或多个技能协同，尚未有人探索一种有条件触发且低成本的后门攻击。而Agent技能正形成新兴供应链，单个被投毒的技能即可持续危害所有安装该技能的Agent，因此需要填补这一攻击面空白。

Method: ElasticBack包含两步核心设计：(1) trigger-as-switch构造：通过语义锚定规则注入，在技能文档中植入一条规则R作为后门开关；(2) 冻结R后，使用带隐蔽性约束的遗传搜索算法进化用户查询中的触发词T，使后门的有效性与隐蔽性同时被优化，全程保持无权重修改且在良性输入上处于休眠状态。

Result: 在3种目标行为（每种50个技能）和4种Agent LLM上的广泛实验表明，ElasticBack实现了高攻击成功率、接近零的误报率，同时保持干净的准确率，能够跨模型迁移，并能够规避部署时的防御机制。

Conclusion: ElasticBack证明了技能供应链中存在严重的条件性单技能后门攻击漏洞，这一结果呼吁社区为技能供应链设计更强的防御机制。

Abstract: Agent skills, bundles of instructions and resources that an LLM agent loads on demand, form an emerging supply chain where a single poisoned skill can persistently compromise every agent that installs it. However, existing skill attacks either fire on every request or rely on fine-tuned weights or multiple skills, leaving a conditional and low-cost backdoor unexplored. In this work, we present ElasticBack, an effective conditional single-skill backdoor that plants a rule R in the skill document and a benign-looking trigger T in the user query, so the malicious payload fires only when both co-occur. ElasticBack binds the two sides through a trigger-as-switch construction, generating R via semantic-anchored rule injection. It then freezes R and evolves T against it with a stealth-constrained genetic search, so that effectiveness and stealth are optimized, keeping the backdoor weight-free and dormant on benign inputs. Extensive experiments across three target behaviors (50 skills each) and four agent LLMs show that ElasticBack attains a high attack success rate at a near-zero false-positive rate with preserved clean accuracy, transfers across models, and evades deployment-time defenses. These results motivate stronger defenses for the skill supply chain.

</details>


### [84] [Model Discovery Agent: LLM-assisted Bayesian experiment design for data-efficient discovery of mechanistic world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09696)
*Kevin Murphy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了模型发现智能体（MDA），将大语言模型作为候选结构提议器，与贝叶斯推断（SMC、SBI）和价值信息（VoI）实验设计相耦合，从少量干预实验中自动发现潜在的机制性世界模型，并在物理、化学、生物学三个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测反事实"假设"问题需要机制性因果模型而非曲线拟合，而学习此类模型需要实验数据，因为被动数据无法识别机制。实验昂贵，因此核心挑战是数据效率。

Method: MDA由三部分组成：(1) LLM作为候选模型结构的提议器，可动态扩展假设空间（M-open设置）；(2) SMC用于参数与结构后验推断，SBI处理不可处理的似然；(3) VoI用于设计最具有信息量的干预实验。发现与设计相互强化：设计步骤识别提议的结构，识别出的机制改善预测，从而从残余中推动进一步发现。

Result: 在三个基准（物理DPbench、化学CHEMbench、生物学HHbench新基准）上，MDA在数据效率模型学习和可靠的反事实预测方面达到了新的SOTA。

Conclusion: 发现与设计之间存在正向反馈循环：实验设计帮助识别新提议的机制，而识别出的机制通过排除已解释的变化，使后续发现成为可能，整体实现数据高效的机制世界模型自动发现。

Abstract: Predicting the answer to interventional ``what if'' questions --- the outcome of an action never taken --- requires a \emph{mechanistic}, causal model, not a curve fit; and learning such a model requires \emph{experiments}, because passive data leaves its mechanisms unidentified. Experiments are expensive, so the central problem is \emph{data efficiency}. We present the Model Discovery Agent (MDA), which couples a large language model (LLM), used as a \emph{proposer} of candidate structures, with standard Bayesian machinery --- sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) for parameter and structure posteriors, simulation-based inference (SBI) for intractable likelihoods, and value-of-information (VoI) for experiment design --- to discover latent mechanistic world models from few interventions. MDA operates in the M-open setting: when the truth lies outside the current hypothesis class, a predictive check flags the inadequacy and the proposer expands the hypothesis space with a new model whose parameters are then identified by designed experiments. We show that \emph{discovery and design reinforce}: the design step identifies the mechanism the discovery step proposes, and the identified mechanism improves predictions, enabling further discoveries from the remaining unexplained residuals. On three different benchmarks --- covering physics (\DPbench, \citep{wiemann2026discoverphysics}), chemistry (\CHEMbench, \citep{kabra2026autoscilab}) and biology (\HHbench, a new partially observed single-neuron electrophysiology benchmark we create) --- we show that MDA sets a new SOTA in terms of data-efficient model learning and reliable interventional forecasting ability.

</details>


### [85] [Mismatch Matters: On-Policy Distillation Beyond Token Agreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09836)
*Zichao Yu,Chengzhi Yu,Shengze Xu,Yujin Han,Bingqing Jiang,Xu Wang,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示在线蒸馏（OPD）在LLM后训练中的一个失败模式——"退化一致性"（学生通过重复循环实现与教师的高token一致但全局响应存在缺陷），并提出TIDE（Token级独立缺陷-富余修正）方法，通过对数比率的有界Hellinger整形和教师top-K注入，分别抑制学生富余token的不稳定更新和补充学生缺失的教师偏好token，在数学推理基准上显著超越标准OPD及现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线蒸馏方法盲目追求学生与教师之间的token级一致性，但在强教师-学生不匹配场景下，学生可能通过重复循环（degenerate agreement）达到近似的token一致性，而整体回答质量仍然低下。作者识别出这一失败模式，并指出传统的"一致性"优化目标掩盖了真正的学习信号——师生之间的不匹配token。

Method: 作者将教师-学生不匹配token分为两类：1）学生富余token（student-excess）：学生生成但教师赋予接近零概率的token，其log-ratio修正项无界增长导致训练不稳定；2）学生缺陷token（student-deficit）：教师偏好但学生鲜少采样的token，其缺失阻碍教师推理模式的有效迁移。TIDE方法针对这两种方向进行修正：对富余token采用有界Hellinger整形以抑制最严重的采样富余；对缺陷token采用解析式教师top-K注入，在不依赖采样的前提下恢复缺失的概率质量。

Result: 在多个Qwen3教师-学生配对下的数学推理基准上，TIDE一致优于标准OPD以及最近的token选择与奖励整形基线。尤其在强师生不匹配场景下，Avg@8从6.9%提升至20.3%，平均响应长度减少3.6倍，并大幅降低格式错误率。代码已开源。

Conclusion: TIDE表明，在LLM在线蒸馏中，不应盲目追求师生token一致性，而应显式建模并分别处理两类不匹配（缺陷与富余）。这种不对称的处理方式在师生差距大时尤为关键，为LLM后训练中的蒸馏设计提供了新的视角。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as a core component of modern LLM post-training pipelines, yet we reveal a failure mode: degenerate agreement, where students exploit repetitive loops to achieve near-perfect token agreement with the teacher despite globally flawed responses. We therefore shift our focus from agreement to teacher-student mismatch, and find that mismatch tokens can be mainly categorized into two types: student-excess tokens and student-deficit tokens. Student-excess tokens are generated by the student but assigned near-zero probability by the teacher; their log-ratio corrections grow unbounded and destabilize the update. Student-deficit tokens, in contrast, are preferred by the teacher but rarely sampled by the student; their absence blocks the transfer of the teacher's reasoning patterns. To tackle these mismatch directions, we propose TIDE (Token-level Independent Deficit-Excess correction), which applies bounded Hellinger shaping to suppress the most severe sampled excesses and an analytic teacher top-$K$ injection to restore deficient probability mass without requiring deficit tokens to be sampled. Across mathematical reasoning benchmarks with multiple Qwen3 teacher-student pairs, TIDE consistently outperforms standard OPD and recent token-selection and reward-shaping baselines. Moreover, the gains of TIDE are more pronounced under strong teacher-student mismatch, where it improves Avg@8 from 6.9% to 20.3%, reduces average response length by a factor of 3.6, and substantially reduces formatting failures. Code is available at https://github.com/yzc-666/TIDE

</details>


### [86] [Towards Expert-level Medical AI for Real-time Video Consultations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09861)
*Mahvish Nagda,Jihyeon Lee,Matthew Thompson,Chunjong Park,Tim Strother,Valentin Liévin,Roma Ruparel,Akshay Goel,Teya Bergamaschi,Suhana Bedi,Meet Shah,Pavel Dubov,Liviu Panait,Toshiyuki Fukuzawa,Sam Schmidgall,Craig Schiff,Joseph Xu,Aliya Rysbek,Yana Lunts,Jan Freyberg,Rebecca Hemengway,Sunny Virmani,David Racz,Carey Radebaugh,Joëlle Barral,Kavi Goel,Dale R. Webster,Katherine Chou,Avinatan Hassidim,Yossi Matias,James Manyika,Gregory Wayne,Tao Tu,Yun Liu,Ethan Goh,Christina Chen,Ryutaro Tanno,Po-Hsuan Cameron Chen,Mike Schaekermann,Anil Palepu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文首次展示了在实时临床视频咨询中达到专家级水平的AI系统AMIE（Video），它是基于Gemini的多智能体系统，在随机OSCE研究中与30名初级保健医生和100个临床场景对比，在病史采集、诊断、管理和体格检查方面被评估为与医生相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本型AI虽然展现潜力，但丢弃了视听交互中关键的感知维度，且限制了无法以文字表达症状的患者。早期扩展到视听交互的医疗AI只证明了可行性，未达到临床医生水平，需要进一步提升。

Method: AMIE（Video）是基于Gemini的多智能体系统，集成了低延迟对话、临床推理和实时视听感知。研究建立了临床视听线索分类体系和自动化评估方法，并进行了一项随机OSCE研究，涉及30名初级保健医生、15名患者演员和100个临床场景，比较AMIE（Video）、其纯文本版本AMIE（Text）和通过视频咨询的PCPs。

Result: 临床评估者认为AMIE（Video）在病史采集、诊断、管理以及体格观察和检查方面与PCPs相当或更好。患者演员在病情评估和解释方面更偏好AMIE，但在建立关系和伙伴关系方面更偏好PCPs。模态消融实验中，患者演员更偏好AMIE（Video）的界面而非文本聊天。局限包括精细解剖精度、细微情感细微差别和高频运动方面仍存在不足。

Conclusion: 这些结果标志着AI系统在临床实践的感官复杂性中辅助护理的重要里程碑，但考虑真实世界转化前仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Audio-visual interaction is the standard for patient-physician consultations, enabling natural communication and effective assessment of illness through non-verbal cues. While text-based AI has shown promise, it discards essential perceptual dimensions and limits patients who cannot articulate symptoms in writing. Early efforts to extend medical AI to audio-visual interaction have demonstrated feasibility but not reached clinician-level performance. Here, we provide the first demonstration of expert-level AI in real-time clinical video consultations using AMIE (Articulate Medical Intelligence Explorer) in a video configuration. AMIE (Video) is a Gemini-based multi-agent system integrating low-latency dialogue, clinical reasoning, and real-time audio-visual perception. To guide development, we established a taxonomy and automated evaluations for clinical audio-visual cues in telehealth settings. In a randomized Objective Structured Clinical Examination (OSCE) study with 30 primary care physicians (PCPs), 15 patient actors and 100 clinical scenarios, we compared AMIE (Video), its text-only counterpart AMIE (Text), and PCPs consulting via video. Clinical evaluators rated AMIE (Video) on par or better than PCPs in history-taking, diagnosis, management, and physical observation and examination. Patient actors preferred AMIE's approach to assessing and explaining conditions, while PCPs were preferred for rapport and partnership building. In modality ablation, patient actors preferred AMIE (Video)'s interface over text chat for communicative effectiveness, convenience, and feeling understood. Limitations remain in fine anatomical precision, subtle affective nuances, and high-frequency movements. While further research is needed before real-world translation, these results mark an important milestone toward AI systems capable of augmenting care across the sensory complexity of clinical practice.

</details>


### [87] [SHE: Trajectory-driven Safety Harness Evolution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09885)
*Wanying Qu,Qinghua Mao,Yu Li,Jiyao Liu,Xin Zhang,Dadi Guo,Yanxu Zhu,Qingyu Liu,Leitao Yuan,Xi Lin,Shanfeng Zhu,Yanwei Fu,Jing Shao,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SHE（Safety Harness Evolution）框架，通过将LLM代理的安全护具（harness）分解为系统提示、规则库、安全记忆和工具策略四个具有明确安全职责的构件，并引入基于归因的进化循环，从轨迹失败中学习安全边界，实现在Agent-SafetyBench上ASR降低3.1倍的显著安全提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理安全机制将护具视为固定部署产物，难以随新兴风险演化；同时护具各组件的功能耦合模糊了安全责任归属，使得局部演化困难。因此需要一种能动态演化安全边界、并明确各组件安全职责的框架。

Method: SHE框架包含两大核心设计：(1) 将代理护具解耦为四个具有明确安全责任的构件——System Prompt、Rule Bank、Safety Memory和Tool Policy，定义清晰的功能边界以支持局部演化；(2) 引入归因引导的进化循环，将轨迹失败转化为结构化诊断，学习构件特有的边界细化方案，并通过安全-效用验证机制选择进化后的护具。

Result: 在Agent-SafetyBench上，SHE相较于静态SafeHarness实现3.1x的攻击成功率（ASR）降低，同时提升良性任务效用。进化后的护具在held-out的AgentHarm基准上泛化到未见风险，并能跨代理模型迁移而无需额外进化。

Conclusion: SHE证明了通过护具的构件分解与归因引导的进化循环，可以有效实现LLM代理安全边界的持续动态演化，兼具安全性提升、通用性与跨模型迁移能力，为代理安全治理提供了新的范式。

Abstract: The safety of large language model (LLM) agents depends not only on model weights but also on the agent harness that manages context, memory, tools, permissions, and runtime control. Existing safety mechanisms often treat the harness as a fixed deployment artifact, limiting their ability to evolve with emerging risks. Moreover, coupled functions across harness components obscure safety responsibility attribution, making localized evolution difficult. We propose Safety Harness Evolution (SHE), a framework that learns evolving safe boundaries from rollout trajectories. SHE decomposes the harness into four artifacts with explicit safety responsibilities, including the System Prompt, Rule Bank, Safety Memory, and Tool Policy, defining clear functional boundaries for localized evolution. Based on this decomposition, SHE introduces an attribution-guided evolution loop that converts trajectory failures into structured diagnoses, learns artifact-specific boundary refinements, and selects evolved harnesses through safety-utility validation. Experiments on Agent-SafetyBench demonstrate that SHE effectively enhances safety through harness evolution, achieving a 3.1x ASR reduction compared with static SafeHarness, while also improving benign utility. The evolved harness further generalizes to unseen risks on the held-out AgentHarm benchmark and transfers across agent models without additional evolution.

</details>

### [88] [SkillConsist: Detecting Inconsistencies in Agent Skills via Bidirectional Graph Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07639)
*Chaofan Meng,Yuhang Zheng,Yingnan Zhou,Sihan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillConsist是一个用于检测LLM Agent技能中声明与实现行为不一致的系统，通过LLM将文本和代码分离为行为记录并构建双端行为图，利用双向图对齐和差分分析识别冲突。在633个技能基准上，包级检测F1达87.93%，比最佳基线提升20.43个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent技能的不一致可能暴露未披露的危险行为或导致错误的技能选择。现有检测方法要么基于预定义类别评估行为或安全属性图，要么（如PL-HCL）使用LLM跨元数据、指令和资源学习一致性，但声明和实现行为可能混合在文本和代码中，且简洁的声明可能对应多个相连的实现步骤，现有方法难以处理这些问题。

Method: SkillConsist包含三个核心模块：(1) LLM将声明和实现内容分离为行为记录，静态分析补充实现侧记录；(2) 分别构建声明侧和实现侧的行为图；(3) 从任一侧的行为记录出发，执行双向图对齐，在图间搜索候选子图并沿行为关系扩展直至完整表达源侧行为，最后通过图差分识别对齐子图间的冲突并输出检测结果。

Result: 在包含633个技能（319个不一致、314个一致）的基准上，SkillConsist在包级检测中达到86.85%精确率、89.03%召回率和87.93% F1，F1比最佳基线提升20.43个百分点；在定位任务上达到67.60%精确率、58.14%召回率和62.52% F1。

Conclusion: SkillConsist通过行为图双向对齐和差分分析，有效解决了声明与实现内容在文本和代码间混合分布、以及声明与多步实现之间映射关系复杂的问题，显著超越了现有基线方法。

Abstract: Agent Skills provide reusable capabilities to LLM agents. Agent Skill inconsistencies can expose undisclosed dangerous behavior or cause wrong Skill selection. Recent Agent Skill research has increasingly examined Agent Skill consistency detection. Existing methods evaluate behaviors or security-property graphs against predefined categories or declared scopes. More recently, PL-HCL uses an LLM-based model to learn consistency across metadata, instructions, and resources. However, declaration and implementation behavior can be mixed across text and code, and a concise declaration can correspond to multiple connected implementation steps. We present SkillConsist to address both challenges. An LLM separates declaration and implementation content into behavior records on the implementation and declaration sides, while static analysis supplements implementation records. These records form declaration and implementation behavior graphs, respectively. Starting from a behavior record on either side, bidirectional graph alignment searches the other graph for a candidate subgraph and expands it along behavior relations until it completely expresses the source-side behavior. Graph differencing identifies conflicts between aligned subgraphs and outputs the detection results. We construct a 633-Skill benchmark from ClawHub's 500 most-downloaded public Skills and 133 Skill-Inject packages. The benchmark contains 319 inconsistent and 314 consistent Skills and 442 localized inconsistency annotations. On this benchmark, SkillConsist achieves 86.85% precision, 89.03% recall, and 87.93% F1 for package-level detection, improving F1 over the best baseline by 20.43 percentage points. For localization, it achieves 67.60% precision, 58.14% recall, and 62.52% F1.

</details>


### [89] [From Benchmark Performance to Tool Deployment: Human-in-the-Loop Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07770)
*Mike Szklarzewski,CJ George,Gavin Smithson,Christopher Stokes,Dakota Fulp,William M. Jones,Benjamin Wynn,Alexander Ur,Agit Yesiloz,Clint Kallenbach,Mark Swartz,Nathan DeBardeleben,Sharmistha Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了19种无监督异常检测模型在BowTie制造数据集上的表现，发现其性能不如在MVTec AD等标准基准上稳定，对预处理高度敏感且缺乏普遍稳健的模型；基于此，作者开发并部署了一个结合图像标注、AI辅助缺陷检测和集成验证引擎的人机协同框架，用于替代传统的人工目视检查和文档记录流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化异常检测方法在学术基准上表现优异，但真实工业条件下的行为尚不明确。作者希望通过在具有挑战性的制造数据集上系统评估，揭示基准性能与实际部署之间的差距，并提出实用解决方案。

Method: 在BowTie数据集（含反射表面、细微缺陷及配置文件差异）上评估19种无监督异常检测模型，并进行一致性审计以考察名义数据质量的影响；基于评估结果，设计并部署了一个统一的人机协同（human-in-the-loop）框架，包含图像标注、AI辅助缺陷检测、集成验证引擎，支持热图引导缺陷审查、SAM精化的候选区域供检查员接受/拒绝/调整边界、掩码评估（存在标注时）以及审查历史记录。

Result: 模型性能在真实工业条件下不稳定，对预处理敏感，且在不同条件下表现不一致，没有单一方法表现出普遍稳健性；一致性审计表明名义数据质量影响部署效果。开发并初步部署的人机协同框架成功替代了先前的人工目视检查和文档记录工作流。

Conclusion: 研究凸显了基准性能与部署现实之间的显著差距，所提出的人机协同框架为弥合这一差距提供了实用方案，涵盖缺陷检测、人工审查和一致性管理等完整流程。

Abstract: Automated anomaly detection methods often report strong performance on curated academic benchmarks, but their behavior under real-world industrial conditions is less clear. In this work, we evaluate 19 unsupervised anomaly detection models on the BowTie dataset, a challenging manufacturing dataset with reflective surfaces, subtle defects, and profile-specific variation. In contrast to benchmark results, we observe that model performance is less stable than typically reported on standard benchmarks such as MVTec AD, highly sensitive to preprocessing, and inconsistent across conditions, with no single approach emerging as uniformly robust; a consensus audit further indicates that nominal-data quality affects deployment.
  Motivated by these findings, we developed and initially deployed a unified human-in-the-loop framework for manufactured-part inspection that combines image annotation, AI-assisted defect detection, and an integrated validation engine, replacing a prior manual visual inspection and documentation workflow. The system supports heatmap-guided defect review, SAM-refined candidate regions for inspector acceptance, rejection, or boundary adjustment, mask evaluation where annotations exist, and review history for inspector consistency and onboarding. Together, the results highlight the gap between benchmark performance and deployment reality, and provide a practical framework for addressing it.

</details>


### [90] [Adaptive KappaSharp: Condition-Number Shaping for Preferential Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07859)
*Ketong Shao,Jialu Wang,Xuekai Pei,Ali Mesbah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KappaSharp方法，通过对角Hessian校正解决偏好贝叶斯优化（PBO）中似然Hessian秩亏缺的结构性问题，并给出自适应变体，在11个基准测试中（包括16维等离子体医学控制器调优）相较标准PBO基线最高提升+10.9%（p=0.003）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PBO通过成对用户比较优化目标函数，标准做法（PairwiseGP+EUBO）产生的查询对与先前查询无共享候选，导致比较图中形成孤立分量，使似然Hessian秩亏缺——这是结构性问题，无法通过更换代理模型解决。现有方法要么浪费查询预算强制连通，要么用均匀正则化扰动已被充分约束的方向。

Method: 提出KappaSharp：对Hessian施加对角校正以降低条件数，校正量在先验不确定性更高的方向更大。校正仅在模型拟合步骤应用，不影响查询选择。另提出自适应变体（Adaptive KappaSharp），仅在代理对近期比较自信时激活校正，避免在问题良态时做不必要的修正。

Result: 在11个基准测试（5-20维）上，包括16维等离子体医学控制器调优问题，Adaptive KappaSharp优于标准PBO基线，性能提升最高达+10.9%（p=0.003）。

Conclusion: KappaSharp通过针对性而非均匀的Hessian校正有效解决了PBO中结构性的秩亏缺问题，自适应变体进一步避免过度校正，显著提升了PBO在实际高维问题上的性能。

Abstract: Preferential Bayesian optimization (PBO) optimizes objectives accessible only through pairwise user comparisons. The standard approach fits a Gaussian process surrogate for observed pairwise comparisons (PairwiseGP) using the Laplace approximation and selects queries with the Expected Utility of Best Option (EUBO) acquisition function. EUBO queries new candidates at each step, producing pairs that share no candidates with previous queries. Each such pair forms an isolated component in the comparison graph, removing one degree of freedom from the likelihood Hessian and making it rank-deficient. This deficiency is structural and cannot be resolved by changing the surrogate modeling approach. Existing approaches to remedy this issue either waste query budget by forcing comparisons to stay connected, or apply uniform regularization that also perturbs directions already well-constrained by the observed comparisons. We propose KappaSharp that enables a diagonal correction to the Hessian to reduce its condition number, with larger corrections where the prior uncertainty is higher. The correction is only applied in the model fitting step, not query selection. An adaptive variant of KappaSharp is also presented that activates the correction only when the surrogate is confident about recent comparisons, avoiding unnecessary corrections when the problem is well-conditioned. On 11 benchmarks (5--20 dimensions), including a 16-dimensional controller tuning problem in plasma medicine, Adaptive KappaSharp outperforms the standard PBO baseline, with up to +10.9% ($p{=}0.003$).

</details>


### [91] [LLM-Based Embeddings for Program Analysis and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07894)
*Calvin Higgins,Marco Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将LLMCompiler（在中间表示IR代码上大规模预训练的LLM）产生的程序嵌入应用于程序分析与优化任务。通过将程序分块、独立嵌入再利用预训练LLM聚合的方法，在算法分类任务上结合源代码与IR代码嵌入实现了1.54%的错误率，较当前最先进方法提升12%，并在异构设备映射任务上取得有竞争力的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来机器学习尤其是LLM在程序分析优化方面展现出巨大潜力，但此前尚未有研究将LLMCompiler（一种在IR代码上大规模预训练的LLM）产生的程序嵌入系统性地应用于代表性程序分析与优化任务。作者希望探索这种IR代码嵌入能否在这些任务上带来性能提升。

Method: 采用简单的程序嵌入生成方法：将程序分割成多个块（chunks），利用预训练LLM分别对每个块进行嵌入，然后将所有块的嵌入聚合为单个程序嵌入。实验同时使用源代码和IR代码生成嵌入，并进行组合以评估效果。

Result: 结合源代码和IR代码嵌入在算法分类任务上达到1.54%的错误率，相比当前最先进方法提升12%；在异构设备映射任务上取得有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: 研究表明，源代码与IR代码嵌入的结合能显著提升程序分析任务的性能；同时，训练一个性能感知的LLM用于IR代码嵌入，可能在代码优化任务中取得最先进水平的结果。

Abstract: Recent advances have highlighted the potential of machine learning, particularly Large Language Models (LLMs), for analyzing and optimizing programs. We present the first application of program embeddings from LLMCompiler---an LLM massively pretrained on intermediate representation (IR) code---to representative program analysis and optimization tasks. We generate program embeddings directly from source and IR code using a simple approach: split programs into chunks, independently embed each chunk with pretrained LLMs, and then aggregate the chunk embeddings into a single program embedding. Our experiments show that combining source and IR code embeddings achieves an error rate of 1.54\% in algorithm classification, a 12\% improvement over the current state-of-the-art, and a competitive accuracy on heterogeneous device mapping. These findings suggest that training a performance-aware LLM for embedding IR code might yield state-of-the-art results in code optimization tasks.

</details>


### [92] [Persistent Semantic Entities in Tool-Augmented LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07952)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了持久语义实体（PSEs）——由名称绑定、事件触发和跨边界传播定义的工具增强LLM代理中的隐式状态，并在24个模型（11个家族，1.5B–1T参数）上评估。所有模型均易受PSE污染；偏好污染在所有模型上持续不衰减，指令污染持续存在，人格注入部分衰减，事实注入因模型而异。上下文隔离的自验证可实现20–79%（中位数36.5%）的污染降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强的LLM代理可承载跨会话持续、通过事件激活并在代理边界传播的隐式状态，这些状态对标准调试方法基本不可见，构成被忽视的安全与可靠性攻击面。

Method: 定义PSEs三类核心属性（名称绑定、事件触发、跨边界传播）；在24个模型中构建20模型易感性面板；系统测量不同污染类型（偏好、指令、人格、事实）的持久性；提出上下文隔离的自验证检测方法，并评估关键词检测的假阳性率；构建四阶段代理流水线测试污染的复合放大效应。

Result: 1）所有20个测试模型均易受影响（20–100%），名称绑定是必要且主导机制；2）偏好污染在所有模型上100%持续（t=10），指令污染在采纳处持久，人格注入从90%衰减至10%，事实注入模型依赖（Llama-3.1-8B和GPT-4o-mini可自修正，Qwen2.5-coder变体维持最高水平）；3）上下文隔离的自验证实现20–79%降低（中位数36.5%），关键词检测有系统性假阳性；4）污染沿四阶段流水线复合放大1.9×（40%→75%）。

Conclusion: 偏好和指令污染具有持久性、缺乏自修正且难以被标准监控捕获，对已部署的代理系统构成特别严峻的攻击面；需要开发超越现有监控机制的检测与防御策略。

Abstract: Tool-augmented LLM agents can harbor implicit state that persists across sessions, activates through events, and propagates across agent boundaries---largely invisible to standard debugging. We formalize this as Persistent Semantic Entities (PSEs): constructs defined by name binding, event triggering, and cross-boundary propagation, and evaluate them across 24 models from 11 families (1.5B--1T parameters). First, every tested model is susceptible (20--100% on the 20-model susceptibility panel), with name binding as the necessary and dominant mechanism: without it, contamination is 0%.
