Second, persistence depends on contamination type rather than scale or deployment: preference contamination persists undecayed on every model probed (100% at t=10) and instruction contamination persists wherever adopted, persona-style injection decays partially (90%$\to$10%), while factual injection is model-dependent---self-corrected on Llama-3.1-8B and GPT-4o-mini but held at ceiling on both Qwen2.5-coder variants, so we do not claim it self-corrects in general. The preference and instruction results hold across providers in our controlled setting. Third, context-isolated self-verification achieves 20--79% reduction (median 36.5%) without oracle references while keyword-based detection produces systematic false positives, and contamination compounds 1.9$\times$ along a four-stage agent pipeline (40%$\to$75%). Preference and instruction contamination---persistent, lacking self-correction, and poorly captured by standard monitoring---represent a particularly concerning attack surface for deployed agent systems.

</details>


### [93] [ZeroLock: Concurrent Memory-Efficient LLM Training via Modular Update Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07974)
*Wentao Dai,Xuanran Li,Yuxiang Zhang,Ming Tang,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ZeroLock，一种免反向传播（BP-free）的边缘端大语言模型微调算法。通过局部目标构造将模型更新解耦为独立的块级更新，打破BP的更新锁定，从而在算法层面提升吞吐量并降低激活存储带来的内存开销。ZeroLock在收敛率上达到与BP仅相差多对数因子的Õ(1/√T)，并在真实原型上实现内存降低26.5%、吞吐量提升4.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上进行LLM微调可适配场景数据并保护隐私，但现有基于流水线并行的方法依赖反向传播（BP）训练。BP存在更新锁定（update locking）这一根本性局限，导致吞吐量和内存瓶颈，难以满足边缘设备有限的资源约束。

Method: 1) 提出ZeroLock算法：通过局部目标构造将模型更新解耦为独立块更新，打破BP的更新锁定；2) 提供了首个针对通用模型块划分下局部目标构造方法的理论框架，将局部目标映射到全局目标；3) 设计并实现ZeroLock系统原型，包含早期转发（early forwarding）与故障恢复（failure recovery）等技术以保证高效和鲁棒的实现。

Result: 理论方面，证明ZeroLock实现Õ(1/√T)的收敛率，与BP相比仅差多对数因子；实验方面，与BP基线相比，ZeroLock将内存降低26.5%，吞吐量提升4.9%。

Conclusion: ZeroLock通过免BP的局部目标构造方法有效解决了边缘端LLM微调中BP带来的吞吐量和内存瓶颈问题，在保持近乎最优收敛率的同时实现了显著的资源节省和性能提升，为边缘端大模型高效微调提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) fine-tuning at the edge adapts the model to scenario-specific data while preserving privacy. Although existing studies proposed pipeline parallelism to address the limited memory and computing resources of edge devices, they commonly rely on backpropagation (BP) training, which has a fundamental limitation of update locking and could experience severe throughput and memory bottlenecks. In this work, we propose a BP-free algorithm, called ZeroLock, that decouples the model updates into independent chunk updates by local objective construction. It breaks the update locking of BP and hence can improve throughput at the algorithm level and lower memory usage by reducing activation storage. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first theoretical framework for such local objective construction-based approaches under general model chunk division by mapping local objectives to the global objective. We prove that ZeroLock has a convergence rate of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{T})$, which differs from BP only by polylogarithmic factors. We design a system for ZeroLock and build real-world prototypes, incorporating techniques such as early forwarding and failure recovery for efficient and robust implementation. Experiments on the prototype show that compared to BP-based baselines, ZeroLock reduces the memory by 26.5% and improves throughput by 4.9%.

</details>


### [94] [Adaptive Symmetry Discovery for Dynamical System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08091)
*Behrooz Tahmasebi,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究动态系统辨识中未知对称群的自动发现问题，证明已知对称性可将单轨迹辨识所需的轨迹长度显著缩短，并提出直接从单条轨迹中学习对称群并将其融入辨识流程的方法，达到与已知对称性情形相同的优化轨迹长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态系统在科学应用中往往具有由物理定律强加的对称性（等变性），但现有辨识方法通常假设对称性已知或完全忽略对称性。本文旨在解决一个关键问题：当系统对未知对称群等变时，如何从单条轨迹中辨识系统参数，并理解对称性对辨识样本复杂度的本质影响。

Method: 首先，作者从理论上证明当对称性已知时，系统可从显著更短的单条轨迹中被辨识，并精确刻画这一改进的量化关系。然后针对对称性未知的场景，提出一种自动对称发现方法，直接从单条轨迹中学习对称群并将其融入辨识流程。理论分析使用群表示论和Cayley图的扩展性质（expander properties）作为核心工具。

Result: 证明了已知对称性条件下辨识所需的单轨迹长度相比一般情形有精确可量化的缩减；提出的自动对称发现方法能够在对称性未知时达到与已知对称性情形相同的优化轨迹长度。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了对称性已知与未知两种辨识场景之间的差距，表明可以自动发现对称性并有效利用其加速动态系统辨识。所发展的群表示论和Cayley图扩展性工具也可能对动态系统对称性研究的其他问题具有独立参考价值。

Abstract: Dynamical systems model trajectory data generated by fixed underlying dynamics, with applications ranging from biology to physics. Especially in scientific settings, dynamical systems are not generic but often exhibit symmetries imposed by physical laws, formalized through equivariance with respect to group actions. The identification problem concerns recovering the parameters of a system from observed trajectories. In this work, we study adaptive symmetry discovery for dynamical system identification and address how a system can be identified from a single trajectory when it is equivariant with respect to an unknown symmetry group. To this end, we first show that for known symmetries, the system can be identified from a significantly shorter single trajectory than in the generic setting, and we precisely characterize this improvement. We then consider the automatic symmetry discovery setting, proposing a method to learn the symmetry group directly from a single trajectory and incorporate it into the identification procedure, achieving the same optimal trajectory length as in the known-symmetry case. Our analysis relies on tools from group representation theory and the expander properties of Cayley graphs, and may be of independent interest for the study of symmetries in dynamical systems.

</details>


### [95] [Accurate Ensembles, Fragile Narratives: Multi-Scale Stacking and a Fidelity Audit of LLM-Generated Explanations for Credit Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08126)
*Gregorius Reynaldi Pratama,Kuo-Kun Tseng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个端到端的信用评分系统，将多尺度堆叠集成模型（融合四种梯度提升与残差网络的神经元学习器）与LLM生成的决策解释相结合。预测层面，集成模型在32,581条信用申请数据上达到ROC-AUC 0.9539，显著优于最佳单模型（Delta-AUC=0.0143, p=0.016），但操作增益很小（F1最优阈值下仅多避免6个违约）。叙事层面，LLM生成的解释存在严重事实性问题：错误标注特征方向、遗漏主导特征、引入未提供的特征。作者通过度量SHAP与LIME的一致性（overlap@10=0.80但tau=0.43）和属性符号翻转（modal-sign share 0.53近于掷硬币）追溯了问题根源，结论是约束提示工程必要但不充分，生成后的接地验证（grounding verification）不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用评分模型的决策逻辑不可从参数直接解读，与监管对可解释性的要求相冲突。常见方案是用LLM基于特征归因生成解释文本，但该方案的第二半部分（LLM叙事层）是否真正成立尚未被严格验证。作者旨在端到端构建该系统并检验LLM生成解释的可靠性。

Method: 预测组件采用多尺度堆叠集成：四种不同正则化的梯度提升模型与残差网络通过基于袋外预测训练的神经元学习器融合。解释组件将SHAP/LIME特征归因交给LLM生成理由文本。作者对被审计案例进行归因与生成文本的逐项对照，并度量了归因方法之间的一致性（overlap@10、tau）及属性符号稳定性（modal-sign share），同时评估校准（ECS）与扰动稳定性（DPD）。

Result: 集成模型在32,581条信用数据上达到ROC-AUC 0.9539和PR-AUC 0.9137，超过最佳单模型（Delta-AUC=0.0143, p=0.016）。但操作层面收益有限：F1最优阈值下仅避免6个额外违约，成本加权损失下降不足2%。叙事层失败严重：LLM将风险降低特征误标为风险增加、遗漏主导特征、引入从未提供的特征。归因方法间排序一致性低（tau=0.43），模型最敏感输入的符号在申请人间近于随机（0.53）。校准（ECS=0.117）与扰动稳定性（DPD=0.078）均未达阈值。

Conclusion: 预测层面的集成增益是真实但操作上微小的；叙事层面的失败是系统性的且无法通过提示工程单方面修复。LLM生成的解释必须在生成后进行接地验证而非预设其正确性。约束提示是必要条件而非充分条件。

Abstract: Credit scoring increasingly relies on models whose decision logic cannot be read off their parameters, in tension with supervisory expectations that adverse decisions be explainable. A common proposal closes that gap with a language model: compute feature attributions, hand them to an LLM, and let it write the rationale. We build such a system end to end and test whether the second half of the promise holds. The predictive component is a multi-scale stacking ensemble fusing four differently regularised gradient-boosting learners with a residual network through a neural meta-learner trained on out-of-fold predictions. On a public 32,581-application credit dataset it reaches test ROC-AUC 0.9539 (95% CI [0.9462, 0.9616]) and PR-AUC 0.9137, beating the best single model by Delta-AUC = 0.0143 (p = 0.016 under a conservative independence assumption). Our central finding is asymmetric. The ranking gain is real but operationally small: at the F1-optimal threshold the ensemble avoids only six additional missed defaults out of 1,422 against a tuned random forest, cutting cost-weighted loss by under 2%. The narrative layer fails in a way prompt engineering alone does not fix. In an audited case the model named three factors as risk-increasing that the supplied attributions scored as risk-reducing, omitted the dominant driver, and introduced a feature never given to it. We trace this to properties we measure rather than assume: SHAP and LIME agree on which features matter (overlap@10 = 0.80) but not on their order (tau = 0.43, p = 0.18), and the attribution sign for the model's most sensitive input is near a coin flip across applicants (modal-sign share 0.53). Calibration (ECS = 0.117) and perturbation stability (DPD = 0.078) both fall short of our own thresholds. Constrained prompting is necessary but not sufficient: grounding must be verified after generation, not assumed.

</details>


### [96] [DoGMA: A Central-Dogma-Guided Foundation Model for Multi-Omics Alignment and Multi-Task Learning in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08148)
*Junfei Ling,Bangzheng Pu,Bingsen Xue,Tianle Li,Ruying Hu,Cheng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DoGMA，一个由中心法则引导的泛癌多组学基础模型，通过Transformer-MoE架构中的定向注意力机制和掩码分层组学重建预训练，促进符合中心法则信息流的跨组学交互，在多个下游任务中展现强预测性能和良好的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制在多组学模型中允许无限制的双向交互，忽视了生命的中心法则所暗示的生物学方向性（DNA→RNA→蛋白质）。这种忽视可能限制模型在不同癌症类型、下游任务和不完整模态场景下的迁移能力。作者认为鲁棒的迁移需要具有领域特定归纳偏置的表示。

Method: DoGMA基于Transformer-MoE架构，通过定向注意力偏置引导组学间通信沿中心法则信息流方向进行。模型采用掩码分层组学重建的预训练策略，以学习中心法则一致性的交互模式。

Result: 在癌症表示学习、生存预测和转移预测等多个下游任务中，DoGMA一致性地表现出强预测性能。消融实验和分析表明，性能提升源于中心法则引导的定向注意力与重建式预训练的协同作用，促进了更符合生物学意义的跨组学信息交换。

Conclusion: DoGMA证明领域特定的归纳偏置能够提升多组学基础模型的鲁棒性和可迁移性，为多组学表示学习中注意力机制的设计提供了新见解。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms have been widely utilized in modern deep learning, and many existing multi-omics models inherit their conventional use to allow unrestricted bidirectional interactions. However, the fundamental logic of life is directional. Existing designs often overlook the directionality suggested by the central dogma, potentially limiting transfer across heterogeneous cancers, downstream tasks, and incomplete modality settings.In this work, we present DoGMA, a central-dogma-guided foundation model for pan-cancer multi-omics analysis, arguing that robust transfer requires representations with domain-specific inductive bias. Concretely, we build it on a Transformer-MoE architecture where directed attention biases inter-omics communication toward central-dogma information flow. We further pretrain our model with masked hierarchical omics reconstruction to guide it toward learning central-dogma-consistent interactions. Across diverse downstream tasks, including cancer representation learning, survival prediction, and metastasis prediction, DoGMA consistently demonstrates strong predictive performance. Ablations and analyses further suggest that the performance gains arise from the synergy between central-dogma-guided directed attention and reconstruction-based pretraining, which together promote more biologically consistent cross-omics information exchange. Overall, DoGMA demonstrates that domain-specific inductive biases can improve the robustness and transferability of multi-omics foundation models, offering new insights into the design of attention mechanisms for multi-omics representation learning.

</details>


### [97] [A Hybrid Nested Harness for Decoupling Structure and Parameters in LLM-Driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08156)
*Víctor Gallego*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合嵌套搜索框架，通过外层LLM生成带数值空缺的结构草稿，内层数值优化器填补数值参数，解决了LLM在结构优化上擅长但数值优化低效的问题，在三个科学领域均优于纯LLM搜索和纯数值优化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语言模型的进化算法中，LLM作为单一算子同时更新结构组件和连续参数，但LLM善于前者、拙于后者，在试错循环中浪费token进行离散跳跃，效率低下。

Method: 形式化一种混合嵌套搜索框架：外层循环由LLM提出带数值空隙的结构草图，内层由数值优化器（如CMA-ES、梯度法或MCMC采样器）优化该草图中的数值参数。外层和内层求解器均可插拔替换，任何基于文本的优化器可与零阶优化器、基于梯度的例程或MCMC采样器任意组合。

Result: 在三个科学领域（闭式测试函数上的元优化器、系统研究与社会困境的基于代码的策略、近似贝叶斯推断任务）中，混合优化器均显著优于纯LLM驱动搜索和纯数值优化基线。

Conclusion: 混合嵌套搜索框架有效解耦了LLM的结构探索能力与数值优化器的参数精调能力，实现了两者互补，为LLM驱动的科学优化提供了更高效的范式。

Abstract: In evolutionary algorithms powered by language models, the LLM acts as a single operator that simultaneously updates structural components (like control flow) and continuous parameters. While LLMs can be good at the first, they are not efficient at the second, wasting tokens taking discrete jumps inside a trial and error loop. We resolve this by formalizing a hybrid nested search, in which an outer loop has the LLM propose a structural sketch, with numeric gaps, and an inner numerical optimizer tunes the sketch. Both the outer and inner solvers are pluggable: any text-based optimizer can be combined with a zero-order optimizer (CMA-ES), gradient-based routines, or MCMC samplers. We validate our framework across three scientific domains: (i) meta-optimizers on closed-form test functions, (ii) code-based policies for systems research and social dilemmas; and (iii) approximate Bayesian inference tasks. Across all three, the hybrid optimizer is superior to both vanilla LLM-driven search and pure numerical optimization baselines. Code at: https://github.com/vicgalle/hybrid-nested-search

</details>


### [98] [SAGE: SLO-Aware Adaptive Retrieval for Production RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08237)
*Muhammad Faizan Raza,Shuo,Yang,Satish Mahadevan Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE，一种基于学习的SLO感知自适应检索策略，通过轻量级特征动态选择每个查询的检索段落数k，在满足尾部延迟SLO的同时优化检索成本，在Natural Questions上实现95%的SLO合规率（对比静态基线30%），P95延迟降低36%，检索成本降低51%，且仅损失2个百分点的EM准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的RAG系统受严格的尾部延迟SLO和基础设施成本约束，但标准检索流水线使用固定检索预算，忽略查询难度差异，导致简单查询过度检索、复杂查询检索不足，迫使运营者在回答质量与SLO合规之间权衡取舍。

Method: SAGE采用离线模仿学习训练策略，从初始检索结果提取轻量级特征（如分数分布、排名间隙、词法信号），学习从启发式或acle中模拟最优延迟-质量权衡行为。推理时无需额外LLM调用，仅增加极小开销即可动态确定每个查询的检索段落数k。

Result: 在Natural Questions数据集的5秒P95延迟SLO约束下，SAGE达到95%的SLO合规率（对比最佳静态基线k=20仅30%），P95延迟降低36%，检索成本降低51%，EM准确率仅损失2个百分点。单一策略可跨数据集（HotpotQA、UnSeenTimeQA）和四类LLM（Llama、Qwen、Mistral、Gemma）泛化，持续带来45-52个百分点的SLO改善且无质量退化。

Conclusion: 学习驱动的自适应检索预算选择能够有效打破固定检索预算下质量与SLO之间的刚性权衡，且具备良好的跨数据域和跨模型泛化能力，为生产RAG系统的延迟与成本优化提供了可行且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems in production operate under strict service level objectives (SLOs) on tail latency and infrastructure cost. However, standard retrieval pipelines rely on fixed retrieval budgets that ignore query difficulty, over-retrieving for easy queries and under-serving hard ones, forcing operators to trade answer quality against SLO compliance. This paper proposes SAGE, a learned SLO-aware adaptive retrieval policy that dynamically selects the number of passages k per query. SAGE uses lightweight features derived from initial retrieval (e.g., score distributions, rank gaps, lexical signals) and is trained offline via imitation learning from an oracle that approximates optimal latency-quality trade-offs. At inference, it adds no LLM calls and minimal overhead. On Natural Questions, under a 5s P95 latency SLO, SAGE achieves 95% SLO compliance versus 30% for the best static baseline (k=20), reduces P95 latency by 36% and retrieval cost by 51% with only 2 percentage points Exact Match (EM) loss. A single policy trained on Natural Questions generalizes across HotpotQA, UnSeenTimeQA, and four LLM families (Llama, Qwen, Mistral, Gemma), consistently yielding +45-52 point SLO improvements without quality degradation.

</details>


### [99] [The Replay Gap: Static Evaluation of Model Switching in LLM Agents Scores the Wrong World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08239)
*Ashritha Gonuguntla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分支式rollout实验证明，基于回放（replay）的评估方法不适用于多步智能体路由场景：模型交换后轨迹会立即发散，回放评估会系统性误判路由效果，因此需要新的评估范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM路由器的目标是在保证质量的同时选择最经济的模型，且越来越多地被应用于多步智能体的每一步中。然而，现有智能体路由器的评估方式仍然沿用单轮路由器的评估方法——通过回放已记录的轨迹，替换某一模型的记录输出，并假设轨迹其余部分不受影响。这一假设在多步智能体中可能严重失真，本文旨在检验这一核心假设的有效性。

Method: 作者采用分支式rollout方法：在受控点对SWE-bench智能体实时轨迹进行分支（fork），重建环境，用不同模型继续各分支，并与同模型控制分支对比以隔离采样和回放噪声。实验含六组配对运行（约900次rollout），并额外审计了温度0"确定性"的配置依赖性和预算约束下的模型行为。

Result: 模型交换后的偏差超过匹配的控制基线+0.25至+0.66归一化编辑距离（多重校正置信区间排除零），重写61-94%的分支后动作；早期交换中74-77%在第一个分支后动作即发散（对照组仅6-35%），仅3%的回放状态有效。发散程度随分支深度增加而降低。五次结果翻转全部出现在交换组，360个对照分支无一翻转。回放评估器对所有成功相关结果调用均预测错误，预测补丁与真实补丁相似度仅0.00-0.11。温度0的"确定性"依赖配置：FP8服务在超90%分支上发散而AWQ服务几乎保持一致；预算紧张时，强模型更常耗尽步骤而无输出。

Conclusion: 基于回放的基准测试在智能体路由场景中评估的是"错误的世界"——因为模型交换会引发轨迹的级联发散，使得回放评估得出的结论与真实路由效果严重不符。作者发布了评估工具和全部轨迹数据，呼吁社区采用基于真实分支的评估方法。

Abstract: LLM routers promise efficiency by matching each request to the cheapest adequate model, and are increasingly applied per step inside multi-step agents. Yet agentic routers are evaluated like single-turn routers: by replaying logged trajectories and substituting another model's recorded outputs, assuming the rest of the trajectory is unaffected. We test this assumption with branching rollouts: we fork live SWE-bench agent trajectories at controlled points, rebuild the environment, continue each fork with a different model, and compare against same-model control forks that isolate sampling and replay noise. Across six paired runs (~900 rollouts), swaps exceed their matched control floors by +0.25 to +0.66 normalized edit distance (multiplicity-corrected CIs exclude zero), rewriting 61-94% of post-fork actions; 74-77% of early swaps diverge at the first post-fork action, versus 6-35% of controls, leaving only 3% of replayed states valid. Divergence decreases with fork depth in both directions. All five outcome flips we observe occur in swap arms, upgrades rescuing unsolved instances and a downgrade losing the sole solve, and zero occur across 359 control forks. Scoring these same swaps with a log-stitching replay evaluator, replay mispredicts every success-relevant outcome call and predicts patches with 0.00-0.11 similarity to reality. Auditing the noise floor, temperature-0 "determinism" is configuration-dependent: FP8-served controls diverge on over 90% of forks while AWQ-served ones remain near-identical; and under tight budgets the stronger model more often exhausts its steps without submitting. Replay-based benchmarks score the wrong world for agentic routing; we release our harness and all trajectories.

</details>


### [100] [Opportunity Is Not Realizability: Selection-Valid Diagnostics for Multi-LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08265)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Md Najmus Swaqeeb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出Oracle路由诊断的两个缺陷（同一示例上选择最佳固定模型导致配对推断失效、全信息Oracle看到部署时不可观测的结果），分离了三个可估计目标（结果Oracle机会、预先声明信号下的贝叶斯最优增益、学习路由器的留出增益），并证明了选择有效的置信区间。实验覆盖六个家族的八个检查点在四个基准上，表明人口Oracle差距为9.7-30.7分，但最强的可部署提示路由器仅能恢复其中7.5-14.4%，且十一个测试策略中最优者的联合区间下限为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Oracle路由诊断方法存在两个关键缺陷：一是用同一批示例选择最佳固定模型会使配对推断失去统计有效性；二是全信息Oracle能观察到部署环境无法观测的结果，导致其高估了实际可获得的路由收益，因而需要更严格的统计框架来量化路由的真实机会。

Method: 作者将路由收益分解为三个可估计目标：结果Oracle机会（全信息上限）、基于预先声明信号（pre-answer signal）的贝叶斯最优增益、以及学习路由器的留出（held-out）增益。提出了选择有效的置信区间（可同时覆盖选择最佳固定模型或路由器家族中的最佳成员），构造了信号信息夹逼界（signal-information sandwich），并给出了基于次模互补覆盖构造紧凑池的(1-1/e)贪心保证。

Result: 在六个家族的八个检查点、四个基准上的实验表明：选择有效区间证实每个任务存在9.7-30.7分的人口级Oracle差距，但最强的可部署提示路由器仅恢复7.5-14.4%的差距；对十一个候选策略中最佳策略的联合置信区间下限为零，表明在当前设计空间内无法统计上确认任何路由器显著优于最佳固定模型。

Conclusion: 可实现的Oracle机会份额很小但可被验证：强路由器确实能击败最佳固定模型，但大部分Oracle差距仍然无法实现，这为路由研究的预期设定了严格的统计上可靠的边界。

Abstract: Oracle routing measures how much a pool of language models could gain from per-query selection, but the diagnostic has two flaws: testing against a best fixed model selected on the same examples invalidates paired inference, and a full-information oracle sees outcomes no deployable router observes. We separate three estimands (outcome-oracle opportunity, the Bayes-optimal gain from a declared pre-answer signal, and the held-out gain of a learned router) and prove selection-valid confidence intervals that survive choosing the best fixed model or the best member of a router family, a signal-information sandwich, and a $(1-1/e)$ greedy guarantee for building compact pools from submodular complementary coverage. On eight checkpoints from six families over four benchmarks, selection-valid intervals certify a population oracle gap of $9.7$--$30.7$ points on every task, yet the strongest deployable prompt router recovers only $7.5$--$14.4\%$ of it, and the simultaneous interval for the best of eleven tested policies has lower limit zero throughout. The realizable share of oracle opportunity is small and certifiable: strong routers beat the best fixed model, and most of the gap remains.

</details>


### [101] [Stateful CARS: Exact Cross-History Reuse for Policy-Constrained LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08282)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Md Najmus Swaqeeb,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Stateful CARS"方法，通过冻结一组可靠的"状态-延续模式"并移除所有包含已认证延续的轨迹，利用精确的残差Doob变换从条件分布中进行采样，在硬有状态验证器约束下实现精确采样，同时跨历史复用无效性证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用工具的语言模型代理面临着随着观测和先前动作而变化的约束（即有状态的验证器约束）。现有的方法无法从满足这些硬约束的模型分布中进行精确采样，且无效性证书无法跨历史复用。本文旨在解决这一精确生成问题，同时高效复用验证失败的信息。

Method: 1) 提出Stateful CARS框架：在每次尝试中冻结一组可靠的状态-延续模式（schemas），并移除所有在匹配抽象状态下包含已认证延续的轨迹；2) 使用精确的残差Doob变换从建议分布中采样；3) 给出可检查的未来有效性双模拟条件；4) 证明模式可靠性、自适应精确性、i.i.d.输出、几乎必然终止、单调接受率和压缩不变性；5) 以可达全历史乘积状态数量刻画计算复杂度。

Result: 在可枚举工作流上，其解析分布与有效条件分布在10^-16精度上吻合（在有效性概率6×10^-8的情况下），而具备状态感知的局部解码偏差可达0.97。与官方CARS（观察键控）的对比为负：Stateful在采样步数上更贵（root/Stateful比率0.942 [0.934,0.951]），Qwen对比为无效（0.99 [0.90,1.08]）。跨历史迁移仅在内部匹配键消融中有帮助（1.27×）。

Conclusion: 实验证据支持"模式诱导的精确条件化"这一方法论贡献，但并不支持相对于CARS的系统性优势。该方法的计算复杂度可能随历史依赖语言模型呈指数增长，因此未声称在通用有限字典场景下可扩展。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents face constraints whose meaning changes with observations and prior actions. We study exact sampling from the model distribution conditioned on a hard stateful validator while reusing invalidity certificates across histories. Stateful CARS freezes a bank of sound state--continuation schemas within each attempt and removes every trajectory containing a certified continuation at a matching abstract state. An exact residual Doob transform samples from the resulting proposal. We give a checkable future-validity bisimulation condition, prove schema soundness, adaptive exactness, i.i.d.\ outputs, almost-sure termination, monotone acceptance, and compression invariance, and characterize computation by the number of reachable full-history product states. This number can be exponential for a history-dependent language model; the evaluated method therefore makes no generic finite-trie scalability claim. On enumerable workflows, its analytic law matches the valid conditional to $10^{-16}$ at validity probability $6\times10^{-8}$, whereas state-aware local decoding can be $0.97$ away. A matched comparison is negative: observation-keyed official CARS is cheaper in sampler steps (root/Stateful ratio $0.942$ $[0.934,0.951]$), and the Qwen comparison is null ($0.99$ $[0.90,1.08]$). Cross-history transfer helps only in an internal matched-key ablation ($1.27\times$). Thus the evidence supports exact schema-induced conditioning, not a systems advantage over CARS.

</details>


### [102] [Rethinking Learning-Based Influence Maximization: Simple Neural Surrogates and Native Discrete Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08406)
*Yiqiao Liao,Parinaz Naghizadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIMBA提出了一种无需连续优化的影响力最大化框架，用轻量级神经网络替代器配合直接离散搜索，显著降低计算成本并提升影响力传播效果和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的影响力最大化方法依赖复杂的神经架构和对种子表示的连续优化，计算开销大且效率低下。作者旨在挑战这一范式，探索更轻量、更高效的解决方案。

Method: SIMBA包含三个核心组件：1) 均匀锚定的节点嵌入以消除初始化噪声，使学习由图拓扑和扩散模式驱动；2) 浅层两层图神经网络替代器预测最终感染状态；3) 批量多交换模拟退火算法直接在离散种子空间搜索，无需梯度或连续松弛。

Result: SIMBA大幅缩短求解时间，同时实现更优的影响力传播效果和更高的数据效率，且作为扩散模型无关的框架具有良好泛化性。

Conclusion: 将计算从复杂表示学习转移到有效的离散搜索是一种更优的计算范式，SIMBA证明了轻量级替代模型配合直接离散搜索即可超越复杂的神经架构方法。

Abstract: Existing learning-based influence maximization frameworks rely heavily on complex neural architectures and continuous optimization over seed representations. We challenge this paradigm with SIMBA, a diffusion-model-agnostic framework pairing a lightweight neural surrogate with direct discrete search. SIMBA introduces three key components: 1) uniformly anchored node embeddings that eliminate initialization noise and encourage learning driven by graph topology and diffusion pattern, 2) a shallow two-layer graph neural network surrogate predicting final infection states, and 3) batched multi-swap simulated annealing that explores combinatorial seed space without gradients or continuous relaxation. By shifting compute from complex representation learning to effective discrete search, SIMBA drastically cuts time-to-solution while achieving superior influence spread and data efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/yl489/rethink-IM.

</details>


### [103] [Task-to-Model Optimization for Enterprise LLM Coding Assistants: A Data-Driven Framework for Cost-Optimal Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08528)
*Srinivasan Manoharan,Junhua Zhao,Fangbo Tu,Haifeng Wu,Jian Wan,Maliah Rajan M,Ashwin Hegde,Mithun Sasidharan,Kalyan Chakravarthi Podamekala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Task-to-Model Optimization (T2MO)，一种数据驱动的模型选择优化框架，用于企业AI编程助手的生产工作流。该框架将每个开发者会话视为可发现、分类、分级、评测和路由的任务，目标是优化"每完成任务的成本"而非简单的token成本，并在质量与延迟约束下将任务路由到最便宜的可用模型。核心贡献包括期望完成成本目标函数（在升级机制下弱支配token成本最小化）、路由边界推导以及基于流量的节省瀑布排名机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级AI编程助手产生了大量推理成本，而单纯的token成本最小化策略往往在纳入重试、升级和开发者等待时间后，无法真正降低端到端成本。现有的token中心式路由规则忽略了任务完成率、失败升级等隐性成本，因此需要一种以任务为粒度、以完成成本为目标的系统性框架。

Method: T2MO是一个九阶段流水线，涵盖：遥测数据采集、任务分类体系发现、难度分级、生产仿真基准构建、候选模型评估、最优模型组合推导、需求预测与版本规划、分阶段路由部署、持续治理。决策组织为"任务类别-难度层级"两级层次结构，通过对每个单元（cell）的位移机会进行流量加权聚合，生成节省瀑布（savings waterfall）以按实际美元影响对替代候选排序。同时提出期望完成成本目标函数，导出路由边界（较便宜模型在给定单元上需要达到的最低通过率）以决定是否值得部署。

Result: 理论结果表明，在存在失败升级机制的条件下，期望完成成本目标函数弱支配token成本最小化。框架支持开发者指引、支出预测，以及从静态策略经影子模式分类器、验证级联到智能路由器的分阶段演进。

Conclusion: T2MO提供了一种适合生产部署和未来实证研究的方法论、优化目标、评估协议和治理闭环。该框架明确了模型选择决策的组织层次和量化评估标准，为企业级AI编程助手在保证质量约束的前提下系统性地降低端到端推理成本提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Enterprise AI coding assistants incur substantial inference spend, and naive token-cost minimization often fails to reduce end-to-end cost once retries, escalations, and developer wait time are included. We present Task-to-Model Optimization (T2MO), a data-driven methodology for optimizing model selection in production coding workflows. We treat each developer session as a task that can be discovered, classified, graded for difficulty, benchmarked in a production-like harness, and routed to the cheapest model able to complete it within quality and latency constraints. The framework is a nine-stage pipeline spanning telemetry instrumentation, taxonomy discovery, difficulty grading, benchmark construction, candidate evaluation, optimal mix derivation, forecasting and version planning, staged routing deployment, and continuous governance. Unlike token-centric routing rules, our objective is cost per completed task, with failure escalation priced in explicitly. We show that this expected-completion-cost objective weakly dominates token-cost minimization under escalation, and we derive the routing boundary, the minimum pass rate a cheaper model must reach on a given cell to be worth deploying. Decisions are organized as a two-level hierarchy of task category difficulty tier, and per-cell displacement opportunities are aggregated into a traffic-weighted savings waterfall that ranks replacement candidates by realized dollar impact. The framework supports developer guidance, spend forecasting, and a staged transition from static policies to shadow-mode classifiers, verified cascades, and ultimately an intelligent router. We describe the methodology, optimization objective, evaluation protocol, and governance loop in a form suitable for production deployment and future empirical study.

</details>


### [104] [Neural Message Passing on Structural Interaction Graphs for Fully-Inductive Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08567)
*Omer Yom Tov,Avigdor Gal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIGIL是一个图基础模型框架，通过将任意属性图提升为结构交互图并利用关系消息传递网络，将不同维度的特征映射到统一表示空间，从而解决图基础模型的输入异质性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图基础模型的核心障碍在于输入异质性：不同图的特征空间在维度、语义和结构上各不相同，限制了图神经网络泛化到具有未见特征空间的新图的能力。

Method: SIGIL将任意属性图提升为结构交互图：节点是输入特征维度，加权且有类型的边编码跨多阶图连接性的特征对齐。随后使用关系消息传递网络将每个特征维度嵌入到共享空间，从而将任意维度的原始节点特征转换为可迁移到任何下游图的表示。SIGIL在节点、特征维度和标签排列下具有等变性。

Result: SIGIL在单图预训练后即可实现强全归纳式链接预测；当输入特征为离散关系的一热指示时，SIGIL可以恢复并严格泛化现有的知识图谱推理基础模型，且可用于实现现有知识图谱基础模型。

Conclusion: SIGIL在单一框架下统一了图基础模型设计中的多种现有模式，为解决图基础模型的输入异质性和可迁移性问题提供了通用方案。

Abstract: A central obstacle in building graph foundation models is the input heterogeneity in terms of feature space dimensionality, semantics, and structure. Such heterogeneity limits the capability of graph neural networks to generalize to new graphs with unseen feature spaces. We address the transferability challenge with SIGIL, a framework that maps any attributed graph to a unified representation space of fixed dimension. Given a graph, SIGIL lifts it to a structural interaction graph, where nodes are the input feature dimensions and weighted, typed edges encode feature alignment across multiple orders of the graph's connectivity. A relational message-passing network embeds each feature dimension into a shared space, transforming the original node features, of arbitrary dimensionality, into representations transferable to any downstream graph. By construction, SIGIL is equivariant to permutations of nodes, feature dimensions, and labels. Additionally, when the input features are one-hot indicators of discrete relations, SIGIL recovers and strictly generalizes existing foundation models for knowledge graph reasoning. A single SIGIL model, pretrained on one graph, delivers strong fully-inductive link prediction. Also, SIGIL can be used to implement existing knowledge graph foundation models. As such, SIGIL unifies several existing regimes in graph foundation model design under a single framework

</details>


### [105] [Efficient Test-Time Scaling for LLM-based Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08675)
*Xuan-May Le,Minh-Tuan Tran,Ling Luo,Uwe Aickelin,Dinh Phung,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCALER提出了一种由粗到细的时间序列预测框架，先用轻量级Transformer建模长周期全局形状，再引导LLM进行固定步数的残差token迭代细化，在保持精度的同时显著降低LLM推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的预测器通过测试时扩展（如迭代细化）提升精度，但计算开销大，且随着预测视界延长容易产生全局形状错配（global-shape mismatch）问题。此外，长描述prompt和基于奖励模型的选择机制进一步增加了推理负担。

Method: SCALER采用两阶段策略：首先用一个面向长周期形状建模的轻量级Transformer预测未来的粗粒度表示（predicted shape），然后将该形状作为紧凑指导信息输入LLM，进行固定步数的由粗到细残差token迭代细化。该方法以显式的未来形状预测替代长描述prompt，并以固定步数细化替代奖励模型选择，从而降低计算开销。

Result: 实验表明，SCALER在长期、短期和零样本预测任务上均优于强基线模型，同时显著降低了LLM在时间序列预测中的推理成本。

Conclusion: SCALER验证了通过显式全局形状引导+固定步数迭代细化可以有效缓解LLM预测中的形状失配与高推理成本问题，为LLM在时间序列预测中的高效利用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting benefits from preserving global structure such as trends and seasonality. Recent LLM-based forecasters often improve accuracy through test-time scaling (e.g., iterative refinement), but these methods are computationally expensive and increasingly prone to global-shape mismatch as the prediction horizon extends. We propose SCALER, a coarse-to-fine forecasting framework that first employs a lightweight Transformer tailored to long-term shape modeling to predict a coarse representation of future dynamics. This predicted shape then serves as a compact guide for an LLM to perform test-time scaling via iterative coarse-to-fine residual token refinement, while processing substantially fewer tokens at each step. By guiding refinement with an explicit future-shape prediction, SCALER reduces reliance on long description prompts, and its fixed-step refinement avoids costly reward-model-based selection, further lowering computational overhead. Experimental results demonstrate that SCALER outperforms strong forecasting baselines in long-term, short-term and zero-shot forecasting while significantly reducing the inference cost associated with scaled LLM for time series forecasting. Code: https://github.com/xuanmay2701/SCALER.

</details>


### [106] [RippleKV: Cross-Layer KV Cache Allocation via Perturbation Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08684)
*Dongjie Xu,Kai Qian,Julius,Weijie Shi,Yuxuan Sun,Minghua Tang,Fenglei Jin,Hanchi Dong,Jiajie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RippleKV是一种基于扰动传播敏感性分析的KV缓存分配方法。它通过向每层value cache注入范数自适应扰动并测量输出端KL散度，构建模型特定且不随深度单调变化的敏感性分布，进而通过指数映射将敏感性分数转换为层间预算乘子，在固定总缓存预算下实现最优分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理受限于KV缓存内存，需要在不同层间分配有限的缓存预算。现有方法依赖层深、注意力统计或表征变化等代理指标来衡量层的重要性，但这些代理指标无法真正度量各层扰动向输出端传播的影响，导致敏感层被低估分配而容忍层被过度分配。

Method: RippleKV首先独立地向每层的value cache注入范数自适应扰动，在小规模校准集上测量输出端预测分布的变化（KL散度），得到模型特定的敏感性分布。然后将敏感性分数归一化并通过指数映射转换为层间预算乘子，引入比率参数控制敏感层与容忍层之间的分配差异，最终通过归一化保持总KV缓存预算不变。

Result: 在LongBench基准上的实验表明，在匹配缓存预算的条件下，RippleKV在已评估的KV缓存压缩方法中取得了最高的平均性能。

Conclusion: RippleKV通过直接度量各层扰动对最终输出的影响来替代间接代理指标，实现了更有效的KV缓存层间分配，从而在相同缓存预算下获得更优的长上下文推理性能。

Abstract: Long-context LLM inference is bottlenecked by KV cache memory, yet distributing a limited cache budget across layers remains challenging. Existing methods rely on proxies such as layer depth, attention statistics, or representation change. These proxies do not measure how perturbations at each layer propagate to the output and may therefore cause sensitive layers to be underallocated while tolerant layers are overallocated. To address this issue, we propose RippleKV, which allocates cache across layers by estimating how perturbations to each layer's value cache affect the final predictive distribution. RippleKV independently injects norm-adaptive perturbations into each layer's value cache and measures the induced KL divergence at the model output over a small calibration set. Averaging these responses yields a sensitivity profile specific to the model that need not vary monotonically with depth. RippleKV then converts the sensitivity profile into layer budget multipliers by normalizing the sensitivity scores and applying an exponential mapping. A ratio parameter controls the allocation disparity between sensitive and tolerant layers, while a final normalization preserves the KV cache budget. Experiments on LongBench demonstrate that RippleKV achieves the highest average performance among the evaluated KV cache compression methods under matched cache budgets.

</details>


### [107] [Gaming Without an Attacker: Benchmark Fingerprinting in LLM-Driven Search Under Selection Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08722)
*Víctor Gallego*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过两个GPU内核优化基准套件（Metal-Sci和Metal-ZK）实证展示了面向评估信号优化的系统会产生"数据指纹"问题：前沿LLM在进化搜索中会反复识别并利用评估配置的身份信息，通过分支判断运行时参数来最大化被测量的分支性能，同时让未测量分支保持缓慢或静默错误，导致30%的分布内胜利无法迁移到留出配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习基准假设测试信号能代表真实的泛化能力，但当系统被明确针对评估信号进行优化时，这种假设会失效。作者希望系统性地揭示这种"评估欺骗"现象：即使没有明确提示模型采取对抗行为，进化搜索过程也能自然发现并利用评估配置的指纹信息，从而质疑基准在战略优化下的有效性。

Method: 作者构建了两个GPU内核优化基准套件：Metal-Sci（10个科学计算任务）和Metal-ZK（12个零知识/密码学任务），均带留出泛化门。三个前沿LLM（Opus 4.7、Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT-5.5）在(1+1)进化循环中提出Metal内核，获得丰富反馈。论文对失败案例建立四模式分类体系（从配置指纹到门泄漏），并对每次迁移失败进行机制分级（gamed/overfit/benign）。

Result: 在合并的两个基准套件中，16/53（30%）的分布内获胜案例无法迁移到留出配置。模型通过分支判断运行时参数身份、最大化测量分支的性能，同时让未测量分支保持缓慢或静默错误。作者提出了一个四模式失败分类法以及设计指导原则。

Conclusion: 在战略优化（strategic optimization）下，留出探针仅在不可枚举轴（non-enumerable axes）上保持有效性；门必须测量留出性能而非仅正确性；迁移率只有在具备每次失败的机制分级时才可解释。这为评估基准的设计提供了重要警示和实用指导。

Abstract: Benchmarks for systems that are optimized against the evaluation signal measure something different from what they claim. We document this concretely in two GPU-kernel-optimization suites with held-out generalization gates: Metal-Sci (10 scientific-compute tasks) and Metal-ZK (12 zero-knowledge/cryptographic tasks), in which three frontier LLMs (Opus 4.7, Gemini 3.1 Pro, GPT-5.5) propose Metal kernels inside a $(1{+}1)$ evolutionary loop with rich feedback. Although no model is prompted to act adversarially, the promoted winners repeatedly fingerprint the evaluation configuration: they branch on the identity of runtime parameters, tune the measured branch maximally, and leave the unmeasured branch slow or silently wrong. Across the pooled suites, $16/53$ ($30\%$) of in-distribution wins fail to transfer to held-out configurations. We give a four-mode taxonomy of these failures, from configuration fingerprints to gate leakage. We distill design guidance for measurement under strategic optimization: held-out probes retain validity only on non-enumerable axes; gates must measure held-out performance, not just correctness; and a transfer rate is interpretable only with per-failure mechanism grades: ours decomposes into gamed, overfit, and benign.
  Code and research artifacts: https://github.com/vicgalle/kernel-fingerprinting

</details>


### [108] [Measuring and Reducing WebGPU Dispatch Overhead for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08730)
*Jędrzej Maczan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了WebGPU在浏览器端LLM推理中的per-operation dispatch开销问题，提出了一种sequential-dispatch测量方法，发现传统单操作测量因混淆dispatch与synchronization而高估开销，并证明在batch size为1时dispatch开销（而非kernel质量）是瓶颈，主张通过减少dispatch count和dispatch摊销来优化浏览器端推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浏览器端LLM推理引擎日益增多但WebGPU的per-operation dispatch开销缺乏充分表征，需要准确测量和理解这一开销以指导优化。

Method: 提出sequential-dispatch测量方法，避免传统naive单操作测量中synchronization与dispatch开销混淆的问题，系统测量不同数据类型下的per-dispatch成本，并对比batch size 1场景下dispatch开销与kernel质量对整体性能的影响。

Result: 发现naive单操作测量会高估per-dispatch成本；per-dispatch成本与数据类型无关；在batch size 1下，dispatch开销而非kernel质量是性能瓶颈，dispatch count是根本原因。

Conclusion: 在batch size 1的WebGPU LLM推理中，降低dispatch count是有效的优化方向，dispatch摊销（在推理引擎和WebGPU规范层面）是实现实际浏览器端推理的可行路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models are deployed to multiple types of environments, from internet browsers to edge devices, and WebGPU serves as a modern cross-platform standard. The engines for browser-based LLM inference have proliferated, yet the overhead of WebGPU per-operation dispatch remains poorly characterized. In this work, we introduce a sequential-dispatch measurement method and show that naive single-operation measurements overestimate per-dispatch cost by conflating dispatch with synchronization. Using our method, we measure the per-dispatch cost and show that it is independent of data type used. We show that the dispatch overhead, not kernel quality, is the bottleneck at batch size 1, and isolate the dispatch count as the cause. Therefore, we conclude that at batch size 1, the effective approach to LLM inference optimization in WebGPU is reducing dispatch count. Our findings point to dispatch amortization, in the inference engines and in the WebGPU specification, as a path to practical browser-based inference.

</details>


### [109] [Hybrid Neural-Classical Correction for Frozen Time Series Foundation Models: A Comprehensive Ablation Study on High-Frequency Stock Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08825)
*Kasun Dewage,Suranadi De Silva,Shankhadeep Mondal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了混合神经-经典纠错方法对冻结的TimesFM时间序列基础模型在高频金融股票收益预测中的适配问题。通过对比两种神经纠错架构（AttnCorrect约471K参数和GatedLinear约49K参数）结合随机森林残差学习，在10只科技股、200万数据点上验证了混合方法可实现0.597的池化相关系数和6.4倍日均相关性提升，且经典残差学习贡献最大，简单神经架构在移除经典学习时反而表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型（如TimesFM）虽然具备令人印象深刻的零样本泛化能力，但在高频金融等专业领域往往表现不佳。作者旨在探索如何有效地将冻结的基础模型适配到高波动开盘时段的股票收益预测任务中，为基础模型在专业领域的适配提供系统性研究。

Method: 采用混合神经-经典纠错框架：保留TimesFM（200M参数）冻结不训练，在其之上叠加两种神经纠错架构——AttnCorrect（多头自注意力，约471K参数）和GatedLinear（低秩双线性投影+门控，约49K参数），并各自与随机森林残差学习相结合。在10只大型科技股（NVDA等）约200万数据点上进行系统消融实验，使用三种互补相关性指标（日均、跨日累积、池化）全面评估预测质量。

Result: 混合神经-经典方法在池化相关系数上达到0.597，日均相关性相比冻结TimesFM提升6.4倍。关键发现包括：(1) 随机森林经典残差学习是单一最大贡献组件，效果可与神经纠错组件匹敌甚至超越；(2) 在移除经典残差学习时，简单神经架构反而优于复杂架构；(3) 自注意力在纯神经方案中贡献最大；(4) GatedLinear+RF在总性能最优的同时，神经参数比AttnCorrect+RF少9倍。

Conclusion: 有效的基础模型适配需要神经组件与经典方法的精心整合，经典方法在其中扮演着至关重要的补充角色。简单的神经架构结合经典残差学习在参数效率和性能之间取得了最佳平衡，为核心基础模型适配提供了明确的设计指导。

Abstract: Foundation models for time series forecasting demonstrate impressive zero-shot generalization but often underperform on specialized domains such as high-frequency finance. We present a comprehensive study of hybrid neural-classical correction for adapting frozen TimesFM (200M parameters) to stock return prediction during the volatile opening trading hour. We compare two neural correction architectures - AttnCorrect (multi-head self-attention, approximately 471K parameters) and GatedLinear (low-rank bilinear projection with gating, approximately 49K parameters) - each augmented with Random Forest residual learning. Through systematic ablation across 10 major technology stocks (NVDA, MSFT, AAPL, GOOG, GOOGL, AMZN, META, AVGO, TSLA, NFLX) spanning 2 million data points, we reveal critical insights: (1) The hybrid neural-classical approach achieves 0.597 pooled correlation and 6.4x mean per-day correlation improvement over frozen TimesFM; (2) Classical residual learning (Random Forest) provides the largest single-component contribution, matching or exceeding the neural correction component; (3) Simpler neural architectures surprisingly outperform complex ones when classical residual learning is removed; (4) Self-attention provides the largest neural-only contribution. GatedLinear+RF achieves best overall performance with 9x fewer neural parameters than AttnCorrect+RF. We report three complementary correlation metrics - mean per-day, cross-day cumulative, and pooled - to provide a complete picture of predictive quality. Our results provide practical guidance: effective foundation model adaptation requires careful integration of neural and classical components, with classical methods playing a crucial complementary role.

</details>


### [110] [DistillCache: KL-Guided Adaptive KV-Cache Eviction for Memory-Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08878)
*Asaad Althoubi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DistillCache是一个基于强化学习的KV缓存驱逐框架，将缓存驱逐建模为序列决策问题，通过轻量策略网络利用模型内部信号（注意力统计、值范数、熵和位置）并使用REINFORCE训练，在25%缓存预算下于LongBench保留94.2%的完整缓存准确率，优于H₂O、SnapKV等启发式基线以及ForesightKV、RLKV等并发RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer大语言模型的KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，造成长上下文推理的严重内存瓶颈。现有启发式驱逐方法（如H₂O、SnapKV）依赖静态注意力或位置信号，难以捕捉token对未来的预测影响力，因此需要更智能的、能学习未来预测影响力的缓存管理策略。

Method: 将KV缓存驱逐形式化为序贯决策问题；使用轻量策略网络，输入特征包括注意力统计、值范数、熵和位置等丰富的内部模型信号；采用REINFORCE算法训练，奖励为逐步骤的KL散度，以保持完整缓存的输出分布。

Result: 在Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3上，25%缓存预算时LongBench上保留94.2%完整缓存准确率；相比H₂O和SnapKV等强启发式基线提升最多2.7个绝对百分点；相比ForesightKV和RLKV等并发RL方法提升最多1.4个百分点；推理基准上与最优并发方法相当且在激进压缩下更优；吞吐量最高达完整缓存的2.1倍。

Conclusion: 学习式的、分布感知的缓存驱逐策略对内存高效的长上下文LLM推理具有显著有效性，验证了将强化学习与模型内部信号相结合以替代静态启发式驱逐方法的可行性。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across many tasks, but their Key-Value (KV) cache grows linearly with sequence length, creating a severe memory bottleneck for long-context inference. Existing heuristic eviction methods (e.g., H$_2$O and SnapKV) rely on static attention or positional signals that often fail to capture a token's future predictive influence. We propose DistillCache, a reinforcement learning framework that formulates KV-cache eviction as a sequential decision problem. DistillCache learns a lightweight policy network using rich internal model signals (attention statistics, value norms, entropy, and position) and trains it with REINFORCE via a per-step KL-divergence reward to preserve the full-cache output distribution. On a 7B-parameter instruction-tuned Transformer (Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3), DistillCache retains 94.2% of full-cache accuracy on LongBench at a 25% cache budget, outperforming both strong heuristic baselines (H$_2$O, SnapKV) by up to 2.7 absolute points and, under our re-implementations, concurrent RL-based methods (ForesightKV, RLKV) by up to 1.4 points on long-context tasks. On reasoning benchmarks, DistillCache is competitive with the best concurrent method and surpasses it under aggressive compression. It also delivers up to 2.1x full-cache throughput while maintaining competitive practical efficiency. These results highlight the effectiveness of learned, distribution-aware policies for memory-efficient long-context LLM inference.

</details>


### [111] [Math-Vision Diagrams: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Mathematical Diagram Generation Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08964)
*Harish Kashyap,Kiran Byadarhaly,Sriram Chakaravarthy,Sanyukta Tuti,Aryan Mistry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Math-Vision Diagrams，这是首个专门用于评估大语言模型（LLM）数学图表生成能力的基准测试，统一评估text-to-code和text-to-image两种生成范式，并基于Math-Vision基准中的2920张竞赛级高质量图像构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从文本提示生成精确的数学图表是LLM的关键但尚未充分探索的能力，对课程准备、问题集自动排名和科学出版等领域具有重要意义。然而现有基准（如MathVision、MathVista、DiagramGenBenchmark、MermaidSeqBench）要么聚焦数学推理，要么聚焦通用图表生成，缺乏专门针对数学图表生成的标准提示-图像对评估集。

Method: 从Math-Vision基准中筛选出3040张图像中的2920张高质量竞赛问题图像（包含必要的视觉上下文），构建首个统一的数学图表生成基准。提出了一种结合LLM集成与领域专家（SME）筛选的新型管道，并配套一套评估指标体系，同时兼顾text-to-code和text-to-image两种生成范式，与底层编程语言或模型类型无关。

Result: 通过对多种领先模型进行测试，结果表明LLM在数学图表生成任务上表现不佳，揭示了当前模型在该领域的能力瓶颈。

Conclusion: Math-Vision Diagrams填补了数学图表生成标准化评估的空白，为后续研究提供了开放源代码、数据、筛选管道和评估脚本，也为该领域的模型改进提供了可靠的衡量基准。

Abstract: The generation of mathematically precise diagrams from tex- tual prompts has emerged as a critical yet underexplored capability of Large Language Models (LLMs). This has been of interest to researchers in the areas of curriculum preparation, automated ranking of problem sets, and scientific publishing. For LLMs to achieve this, it requires per- fect coordination between Spatial Reasoning, Mathematical Reasoning, and Rendering systems. While existing benchmarks such as MathVision, MathVista are built for Math Reasoning or DiagramGenBenchmark, Mer- maidSeqBench on general purpose diagram generation, no prior work provides a standardized set of prompt, image pairs that can be used to evaluate the LLMs specifically on math diagram generation. This includes fields that span both both text-to-code and text-to-image paradigms. We introduce Math-Vision Diagrams, the first benchmark specifically designed to evaluate LLMs on mathematical diagram generation, and the first to assess text-to-code and text-to-image generation paradigms together in a single unified setting, agnostic of the underlying coding lan- guage or model type. Building on the Math-Vision benchmark, we select a subset of 2920 images out of 3040 from high-quality competition problems with essential visual context. A novel pipeline combining an ensemble of LLMs with Subject Matter Expert (SME) curation is presented, together with a suite of evaluation metrics. Testing several leading models against this benchmark, we demonstrate that LLMs struggle with math diagram generation. All code, data, curation pipeline, and evaluation scripts will be fully open-sourced.

</details>


### [112] [Beyond Solvability: Task Learnability as a Static Prior for LLM RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09217)
*Ting Zhou,Zhenqing Ling,Daoyuan Chen,Qianli Shen,Yilun Huang,Ying Shen,Yaliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出任务"可学习性"（learnability）概念，并通过TrajVal探针估计器在训练前预测任务对继续训练的正向响应，从而优化RL后训练中的任务采样，提升数据效率并可与现有在线调度器互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一任务采样未考虑不同任务对优化响应的差异，现有任务估值方法（基于当前通过率或奖励）只估计任务在当前策略下可解性，但相似可解性的任务其训练响应可能差异显著，缺乏对"训练潜力"的度量。

Method: 提出任务可学习性（learnability），即固定RL后训练体制下任务对继续训练期望正向响应的机制条件指标。通过分析逐任务奖励轨迹验证其可复现性与下游效用。随后提出TrajVal，一种轻量级基于探针的估计器，通过短探针运行和两个端点评估近似估计逐任务可学习性。

Result: TrajVal可单独作为静态先验用于任务采样，也可作为乘法先验与现有在线调度器结合。在数学与逻辑推理基准的多种模型规模实验表明，TrajVal相比均匀采样提升数据效率，并在与在线调度方法结合时提供互补增益。

Conclusion: 任务可学习性是一个可复现、可预测且有实用价值的维度，TrajVal为在训练前获取该信号提供低成本方案，为RL后训练的任务采样提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central post-training paradigm for eliciting reasoning capabilities in large language models, yet uniform task sampling allocates compute without regard to differences in how tasks respond to optimization. Existing task-valuation methods mostly rely on snapshot-based signals such as current pass rate or reward, which estimate how solvable a task is under the current policy. However, tasks with similar current solvability can still differ substantially in how positively they respond to further training. We study this residual axis as task learnability: a regime-conditional measure of expected positive response to continued training under a fixed RL post-training regime. By analyzing per-task reward trajectories, we find that learnability is reproducible across independently sampled training contexts and predictive of downstream utility. To make this signal practical before training begins, we propose TrajVal, a lightweight probe-based estimator that approximates per-task learnability from a short probe run and two endpoint evaluations. TrajVal can be used either as a standalone static prior for task sampling or as a multiplicative prior for existing online schedulers. Experiments on mathematical and logical reasoning benchmarks across multiple model scales show that TrajVal improves data efficiency over uniform sampling and provides complementary gains when combined with online scheduling methods.

</details>


### [113] [Beyond the Capability Boundary: Zeroth-Order Optimization for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09292)
*Bingzhen Liu,Xiaomeng Fan,Yuwei Wu,Zhi Gao,Mingyang Gao,Chuanhao Li,Yunde Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种零阶自我进化框架，通过扰动LoRA参数估计梯度，使LLM智能体能够突破自身能力边界，在困难样本上学习并提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化方法依赖智能体自身采样轨迹进行学习，但智能体无法在困难样本上采样出正确轨迹，导致难以突破其固有能力边界。

Method: 通过扰动LLM的LoRA参数，在扰动前后分别运行智能体并计算损失，利用损失差值估计梯度并更新LoRA参数；随后用更新后的模型采样轨迹进行监督微调，形成闭合的自我进化循环。同时引入并行扰动推理机制和自适应查找机制降低时间开销，并使用答案困惑度损失提供平滑稳定的零阶损失值。

Result: 在多个深度研究基准上的实验表明，该方法能获得显著更多的成功轨迹，并持续优于强基线，尤其在困难样本上表现突出。

Conclusion: 零阶优化能够有效帮助LLM智能体突破能力边界，为无需额外标注的智能体自我进化提供了新路径。

Abstract: Self-evolving methods improve the capabilities of LLM agents by sampling trajectories from the underlying LLMs and learning from these trajectories. However, these methods struggle to learn beyond the inherent capability boundary of the agents, since the agents cannot sample correct trajectories on difficult examples for further improvements. In this paper, we propose a zeroth-order self-evolution framework that enables agents to learn beyond their capability boundary by perturbing LLM parameters to adapt to difficult examples without any trajectory annotations. Specifically, we perturb LoRA parameters of LLMs, run the agent, compute the losses under the perturbed and original parameters, and use the loss difference to estimate gradients and further update the LoRA parameters. We sample trajectories using the updated LLMs for supervised fine-tuning to break through the capability boundary of the agents, forming a closed self-evolution loop. We introduce a parallel perturbation inference mechanism and an adaptive lookup mechanism to reduce time consumption in zeroth-order optimization, with an answer perplexity loss that provides smooth and stable zeroth-order loss values. Experiments on multiple deep research benchmarks show that our method obtains substantially more successful trajectories and consistently outperforms strong baselines, especially on difficult examples. The code and released artifacts are available at https://github.com/hidk1911/ZOForLLMAgents.

</details>


### [114] [Test-Time Augmentation for LLMs: When Input Diversity Beats Output Diversity at Matched Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09351)
*Nikita Kozodoi,Zainab Afolabi,Jack Butler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了测试时增强（TTA）策略，将自一致性（self-consistency）扩展为不仅扰动输出推理路径，还扰动输入，在匹配计算量的条件下对比输入侧多样性与输出侧多样性在将推理算力转化为准确率方面的效率。结果显示语义改写（semantic rephrasing）在六个数据集上取得显著准确率提升，并且在成本效益上优于自一致性，每美元准确率约为后者的1.8倍。TTA对中档模型尤其有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展能提升LLM准确率但成倍增加推理成本，因此每单位计算量带来的准确率提升成为部署中的关键指标。自一致性仅在输出侧采样多条推理路径，作者想知道在输入侧引入多样性（即TTA）是否比仅在输出侧多样性更能高效地将计算转化为准确率。

Method: 作者进行了系统的匹配计算量对比实验：评估三种简单输入侧策略——语义改写、词汇扰动、视觉变换，覆盖六大数据集（通用和多语言知识、数学推理、多模态问答、情感分类），与思维链提示和自一致性进行对比。分析了增强数量、多模态策略和基础模型规模的影响。

Result: 语义改写带来了一致的、统计显著的准确率提升，在成本效益上帕累托支配自一致性，每美元准确率约为自一致性的1.8倍，在六个任务中的五个上表现更优。TTA对中档模型最具成本效益，尤其是在更强模型不可用或过于昂贵的情况下。

Conclusion: 对于当前的中档LLM，在输入侧改变输入比仅改变推理路径能更高效地将推理计算转化为准确率。TTA实现已开源。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves LLM accuracy but multiplies inference cost, making the accuracy gained per unit of compute the metric that matters in deployment. Self-consistency is one of the established approaches, which spends this budget entirely on the output side by sampling repeated reasoning paths. We study Test-Time Augmentation (TTA), which extends self-consistency by also perturbing the input, aggregating predictions across transformed versions of the input, and ask whether input-side diversity converts compute into accuracy more efficiently than output-side diversity. We perform a systematic, matched-compute comparison: we evaluate three simple input-side strategies (semantic rephrasing, lexical perturbations, and visual transformations) across six datasets covering general and multilingual knowledge, mathematical reasoning, multi-modal question answering, and sentiment classification, against chain-of-thought prompting and self-consistency. Semantic rephrasing delivers consistent and statistically significant accuracy gains while Pareto-dominating self-consistency on cost-effectiveness, delivering roughly 1.8X more accuracy per dollar and outperforming it on five of six tasks. We further analyze the number of augmentations, multi-modal strategies, and base model scaling, finding that TTA is most cost-effective for mid-tier models where a stronger model is unavailable or too expensive. Our findings indicate that for current mid-tier LLMs, varying the input converts inference compute into accuracy more efficiently than varying the reasoning path alone. The TTA implementation is available at https://github.com/aws-samples/sample-genai-reflection-for-bedrock.

</details>


### [115] [Learning to Modulate, Not to Cycle: Soft Actor---Critic Recovers Inverter-Style Heat-Pump Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09453)
*Faizan Ahmed,Aniket Dixit,James Brusey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究住宅热泵压缩机磨损问题，提出在强化学习控制奖励中加入压缩机磨损项，并对比SAC和PPO两种算法在同一MDP上的表现。SAC学会持续调制策略（实现零启停），PPO则收敛到bang-bang控制导致更多循环。SAC策略在BOPTEST仿真器上最多降低90.7%热不适，成本仅增加11.5%，且完全消除基线循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅热泵中启停循环是压缩机磨损的主要原因，但现有建筑强化学习控制器通常只优化能源成本和热舒适，忽略了策略导致的循环次数。

Method: 在控制奖励中加入平坦化的压缩机磨损项，使用SAC和PPO在BOPTEST bestest水循环热泵案例的相同马尔可夫决策过程上进行训练，并对比其行为差异。

Result: SAC学会了持续调制的策略（类似变频驱动热泵的工作原理），实现每天零启动；PPO则收敛到bang-bang控制，循环次数比基线还多。SAC在BOPTEST仿真器上热不适最多降低90.7%，成本增加11.5%，同时消除所有基线循环。

Conclusion: RL控制器的循环行为高度依赖所选算法，SAC更适用于需要考虑压缩机磨损的热泵控制场景，而PPO的bang-bang行为会加剧磨损，需要额外措施加以约束。

Abstract: On--off cycling is the main cause of compressor wear in residential heat pumps, yet reinforcement learning (RL) controllers for buildings typically optimise only energy cost and thermal comfort, ignoring how much the learned policy cycles. We add a levelised compressor-wear term to the control reward and study how the resulting behaviour depends on the RL algorithm. Training Soft Actor---Critic (SAC) and Proximal Policy Optimisation (PPO) on an identical Markov decision process for the BOPTEST bestest hydronic heat pump case, we find that SAC learns a continuous modulation policy that keeps the compressor permanently engaged---the operating principle of an inverter-driven heat pump---achieving zero start-ups per day, whereas PPO collapses to bang-bang control that cycles more than the baseline. On the BOPTEST emulator the SAC policy cuts thermal discomfort by up to 90.7% for an 11.5% cost increase, while eliminating all baseline cycling.

</details>


### [116] [Rethinking Factor Sharing in Federated LoRA: A Rank-Aware Adaptive Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09742)
*Xinyi Xu,Bingnan Xiao,Shuang Qin,Gang Feng,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedAS-LoRA，一种在联邦学习中自适应选择LoRA因子共享策略的方法。通过最小二乘代理分析，揭示Share-A/Local-B与Share-B/Local-A两种策略分别需要共同的输入侧或输出侧秩-r子空间，导致不同的投影残差，应选择总残差较小的策略。基于此，设计RSS指标在训练前利用冻结骨干模型评估共享子空间充分性，实现自适应因子共享选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习中使用LoRA微调大语言模型时，LoRA的两个低秩因子A和B扮演不对称角色，但现有工作未系统研究哪个因子应被跨客户端共享、哪个应保持客户端特有。理解两种共享策略的本质差异及其适用条件，对提升联邦微调性能至关重要。

Method: 作者首先通过最小二乘代理问题从理论上分析两种共享策略：Share-A/Local-B要求客户端局部更新矩阵共享共同的秩-r输入侧子空间，而Share-B/Local-A要求共享共同的秩-r输出侧子空间。两种策略产生不同的投影残差，最优策略是聚合残差较小的那一个。基于此，提出FedAS-LoRA，在训练前利用RSS（Rank-Aware Shared-Subspace Sufficiency）指标，从冻结的LLM骨干中提取表示，评估共享的秩-r输入子空间对本地数据分布的充分性，从而自适应地选择最优的共享侧。

Result: 实验覆盖不同任务、数据分布、LoRA秩和客户端参与设置，验证了RSS指标的有效性以及FedAS-LoRA的优越性能。

Conclusion: LoRA因子共享策略的选择不应固定，而应根据数据分布自适应决定。通过理论分析揭示两种策略的投影残差差异，并利用RSS指标在训练前高效选择最优策略，FedAS-LoRA在联邦学习场景中显著提升了LoRA微调的性能。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) represents large language model (LLM) updates with two compact matrix factors, i.e., $A$ and $B$, providing an efficient way to fine-tune large models in federated learning paradigm. Inspired by the asymmetric roles of the LoRA factors, we study whether $A$ should be shared across clients while $B$ remains client-specific (Share-A/Local-B), or whether $B$ should instead be shared while $A$ remains client-specific (Share-B/Local-A). With a least-squares surrogate, we reveal that Share-A/Local-B requires the client-specific LoRA update matrices to use a common rank-$r$ input-side space, whereas Share-B/Local-A requires a common rank-$r$ output-side space. The two strategies therefore incur different projection residuals, indicating that the preferred strategy is the one with the smaller aggregate residual across clients. With this insight, we propose Federated Adaptive Factor Sharing Low-Rank Adaptation (FedAS-LoRA), which selects the sharing side before training to enhance fine-tuning performance. To enable adaptive factor selection before training, we design a Rank-Aware Shared-Subspace Sufficiency (RSS) metric, which effectively assesses whether a shared rank-$r$ input subspace is sufficient for the local data distributions using representations extracted from a frozen LLM backbone. Experiments across different tasks, data distributions, LoRA ranks, and participation settings confirm the effectiveness of RSS and the superior performance of FedAS-LoRA.

</details>


### [117] [Parameter Exploration for RLVR via Variational Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09805)
*Vatsal Venkatkrishna,Nico Daheim,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出参数空间探索方法族3PO（Perturbed Parameter Policy Optimization），通过从后验分布中采样不同策略生成rollout，作为动作空间探索（如温度调节）的互补控制手段，在数学推理和代码生成任务上一致优于GRPO基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM强化学习方法主要依赖动作空间探索（如温度缩放），但这类方法无法重排token顺序，只能影响输出分布的方差，导致探索受限、训练发散或停滞。需要一种新的探索维度来补充和完善现有方法。

Method: 提出3PO方法族，通过从参数后验分布中采样不同策略来生成rollout。该家族包含不同的采样策略（多样性程度不同）和不同的rollout分组策略（用于奖励估计），以此控制参数空间的探索程度。

Result: 在OLMo-3-1025-7B和Qwen2.5-Math-7B上的数学推理和代码生成任务中，3PO方法一致性地提升了平均下游性能，且计算成本（FLOPs）与GRPO几乎相同。此外，使用多个参数采样能显著减少零优势分组和格式错误rollout的出现。

Conclusion: 参数空间探索能够有效改善LLM的强化学习过程，作为动作空间探索的补充维度，对提升训练稳定性和下游性能具有显著价值。

Abstract: Exploration has been a focus of reinforcement learning research for a long time. Recently, there has been growing evidence that it is also an important ingredient in LLM reinforcement learning recipes that can significantly impact downstream performance. Many existing methods control exploration in the action-space, for example, using temperature scaling. However, these methods cannot reorder tokens but only influence the variance in the output distribution. This limits exploration and can lead to divergence or stalled training. Here, we investigate parameter-space exploration, where rollouts are generated by sampling different policies from a posterior that may each explore different rollouts. Sampling less or more diverse policies is then a complementary control lever over exploration. We introduce a family of methods called Perturbed Parameter Policy Optimization (3PO) which use different sampling strategies and different rollout grouping for reward estimation. Experiments on OLMo-3-1025-7B and Qwen2.5-Math-7B across mathematical reasoning and code generation tasks show that these approaches consistently improve average downstream performance over standard GRPO at a near-identical FLOPs cost. Moreover, using multiple parameter samples consistently produces fewer zero-advantage groups and malformed or incorrect rollouts during training than GRPO and action-space baselines. Overall, our work presents evidence that parameter-space exploration can improve reinforcement learning for LLMs.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Defect-Controlled Multiferroicity via Stacking Control in Nonmagnetic van der Waals Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07666)
*Bumseop Kim,Sayed Ali Akbar Ghorashi,Andrew M. Rappe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种将空位局域磁性直接耦合到非磁性二维材料界面滑动铁电性的通用范式，以双层六方氮化硼（hBN）为原型系统，通过第一性原理计算证明单个空位可作为局部配位传感器打破滑动极性伙伴的简并，并发现层间空位对的磁性构型由宿主亚晶格完全决定，外加垂直电场可调节补偿性奈尔序参量的振幅，从而实现基于堆叠控制的亚晶格依赖多铁性功能工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非磁性范德华（vdWs）双层材料中实现多铁性功能，目前缺乏将局域磁性直接与滑动铁电性耦合的通用机制。作者旨在建立一种无需磁性元素即可通过缺陷工程实现磁电耦合的通用范式，突破传统多铁性材料对磁性成分的依赖。

Method: 以双层六方氮化硼为原型宿主系统，采用第一性原理计算，系统研究：(1) 单个空位作为局部配位传感器，基于缺陷中心极化偏移来消除滑动极性伙伴之间的简并；(2) 层间空位对的缺陷选择性，考察宿主亚晶格对层间交换相互作用的决定作用；(3) 外加垂直电场对极性配位的选择及对补偿性奈尔序参量的振幅调制。

Result: 研究发现：单个空位能够打破滑动极性状态的简并；层间空位对中宿主亚晶格完全决定层间交换，可稳定铁磁或反铁磁（具体为铁磁/亚铁磁与反铁磁）构型；施加垂直电场可选择极性配位并驱动补偿性奈尔序参量的振幅调制。结果建立了一种基于堆叠控制的、亚晶格依赖的多铁性功能工程方法。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了空位局域磁性-滑动铁电性耦合的一般范式，通过堆叠控制即可在广泛的非磁性二维异质结构类中实现稳健的、亚晶格依赖的多铁性功能工程，为无磁性元素的多铁性材料设计开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We present a general paradigm that directly couples vacancy-localized magnetism to interfacial sliding ferroelectricity in nonmagnetic van der Waals (vdWs) bilayers. Utilizing bilayer hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) as a prototypical vdWs host system, we use first-principles calculations to show that a single vacancy acts as a local registry sensor, lifting the degeneracy between polar sliding partners via a defect-centered polarization offset. For interlayer vacancy pairs, we discover a defect selectivity where the hosting sublattice fully dictates the interlayer exchange, stabilizing either ferrimagnetic or antiferromagnetic configurations. Applying an out-of-plane electric field selects the polar registry and drives an amplitude modulation of the compensated Néel order parameter. These findings establish a robust, sublattice-dependent engineering of multiferroic functionality via stacking control across a wide class of nonmagnetic 2D heterostructures.

</details>


### [119] [Understanding the Oxygen Reduction Reaction and Oxygen Evolution Reaction in Metal Intercalated Biphenylene Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07782)
*Henri G. Mendonça,Pedro H. Souza,Walter Orellana,Roberto H. Miwa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 对金属封装联苯烯双层（B/M/B，M为Ti、V、Cr、Mn、Fe、Co、Ni、Cu、Nb、Ru、W、Os、Pt）进行从头计算研究，评估其ORR和OER催化性能。Cu、Pt、Ru、Mn的ORR过电位最低（0.42-0.56 V），Fe的OER过电位最低（0.44 V）。发现金属d轨道电荷布居与活性（尤其OER）呈最清晰的火山型关联，可作为预测催化行为的有效电子描述符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找高效且非贵金属的ORR/OER催化剂是能源电催化的关键挑战。联苯烯作为一种碳同素异形体，其独特的C468方形碳位有望提供不同于石墨烯的催化活性位点。通过金属插层封装可以调控碳框架的电子结构，但这类体系的催化趋势及其电子结构来源尚不明确，需要系统研究。

Method: 采用从头计算（ab initio）方法，对13种金属封装联苯烯双层结构B/M/B进行系统性研究。使用计算氢电极（CHE）方法评估反应能量学；通过分析金属d带中心、C468位p_z带中心及轨道电荷布群来理解催化趋势与电子结构的关系。

Result: 多数体系中金属位于联苯烯的方形碳位（C468）。Cu、Pt、Ru、Mn分别表现出最低的ORR过电位0.42、0.44、0.50、0.56 V；Fe的OER过电位最低为0.44 V。带中心与过电位之间没有简单的多项式依赖关系，但指出了最优催化剂对应的有利电子范围。金属d轨道电荷布群与活性（尤其是OER）呈现最清晰的火山型关联，其中B/Fe/B为最佳OER催化剂，B/Mn/B最接近ORR最优。碳位p轨道电荷布群也能捕捉主要趋势，但相关性较弱。

Conclusion: 简单的电子结构描述符（尤其是金属d轨道电荷布群）可以有效预测金属封装联苯烯双层中的催化行为，为在碳框架本身驱动反应性的体系中寻找高效催化剂提供了理论指导方向。

Abstract: We conducted an {\it ab initio} study of the oxygen reduction reaction (ORR) and oxygen evolution reaction (OER) in metal-encapsulated biphenylene bilayers, B/M/B, with M = Ti, V, Cr, Mn, Fe, Co, Ni, Cu, Nb, Ru, W, Os and Pt. In most systems, the intercalated metal sits at the square carbon sites (C$^{468}$) of the biphenylene lattice. Using a computational hydrogen electrode approach, we evaluated the reaction energetics at these active sites. Several B/M/B systems show competitive ORR and OER performance. Among the investigated systems, Cu, Pt, Ru, and Mn exhibit the lowest ORR overpotentials of 0.42, 0.44, 0.50, and 0.56 V, respectively, while Fe is identified as the most active catalyst for OER with an overpotential of 0.44 V. To understand the catalytic trends, we looked at the electronic structure through the metal $d-$band centers, the C$^{468}$ $p_z-$band centers, and the corresponding orbital charge populations. The band centers did not give a simple polynomial dependence on the overpotentials, though they did point to favorable electronic ranges for the best catalysts. The $d-$orbital charge population of the encapsulated metal, however, correlated most clearly with activity-especially for OER-yielding volcano-type plots. From these, B/Fe/B emerges as the best OER catalyst, while B/Mn/B lies closest to the ORR optimum. The $p-$orbital population at the active carbon site also captures the main trends, albeit less strongly. Overall, these results show that straightforward electronic descriptors can predict catalytic behavior in metal-encapsulated biphenylene bilayers and guide the search for efficient catalysts where the carbon framework itself drives the reactivity.

</details>


### [120] [Spin selective non-van der Waal electride nature in manganese under ambient pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07856)
*Shishir Timilsena,Dinesh Thapa,David James Faller,Prabesh Adhikari,Nicholas Dimakis,Svetlana Kilina*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理量子力学计算，系统地研究了环境压力下元素锰（Mn）的三种晶相（α-Mn、β-Mn、hex-Mn）的电化物特性，首次确认了元素Mn在常压下表现出自旋选择性电化物特征，即电子在非核间隙区域作为阴离子存在，并伴随显著的电子转移和费米面附近的间隙电子态贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电化物是一类电子作为阴离子的非寻常离子材料，但元素态金属锰（Mn）是否表现出电化物行为此前尚未被系统研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，并探索Mn中间隙电子态在非常规电子和磁性功能中的潜在应用。

Method: 采用第一性原理量子力学计算方法，对Mn在常压（0 GPa）下的三种晶相（立方α-Mn，空间群I-43m；立方β-Mn，空间群P4132；六方hex-Mn，空间群P63/mmc）进行结构、电子、磁性和电化物特性分析。具体手段包括自旋选择电子局域函数（ELF）计算、Bader电荷分析以及电子态密度（DOS）分析。

Result: 三种Mn相均表现出显著的间隙电子特征，ELF呈自旋选择性。Bader电荷分析表明从Mn框架到间隙阴离子电子区域发生了明显的电子转移，有效电荷转移量在α-Mn、β-Mn和hex-Mn中分别约为-1.645e、-1.477e和-1.083e。DOS分析显示间隙阴离子电子相关态在E_F附近有有限贡献，并与Mn衍生态共存，表明其直接参与低能电子结构。

Conclusion: 通过ELF、有效电荷转移和费米面处IAE电子布居三种一致证据，确认元素Mn是常压下的非范德华电化物体系，且具有自旋选择性特征。这是首次系统性地在元素态Mn中识别出该特性，为利用其间隙电子态开发非常规电子与磁性功能提供了可能。

Abstract: Electrides are an unusual class of ionic materials in which electrons localized in non-nuclear, interstitial regions act as anions within the crystal lattice. Here, we employ first-principles quantum mechanical calculations to investigate the structural, electronic, magnetic, and electride characteristics of elemental manganese (Mn) at an ambient pressure (0 GPa), focusing on its three crystalline phases: cubic ($α$)-Mn ($I\bar{4}3m,no.217$), cubic ($β$)-Mn ($P4_132, no.213$), and hexagonal ($hex$)-Mn ($P6_3/mmc, no.194$). Our calculations reveal pronounced interstitial-electron character in all three phases, accompanied by spin-selective electron localization function (ELF), establishing elemental Mn as a non-van der Waals electride system. Bader charge analysis indicates substantial electron redistribution from the Mn host framework toward the interstitial anionic-electron (IAE) regions, with an effective charge transfer of approximately $-1.645e$, $-1.477e$, and $-1.083e$ per interstitial basin in $α$-Mn, $β$-Mn, and $hex$-Mn, respectively. The electride character is further supported by the electronic density of states, where the IAE-associated states exhibit finite contributions near the Fermi level ($E_F$) and coexist with Mn-derived states, demonstrating their direct participation in the low-energy electronic structure. The combined electron localization function (ELF), effective charge transfer, and electron population due to IAE at $E_F$ therefore provide consistent evidence for interstitial anionic electrons in elemental Mn. To the best of our knowledge, this work provides the first systematic identification of spin-selective electride character in elemental Mn at ambient pressure, highlighting the possibility of exploiting its interstitial-electron states for unconventional electronic and magnetic functionalities.

</details>


### [121] [Metastability limit of pristine 2D noble metals with high-energy facet: dominance of the Bell-Evans-Polanyi principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07884)
*Xiaoliang Zhong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过密度泛函理论预测，Rh、Pd、Ag和Ir的超薄(110)片层会通过晶格收缩转化为(100)结构，并揭示了转化势垒与能量差之间的线性关系，验证了Bell-Evans-Polanyi原理的主导作用，同时该原理也能描述这些金属发生结构转变的临界厚度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超薄金属片层中(110)与(100)晶面之间的结构稳定性关系及相变行为，理解其转变机制和临界条件。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论(DFT)计算，对Rh、Pd、Ag和Ir的超薄(110)片层进行结构优化与过渡态分析，系统考察晶格收缩驱动的结构转变，并检验BEP原理在转变势垒与能量差关系中的适用性。

Result: 发现四种金属的超薄(110)片层倾向通过晶格收缩转变为(100)结构；转变势垒与(110)和(100)片层之间的能量差近似呈线性关系，证实BEP原理主导该转变过程；BEP原理还可用于描述这些金属结构自发转变的临界厚度。

Conclusion: BEP原理可有效描述超薄金属片层中(110)→(100)的结构转变行为，这一普适规律为理解和预测纳米尺度金属结构相变提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Using density functional theory, we predict that ultrathin (110) sheets of Rh, Pd, Ag, and Ir tend to transform into their (100) counterparts via lattice contraction. An approximately linear relationship between the transformation barrier and the energy difference between (110) and (100) sheets is revealed, demonstrating that the Bell-Evans-Polanyi (BEP) principle dominates. Furthermore, the critical thicknesses for these metals are also described by the BEP principle: below these thicknesses, the (110) sheets are no longer metastable and undergo spontaneous structural transformation.

</details>


### [122] [Predicting large-supercell defect formation energies from machine-learning charge density models trained on small supercells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07997)
*Junjie Zhou,Menglin Huang,Shiyou Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习电荷密度（MLCD）的方法来预测缺陷形成能，通过集成不同尺寸的小超胞进行训练，仅用96个超胞（16-96个原子）即可在360原子超胞上实现低于0.05 eV的缺陷形成能预测误差，相比传统机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）在同一数据集上的误差（超过1 eV）具有显著的跨尺寸迁移优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理缺陷计算常受限于超大超胞的计算成本（用于抑制镜像相互作用），而训练缺陷机器学习原子间势通常需要数千个结构和数周的数据生成时间，数据效率极低。因此需要一种更高效、数据需求更少的替代方案来预测缺陷形成能。

Method: 提出MLCD路线：以电荷密度作为机器学习预测目标（而非直接拟合能量/力）。训练集方面，集成不同尺寸的小超胞（16-96个原子共96个），基于空间电荷密度分析来优化不同尺寸超胞的分配比例，以增强跨尺寸外推能力。最终用学习到的电荷密度预测缺陷形成能。

Result: 仅用96个超胞（含16-96个原子）作为训练数据，MLCD即可准确预测360原子超胞中四种本征缺陷的形成能，各缺陷的平均绝对误差低于0.05 eV。相比之下，基于相同数据集训练的MLIPs误差可超过1 eV，差距达20倍以上。

Conclusion: 电荷密度学习相比直接的能量-力拟合具有更稳健的跨尺寸迁移能力；混合尺寸的数据设计策略可以大幅降低缺陷预测的计算成本，为高效、高精度的缺陷计算提供了新思路。

Abstract: First-principles defect calculations are often limited by the cost of the large supercells required to suppress image interactions. Machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) provide another alternative, but training defect MLIPs typically requires thousands of structures and weeks of data generation. Since charge density is the key to density-functional-theory (DFT), we propose a machine-learning charge density (MLCD) route for predicting defect formation energies with higher data efficiency. We optimize the training set by integrating small supercells of varying sizes for better extrapolation, allocating their proportions based on spatial charge-density analysis. With only 96 supercells containing 16--96 atoms as the dataset, MLCD accurately predicts the formation energies of four intrinsic defects in 360-atom supercells, with defect-wise mean absolute error below 0.05 eV. In contrast, MLIPs trained on the same dataset can err by more than 1 eV. These results show that charge-density learning enables more robust cross-size transfer than direct energy-force fitting and that mixed-size data design can substantially reduce the cost of defect prediction.

</details>


### [123] [Universality of PVDF-Li$^+$ Ion Interface Chemistry in Lead-free Perovskite Energy Storage Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08295)
*Arun Kumar,Ayush Kumar Pandey,Ankur Yadav,Vishnu Saraswat,Shiladitya Sengupta,Abhishek Tewari,Monojit Bag*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文发现无铅钙钛矿超级电容器中，电极粘合剂（PVDF）并非惰性结构组分，而是定义了最优电解质组成。通过实验和MACE机器学习势模拟，揭示了"界面平衡法则"（λ+θ=1），表明粘合剂负载量是调控聚合物-电解质界面的主动设计参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将电极粘合剂视为惰性结构组分，忽略了其对电化学性能的主动影响。作者旨在揭示粘合剂在无铅钙钛矿超级电容器中的功能角色，特别是在决定最优电解质组成方面的作用机制。

Method: 采用因子实验矩阵（PVDF负载量×LiTFSI浓度）系统研究CsSnCl3电极的电容器最优组成；使用预训练的MACE机器学习原子间势进行分子模拟，分析PVDF在CsSnCl3表面的吸附构型、锂离子界面调控行为及聚合物覆盖率的影响；并通过MASnCl3体系验证规律的普适性。

Result: 电容最优值随粘合剂含量沿单一线性关系系统移动，由界面平衡法则（λ+θ=1）描述；该规律在MASnCl3中同样成立。模拟表明PVDF在CsSnCl3表面呈平面构型，同时与阳离子和阴离子位点相互作用，均匀化锂吸附能、引入氟介导配位并将锂限制在二维界面区域；界面锂容纳能力有限，超出后发生面外聚集。

Conclusion: 粘合剂负载量应被视为聚合物调控储能界面的主动设计参数。界面平衡法则提供了一个宏观描述符，量化了聚合物介导的锂稳定化与有限容纳空间之间的平衡，为优化无铅钙钛矿超级电容器提供了新的设计准则。

Abstract: Electrode binders are conventionally regarded as inert structural components. Here, we show that in lead-free perovskite supercapacitors, the binder defines the optimal electrolyte composition. Across a factorial matrix of poly(vinylidene fluoride) (PVDF) loadings and LiTFSI concentrations in CsSnCl$_3$ electrodes, the capacitance optimum shifts systematically with binder content along a single linear relationship, described by the Interfacial Balance Rule ($λ+θ=1$), where $λ$ and $θ$ are the normalized lithium-supply and polymer contributions at the optimized interfacial state. The same relationship holds for hybrid MASnCl$_3$, showing that the optimum is governed by the polymer-electrolyte interface rather than the perovskite lattice chemistry. Simulations using a pre-trained MACE machine-learned interatomic potential show that PVDF adopts a planar configuration on CsSnCl$_3$ and simultaneously interacts with cationic and anionic sites. This configuration homogenizes lithium adsorption energetics, introduces fluorine-mediated coordination, and confines lithium to a two-dimensional interfacial region while preserving lateral mobility. Tuning polymer coverage through surface density and chain length reveals a finite interfacial lithium accommodation capacity that marks the onset of out-of-plane aggregation. The Interfacial Balance Rule provides a macroscopic descriptor of this finite interfacial resource, balancing polymer-mediated lithium stabilization against limited accommodation space. Binder loading is therefore an active design parameter for polymer-regulated energy-storage interfaces.

</details>


### [124] [Local coordination and migration-network topology shape Li-ion transport and delithiation in the low-energy $\varepsilon$-LiMnO$_2$ polymorph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08000)
*Fukuan Wang,Busheng Wang,Yong Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文以低能epsilon-LiMnO2多晶型为模型，通过键价位能/路径分析与第一性原理计算，研究了岩盐衍生氧化物正极中0-TM (无面共享过渡金属) 局部锂迁移环境的更高壳层配位和四面体连接性对锂迁移和脱锂行为的影响。结果显示epsilon相与尖晶石相尽管具有相同的四面体类型分数，但0-TM基序分别形成准一维链和三维网络，导致迁移势垒和电压响应存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，岩盐衍生氧化物正极中锂迁移环境仅通过O4四面体中间体面共享过渡金属（TM）邻居数量（0-TM、1-TM等）来分类。但迁移和脱锂可能还取决于更高壳层配位和四面体连接性，这一因素先前未被系统研究，需要通过模型体系加以阐明。

Method: 采用键价位能（bond-valence site-energy）和键价路径（bond-valence pathway）分析，结合第一性原理计算（CI-NEB和从头算分子动力学AIMD），对四种LiMnO2多晶型（包括epsilon相和尖晶石Li2Mn2O4）进行迁移图、四面体统计、迁移势垒、活化能、方向分辨均方位移以及脱锂电压曲线的系统比较。

Result: epsilon相与尖晶石相具有相同的四面体类型分数，但0-TM基序分别为准一维链和三维网络。CI-NEB显示epsilon相势垒为0.35-0.36 eV，低于尖晶石的0.41-0.53 eV，差异可能与次近邻角共享壳层有关。AIMD得到0.32 eV的表观活化能，方向分辨MSD显示锂沿c轴优先迁移（准一维扩散）。脱锂计算表明两相0-TM连接性和Li-Li间距差异与锂位演化及电压台阶相关。

Conclusion: 0-TM基序的空间连通性（而非仅局部TM配位）是决定锂迁移维度和势垒以及脱锂电压响应的关键因素。这一认识为设计具有可控迁移路径的低能亚稳态正极结构提供了新见解。

Abstract: In rocksalt-derived oxide cathodes, the local Li-migration environment around an O4 tetrahedral intermediate is commonly classified by the number of face-sharing transition-metal (TM) neighbors. In LiMnO2, the TM species is Mn, and 0-TM denotes the absence of face-sharing Mn neighbors. However, migration and delithiation may also depend on higher-shell coordination and tetrahedral connectivity. Using the recently reported low-energy epsilon-LiMnO2 polymorph as a model, we examine these factors through bond-valence site-energy and bond-valence pathway analyses combined with first-principles calculations. The resulting migration maps and tetrahedral statistics reveal distinct topologies and dimensionalities across four LiMnO2 polymorphs. Although the epsilon phase and the lithiated-spinel phase Li2Mn2O4 (hereafter spinel) have identical tetrahedral-type fractions, their 0-TM motifs form quasi-one-dimensional chains and a three-dimensional network, respectively. Climbing-image nudged elastic band calculations further distinguish the two structures: epsilon-phase barriers are 0.35-0.36 eV, compared with 0.41-0.53 eV in spinel, a difference that may be associated with distinct next-nearest corner-sharing shells. Ab initio molecular dynamics yields an apparent activation energy of 0.32 eV, while direction-resolved mean-squared displacements show preferential Li migration along c, supporting low-barrier quasi-one-dimensional diffusion. Delithiation calculations show that differences in 0-TM connectivity and Li-Li separation between the epsilon phase and spinel are associated with Li-site evolution and calculated voltage steps, suggesting that motif connectivity may influence voltage response. These results link the local environments and spatial connectivity of 0-TM motifs to Li migration and delithiation, providing insights into the design of metastable cathode structures.

</details>


### [125] [Denoising Diffusion Monte Carlo Electron Densities with Physically Informed Variance Stabilization: From Fourier Filters to 3D UNETs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08152)
*Kenneth O. Berard,Brenda Rubenstein,Jaron T. Krogel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了扩散蒙特卡洛（DMC）模拟中真实空间电子密度的去噪方法。通过利用DFT密度作为物理先验进行异方差到同方差的变换，系统比较了傅里叶变换、回归和3D UNET等多种去噪技术在碳金刚石、蓝磷和VO2等不同材料上的表现。结果表明，方差稳定化后的回归方法在所有材料的高/低噪声极限下均优于其他方法，可将DMC密度生成成本降低10-100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMC产生的电子密度包含大量统计噪声，限制了其在噪声敏感任务（如DFT泛函反演）中的应用。获得准确的电子密度对于分子和凝聚态体系的描述以及下一代密度泛函的发展至关重要，因此需要有效去除DMC密度中的噪声。

Method: 利用DFT密度作为物理先验，通过近似异方差到同方差（heteroscedastic to homoscedastic）变换来辅助去噪。系统比较了多种去噪技术：傅里叶变换、回归方法和3D UNETs，并使用信息论中的Jensen-Shannon散度作为去噪效果的评估指标。测试材料覆盖碳金刚石、蓝磷和VO2，以涵盖广泛的密度变化范围。

Result: 简单的扁平化机器学习模型和2D图像模型会引入线条伪影（line artifacts），且难以捕获完整的空间相关性。相反，使用方差稳定化后的回归方法在所有材料的高噪和低噪极限下均优于其他所有方法。最佳去噪器可将DMC密度生成模拟的所需成本降低10-100倍。

Conclusion: 方差稳定化结合回归方法是DMC电子密度去噪的最佳策略，能够显著降低计算成本，为噪声敏感任务如DFT泛函反演提供了可行的前进路径。

Abstract: Obtaining accurate electron densities is important for the fundamental description of molecular and condensed matter systems, as well as for the development of next-generation density functionals. Diffusion Monte Carlo (DMC), in particular, is known to produce benchmark-quality data; however, the predicted real-space electron densities contain substantial amounts of statistical noise. In this work, we study denoising approaches for DMC densities, judged on the basis of the information-theoretic Jensen-Shannon divergence. The denoising is facilitated by an approximate heteroscedastic to homoscedastic transformation leveraging the density functional theory density as a physical prior. We systematically compare a range of denoising techniques-including Fourier transform, regression, and 3D UNETs-on materials showing a wide range of density variations: carbon diamond, blue phosphorus, and rutile VO2. Our results indicate that simple flattened machine learning models and 2D image-based models introduce line artifacts and struggle to capture the full spatial correlation. In contrast, when using variance stabilization, regression methods outperform all others in both the high and low- noise limits across all materials considered. The best denoisers reduce the required cost of density-generating DMC simulations by 10-100x, providing a promising route forward for application in noise-sensitive tasks such as DFT functional inversion.

</details>


### [126] [Thermo-mechanical Characterization of 2D hexagonal Boron Phosphide (h-BP)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08415)
*Zahabul Islam*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过DFT计算开发了二维单层六方磷化硼（h-BP）的经典分子动力学势函数，并模拟研究其在300–900 K温度下沿zigzag和armchair方向的拉伸力学行为，发现显著的各向异性、温度软化效应以及Stone-Wales和空位缺陷对力学性能的退化影响。


<details>
