  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: h-BP被预测具有适中的带隙、高热稳定性和优异的载流子迁移率，是先进电子、传感和能源应用的有前景材料。然而其热力学性能缺乏系统的分子动力学研究，尤其是温度效应和缺陷影响下的力学行为尚未被深入探索，因此需要开发可靠的MD势函数并建立其力学性能的全面理解，以支撑其在极端环境中的应用。

Method: 首先利用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算来拟合开发针对h-BP的经典分子动力学（MD）势函数参数；随后将所得参数应用于MD模拟中，在300 K至900 K的温度范围内，沿zigzag和armchair两个方向对单层h-BP施加拉伸载荷以评估其力学行为；同时引入Stone-Wales缺陷和空位缺陷，研究结构缺陷对拉伸强度和失效应变的影响。

Result: 结果表明：（1）h-BP在所有温度下均表现出明显的力学各向异性，zigzag方向的拉伸强度和弹性模量均高于armchair方向；（2）温度升高导致拉伸强度和刚度下降，归因于热软化和原子振动加剧；（3）Stone-Wales缺陷和空位缺陷均会降低拉伸强度和失效应变，其中双原子空位缺陷由于局部应力集中和过早裂纹萌生造成的力学退化最为显著。

Conclusion: 该研究系统揭示了h-BP在温度和缺陷影响下的力学行为规律，为其在极端环境中的机械稳定性研究提供了基础，并为后续h-BP器件的设计与可靠性评估奠定理论依据。

Abstract: This study explores the thermo-mechanical properties of a two-dimensional (2D) monolayer hexagonal boron phosphide (h-BP). h-BP is predicted to possess a moderate band gap, high thermal stability, and excellent carrier mobility, making it suitable for advanced electronic, sensing, and energy applications. A classical molecular dynamics (MD) potential for h-BP was developed using density functional theory (DFT) calculations. The derived parameters were implemented in MD simulations to evaluate mechanical behavior under tensile loading along both zigzag and armchair directions at varying temperatures (300 K to 900 K). The results reveal significant anisotropy in mechanical performance, with higher tensile strength and elastic modulus in the zigzag direction across all temperatures. Increasing temperature reduces both tensile strength and stiffness due to thermal softening and increased atomic vibrations. The influence of structural defects was also investigated, revealing that Stone-Wales and vacancy defects reduce the tensile strength and failure strain of h-BP, with the two-atom vacancy producing the most pronounced mechanical degradation due to localized stress concentration and premature crack initiation. These findings provide a foundation for future research on the mechanical stability of h-BP in extreme environments.

</details>


### [127] [Unified open-boundary electrostatics in real-space density functional theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08474)
*Rajat Kumar,David Codony,Phanish Suryanarayana,Abhiraj Sharma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于实空间密度泛函理论的静电公式，用于统一处理孤立体系和部分周期性体系的开边界静电问题，包括沿开放（有限）方向施加均匀电场的情况。该框架通过局部静电能量泛函的平稳性得到泊松方程，并在SPARC电子结构代码中实现，验证了其精度和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DFT实现对于孤立体系和部分周期性体系的开边界静电处理缺乏系统且统一的框架，尤其在施加均匀电场时，且没有现有实现能提供应力张量。这限制了相关材料性质（如极化率、压电系数）的精确计算。

Method: 1) 构建局部静电能量泛函，其平稳性条件给出泊松方程，在周期方向施加周期性边界条件，在有限方向施加Dirichlet边界条件；2) 利用Green函数方法推导由体系总电荷密度决定的Dirichlet边界值的解析表达式；3) 推导能量、原子受力和应力张量的表达式；4) 在SPARC大规模并行实空间电子结构代码中实现这些表达式。

Result: 通过代表性例子验证了框架的精度和效率：计算量随真空尺寸呈指数收敛；与成熟平面波代码相比，在同等精度下所需真空区域显著更少；由于现有实现均无法提供此类体系的应力，应力张量通过与能量数值导数对比验证，结果吻合良好；计算静态极化率和压电系数与文献报道值取得很好的一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个系统统一的开边界静电处理框架，不仅填补了施加电场时部分周期性体系静电处理的空白，还首次提供了此类体系的应力张量计算能力，使材料极化率和压电系数的计算在显著降低的计算成本下达到高精度。

Abstract: We present an electrostatic formulation in real-space density functional theory that provides a systematic and unified treatment of the open-boundary electrostatics of isolated and partially periodic systems, including in the presence of an applied uniform electric field along the open (finite) directions. Specifically, we formulate a local electrostatic energy functional whose stationarity yields the Poisson equation for the electrostatic potential, subject to periodic and Dirichlet boundary conditions along the periodic and open directions, respectively. Using a Green's function approach, we derive analytical expressions for the Dirichlet values arising from the total charge density of the system. We also derive the expressions for the energy, atomic forces, and stress tensor. We implement the resulting expressions within the large-scale parallel real-space SPARC electronic structure code. Using representative examples, we verify the accuracy and efficiency of the framework, demonstrating exponential convergence of the computed quantities with vacuum size and excellent agreement with established plane-wave codes while requiring significantly less vacuum at comparable accuracy. Since no existing implementation provides the stresses for such systems, we instead verify them against numerical derivatives of the energy, finding close agreement. Finally, we apply the framework to compute static polarizabilities and piezoelectric coefficients, obtaining very good agreement with values reported in the literature.

</details>


### [128] [Microscopic Origin of Spin Splitting in Altermagnetic CrSb Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08741)
*Dai Mingyang,Song Hongquan,Kang Zhuo,Xu Yuanji,Tian Fuyang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文通过第一性原理计算系统研究了不同表面取向的CrSb薄膜的电子结构，发现(2̄1̄10)取向薄膜保留显著的交错磁自旋劈裂，而(0001)和(101̄0)取向薄膜几乎自旋简并。研究揭示维度降低从根本上改变了交错磁自旋劈裂的微观起源：薄膜中的自旋劈裂需要长程跨晶胞共面Cr-Sb跳跃，而Sb-Sb跳跃仅提供额外贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属性CrSb因其巨大的自旋劈裂和高奈尔温度成为极具吸引力的交错磁自旋电子学材料，但块体和薄膜中不同的自旋劈裂行为的微观起源尚未解决，理解这一机制对于低维交错磁材料的应用至关重要。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，系统研究不同表面取向（(2̄1̄10)、(0001)和(101̄0)）的CrSb薄膜电子结构，分析各薄膜的自旋群对称性、能带结构及自旋劈裂特征，并通过解析不同的原子间跳跃路径（跨晶胞共面Cr-Sb跳跃和Sb-Sb跳跃）来阐明自旋劈裂的微观机制。

Result: 所有研究的薄膜均保持与交错磁性兼容的自旋群对称性，但电子结构显著不同：(2̄1̄10)薄膜保留明显的交错磁自旋劈裂，而(0001)和(101̄0)薄膜几乎自旋简并。维度降低改变了自旋劈裂的微观起源，薄膜中需要长程跨晶胞共面Cr-Sb跳跃，Sb-Sb跳跃仅起附加作用。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了理解低维交错磁性的微观机制，为低维交错磁材料中自旋劈裂的工程调控提供了普适性原则，对推动交错磁自旋电子学器件的开发具有重要意义。

Abstract: Altermagnets have recently emerged as promising materials for spintronic applications owing to their momentum-dependent spin splitting. Among them, metallic CrSb is particularly attractive owing to its giant spin splitting and high Néel temperature. However, the microscopic origin of the distinct spin-splitting behaviors in bulk and thin-film CrSb remains unresolved. Here, we systematically investigate the electronic structures of CrSb slabs with different surface orientations using first-principles calculations. Although all considered slabs preserve spin-group symmetries compatible with altermagnetism, they exhibit markedly different electronic structures: the (2$\bar{1}\bar{1}$0) slab retains pronounced altermagnetic spin splitting, whereas the (0001) and (10$\bar{1}$0) slabs display nearly spin-degenerate bands. We demonstrate that dimensional reduction fundamentally changes the microscopic origin of altermagnetic spin splitting. Unlike bulk CrSb, altermagnetic spin splitting in thin films requires long-range inter-unit-cell coplanar Cr--Sb hopping, while Sb--Sb hopping provides an additional contribution. The preservation or suppression of these hopping pathways explains the strong surface dependence of the spin splitting. Our findings establish a microscopic mechanism for understanding altermagnetism in reduced dimensions and provide a general principle for engineering spin splitting in low-dimensional altermagnetic materials.

</details>


### [129] [Distribution of Relaxation Times analysis of evolution of Oxygen Reduction Pathways for ionic conductor infiltration on MIEC cathode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08767)
*Rakhi Saha,Preethi Sudarsan,Manju Kumari,Hee Jung Park,Abdelkrim Mekki,Khalil Harrabi,Somaditya Sen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用弛豫时间分布（DRT）分析，将固体氧化物燃料电池（SOFC）阴极氧还原反应（ORR）中的各个子过程进行解卷积，揭示Sm0.2Ce0.8O2-delta（SDC）浸润对SrFe0.9Ti0.1O3-delta（STF）混合离子电子导体的改性机制。研究表明SDC浸润优先加速氧表面交换和活化过程，使极化电阻在800°C降至0.04 Ω·cm²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOFC在中温工况下阴极氧还原反应动力学迟缓是主要瓶颈，而ORR各子过程在传统等效电路中难以区分，阻碍了对反应机理的深入理解和电极的设计优化。

Method: 在混合离子电子导体STF上浸润离子型SDC以调控ORR子过程，并采用弛豫时间分布（DRT）分析对阻抗谱数据进行解卷积，区分氧表面交换、活化及电荷转移等不同频率域的过程，结合温度变化和表面重构探究其动力学规律。

Result: SDC浸润优先加速氧表面交换和活化过程（低频过程），而非高频电荷转移过程；各子过程得到系统性重新分布，800°C下总极化电阻RP显著降低至0.04 Ω·cm²，同时建立了活性位点数量、温度与RP之间的联系。

Conclusion: DRT分析是解卷积复杂电极过程的强大工具，SDC浸润的STF可作为模型阴极系统，为深入理解ORR动力学及高性能SOFC空气电极的理性设计提供机制性见解。

Abstract: Solid oxide fuel cells (SOFCs) are promising electrochemical energy conversion devices; however, the sluggish cathodic oxygen reduction reaction (ORR) remains a major limitation for intermediate-temperature operation. ORR subprocesses can be modified by infiltrating ionic Sm0.2Ce0.8O2-delta (SDC) on mixed ionic electronic SrFe0.9Ti0.1O3-delta (STF). However, the processes are indistinguishable in most cases and poorly understood using conventional equivalent circuits. Distribution of Relaxation Times (DRT) analysis distinguishes these processes to identify the dynamics with temperature and surface reconstruction. Such analysis is being reported, revealing the connection between increase of active sites, temperature, and polarization resistance (RP). SDC infiltration preferentially accelerates oxygen surface exchange and activation processes over the relatively high frequency charge transfer process related to cathode surface at elevated temperatures for the infiltrated cells. RP was reduced substantially with the systematic redistribution of each process to as low as 0.04 ohm.cm2 at 800 degC. This work underlines the deconvolution of the processes using DRT as a tool, and SDC infiltrated STF as a model cathode system to provide a mechanistic insight of understanding ORR kinetics and design a rationale for developing high-performance SOFC air electrodes

</details>


### [130] [Light-Stabilized Metastable Electronic State in NiO with Enhanced Orbital Hybridization2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08842)
*Thomas C. Rossi,Fabio G. Santomauro,Lars Mewes,Janina Löffler,Dominik Kinschel,Giacomo Rossi,Mario Gutiérrez,Oliviero Cannelli,Boris V. Sorokin,Jochen Rittmann,Jakob Kel,James Budarz,Anna Wach,Adam H. Clark,Emiliano Dal Molin,Maged F. Bekheet,Albert Gili,Sebastian Praetz,Daniel Grötzsch,Delphine Cabaret,Renske M. van der Veen,Majed Chergui*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了紫外光激发NiO（镍氧化物）产生的长寿命亚稳态，该状态具有增强的Ni 3d-O 2p轨道杂化。通过Ni K-edge X射线吸收光谱（XAS）结合DFT+U+V第一性原理计算，证明这种状态源于光激发后电子关联屏蔽的动态变化，非单纯热效应所致，为调控关联氧化物光电性质提供了新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光控电子结构调控为优化光伏和光电化学材料提供新机遇，但此前对于光激发如何影响过渡金属氧化物的轨道杂化及电子关联的微观机制尚不清楚。理解这一机制有助于设计性能更优的光电材料。

Method: 使用Ni K-edge X射线吸收光谱表征连续和脉冲紫外激发下的未占据态密度变化；采用连续（低载流子密度约10^13 cm^-3）与脉冲（高载流子密度约10^20 cm^-3）激发对比以区分电子效应与热效应；结合第一性原理DFT+U+V计算解释光谱变化并归因于Ni 3d-O 2p杂化增强导致的Ni 4p态重分布。

Result: 脉冲激发下产生载流子密度约10^20 cm^-3、寿命约600皮秒的亚稳态，其中增强杂化与晶格加热共存；而连续紫外照射在极低载流子密度（约10^13 cm^-3）下也能稳定类似电子态且晶格加热可忽略，证明该状态的产生非热驱动。DFT+U+V计算表明光谱变化源于更强Ni 3d-O 2p杂化对未占据Ni 4p态的影响，归因于光激发后电子关联的动态屏蔽导致电荷密度重新分布。

Conclusion: 光激发诱导的电子关联动态屏蔽是调控关联氧化物轨道杂化的新机制，由于轨道杂化主导载流子输运和电荷转移能量，这一发现为动态调控关联氧化物和设计新型光电材料提供了新思路。

Abstract: Light-driven control of electronic structure in correlated metal oxides offers new opportunities for optimizing materials used in photovoltaic and photoelectrochemical technologies. We show that photoexcitation of NiO, a prototypical transparent semiconductor and hole-transport material, across its charge-transfer gap produces a long-lived metastable state with enhanced Ni 3d-O 2p orbital hybridization. We characterize this state using Ni K-edge X-ray absorption spectroscopy, which probes how structural and electronic changes affect the unoccupied p density of states during continuous and pulsed ultraviolet excitation. Under pulsed excitation, high carrier densities of approximately 10^20 per cubic centimeter generate a state with a lifetime of approximately 600 picoseconds, in which enhanced hybridization coexists with lattice heating. By contrast, continuous ultraviolet irradiation at much lower carrier densities of approximately 10^13 per cubic centimeter stabilizes a similar electronic state with negligible lattice heating, demonstrating that its formation is not solely thermally driven. First-principles DFT+U+V calculations attribute the spectral changes to stronger Ni 3d-O 2p hybridization, which alters the unoccupied Ni 4p states probed by dipole-allowed K-edge transitions. We attribute this change to the dynamic screening of on-site electronic correlations following photoexcitation, which redistributes the charge density. Because orbital hybridization governs carrier transport and charge-transfer energetics, our results identify photoinduced screening as a mechanism for dynamically tuning correlated oxides and suggest new design principles for optoelectronic materials.

</details>


### [131] [Anharmonic dephasing in the electron-phonon interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09039)
*Mingran Kong,Bartomeu Monserrat*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文推导了包含非谐三声子相互作用（导致声子退相和有限声子寿命）的电子-声子耦合散射率表达式，并给出了可无缝集成到现有EPC和PPC计算流程中的第一性原理实现方案，在多种材料中计算了包含声子退相的电子-声子散射率，揭示了非谐声子影响电子-声子耦合的不同微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电子-声子耦合（EPC）计算通常假设声子具有无限寿命，但实际声子因非谐声子-声子相互作用而具有有限寿命。这一被忽视的效应可能显著影响电子-声子散射率的精确评估，因此需要建立包含声子退相效应的理论框架和计算方法。

Method: 作者从理论上推导了包含三声子非谐相互作用的电子-声子耦合散射率表达式（引入声子退相和有限声子寿命），并提出了相应的第一性原理实现方案，该方案可无缝集成到现有的电子-声子耦合与声子-声子耦合相互作用评估工作流程中。随后在多种材料体系中进行了实际计算验证。

Result: 在多种材料中成功计算了包含声子退相的电子-声子散射率，识别并讨论了几种非谐声子影响电子-声子耦合的不同微观机制，证实了有限声子寿命在电子-声子耦合评估中的重要性。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了有限声子寿命在电子-声子耦合评估中的关键作用，构建了一个可在广泛材料和现象中探索这些效应的计算平台，为更精确的材料物性预测提供了新途径。

Abstract: Electron-phonon coupling has been a central topic in condensed matter physics for decades, and firstprinciples methods have demonstrated remarkable success in quantitatively capturing its role in a wide variety of physical phenomena and materials. Conventional calculations of electron-phonon coupling typically assume that phonons have infinite lifetimes, but phonons can exhibit finite lifetimes due to anharmonic phonon-phonon interactions. In this work, we derive an expression for the electron-phonon coupling scattering rates including the effects of anharmonic three-phonon interactions, which lead to phonon dephasing and finite phonon lifetimes. We also describe a first-principles implementation of this anharmonic electron-phonon coupling which can be seamlessly integrated within existing workflows for the evaluation of electron-phonon and phonon-phonon coupling interactions. Finally, we present calculations of electron-phonon scattering rates including phonon dephasing in a range of materials, and discuss the different microscopic mechanisms by which anharmonic phonons influence electron-phonon coupling. This study establishes the importance of finite phonon lifetimes in the evaluation of electron-phonon coupling, and provides a platform to explore these effects in a wide range of materials and phenomena.

</details>


### [132] [Electronic conductivity in anharmonic crystals: Phonon dephasing in the electron-phonon interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09050)
*Mingran Kong,Bartomeu Monserrat*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套电子与有限寿命（退相干）声子相互作用的完整理论，并通过第一性原理实现证明非谐退相干效应会显著增强MgB2中的电子-声子散射率，从而抑制电导率，使计算结果更接近实验值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有所有第一性原理电子-声子相互作用计算均假设声子具有无限寿命，这一近似在非谐声子-声子相互作用较强时会失效，可能导致电子输运性质的计算出现显著偏差。

Method: 提出电子与经历退相干的有限寿命声子相互作用的理论框架，并将其以第一性原理方式实现，引入声子退相干对电子-声子散射率的修正。

Result: 在金属MgB2中，非谐退相干显著增强电子-声子散射率，通过声子-声子相互作用开辟新的散射通道，扩大散射相空间，强烈抑制电导率，使玻尔兹曼输运方程框架下的计算值与实验更吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了有限声子寿命在电子-声子散射评估中的重要性，揭示了非谐退相干在许多金属电导率中的潜在关键作用，为在其他材料中探索这一机制提供了坚实的理论基础和第一性原理实现工具。

Abstract: The electron-phonon interaction underpins many material properties, for example, the conductivity of metals and the optoelectronic response of semiconductors. First-principles calculations of the electron-phonon interaction are a powerful tool to quantitatively describe many of these properties in increasingly complex materials. However, one key assumption of all calculations is that phonons have infinite lifetimes, an approximation that may break down when anharmonic phonon-phonon interactions are strong. In this work, we present a theory for the interaction of electrons with finite-lifetime phonons experiencing dephasing. Using a first-principles implementation of the theory, we find that anharmonic dephasing dramatically enhances electron-phonon scattering rates in metallic MgB2. Microscopically, phonon-phonon interactions create new scattering channels that increase the phase space available for electron-phonon scattering. As a result, anharmonic dephasing strongly suppresses conductivity in MgB2, bringing the calculated values substantially closer to experiment within the Boltzmann transport equation framework. This example establishes the importance of finite phonon lifetimes in the evaluation of electron-phonon scattering, and the microscopic mechanism suggests that anharmonic dephasing could play an important role in the conductivity of many metals. More broadly, our theory and first-principles implementation of anharmonic dephasing in the electron-phonon interaction provides a solid foundation to explore this regime in other materials.

</details>


### [133] [Altermagnetism-Induced Spin-resolved electronic structure in Janus FeX0.5Y0.5 Monolayers (X, Y = S, Se, Te)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09054)
*Mengyang Zhang,Jie Li,Yifei Chen,Shifang Li,Zhentao Fu,Jianxin Zhong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 预测了一系列由铁基超导体（FeSe、FeTe、FeS）衍生的Janus FeX0.5Y0.5单层结构，这些结构展现出显著的自旋劈裂电子态、大拓扑带隙（51.4 meV）和高的Néel温度（415 K），并可通过面内应变调控谷极化，为超导自旋电子学提供了基于交变磁性（altermagnetism）的新策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超导材料中实现自旋分辨的电子性质是凝聚态物理的前沿方向，既关乎新颖的基础物理，也为无耗散自旋基器件提供可能。作者旨在探索一种新的策略，在超导体系中工程化自旋劈裂态。

Method: 采用Kondo型模型与第一性原理计算，预测了由铁基超导体（FeSe、FeTe、FeS）衍生的系列Janus FeX0.5Y0.5单层结构，并通过计算分析其电子结构、拓扑带隙、Néel温度及外加面内应变下的谷极化行为。

Result: 研究得出：这些Janus结构具有显著的自旋劈裂电子态，大拓扑带隙（51.4 meV）和高的Néel温度（415 K）。面内应变可有效调控谷极化，且谷极化的反常霍尔电导可通过移动费米能级进行操控。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种基于交变磁性在超导体系中工程化自旋劈裂态的新策略，为超导自旋电子学的进一步探索提供了思路和启发。

Abstract: Realizing the spin-resolved electronic properties in superconducting materials stands as a critical frontier, offering both novel fundamental physics and potential for dissipationless spin-based devices. Here, we predict a series of Janus FeX0.5Y0.5 monolayers derived from iron-based superconductors (e.g., FeSe, FeTe, and FeS) by using Kondo-type model and first-principles calculations. These Janus structures exhibit significant spin-splittingelectronic states, large topological band gaps (51.4 meV) and high Néel temperatures (415 K). We further reveal that valley polarization can be effectively tuned via applied in-plane strain and the resulting valley-polarized anomalous Hall conductivity can be manipulated by shifting the Fermi level. Our work suggests a new strategy based on altermagnetism for engineering spin-splitting states in superconducting systems and inspires further exploration of superconducting spintronics.

</details>


### [134] [Multitask Scanning Probe Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09104)
*Aditya Raghavan,Yu Liu,Ian Mercer,JP Maria,Sergei Kalinin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多任务扫描探针显微镜（multitask SPM）工作流，利用多任务高斯过程实时学习空间与跨模态关系，自主选择下一个测量位置和实验协议，实现高效的多模态空间分布测量。该系统在自动大样品原子力显微镜上，通过对AlScN组合材料晶圆进行tapping模式和DART模式的测量得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描探针显微镜在晶圆级表征和组合材料探索中需要高效的大空间域测量分配。不同模态的采集时间和对针尖/样品的损伤风险差异显著，导致全空间多模态穷尽式映射不切实际，亟需一种能自主分配测量模态的智能工作流。

Method: 将多任务高斯过程（multitask Gaussian process）嵌入实时闭环工作流，学习空间相关性及跨模态（任务间）相关性。算法同时确定下一个测量位置与下一个实验模态，成对的初始测量建立任务间关系，此后利用非重合测量同时更新两个响应图谱。该方法在自动大样品原子力显微镜上实现，并采用tapping模式与DART模式对AlScN成分梯度晶圆进行验证。

Result: 成功演示了通过非重合测量更新多任务响应图谱的闭环主动学习流程，将扫描探针显微镜中的主动学习从单纯的空间采样扩展到测量模态的自主分配。该工作流能够将快速、弱扰动的成像模态与较慢的接触、电学、机电、磁学或光谱测量相结合。

Conclusion: 该工作为扫描探针显微镜在晶圆级和组合材料表征中的多模态自主测量提供了新范式，为结合快速低扰动成像与慢速高信息量模态的分布式测量奠定了基础。

Abstract: Scanning probe microscopy provides nanoscale access to structural, electrical, electromechanical, magnetic, and mechanical properties of materials. Its increasing use for wafer-scale characterization and combinatorial materials exploration creates a need to distribute measurements efficiently across large spatial domains. This is particularly important when available modalities differ in acquisition time and potential for tip and sample damage, making exhaustive multimodal mapping over spatial grids impractical. Here, we demonstrate multitask scanning probe microscopy, a live, closed-loop workflow in which a multitask Gaussian process learns spatial and cross-modal relationships and autonomously selects both the next measurement location and the next experimental protocol. The approach is implemented on an automated large-sample atomic force microscope and demonstrated on a composition-spread AlScN wafer using tapping-mode and Dual AC Resonance Tracking (DART) measurements. Paired initial measurements establish the relation between the tasks, after which noncoincident measurements are used to update both response landscapes. The resulting workflow extends active learning in scanning probe microscopy from spatial sampling to autonomous allocation of measurement modalities and provides a basis for combining rapid, weakly perturbative imaging with slower contact, electrical, electromechanical, magnetic, or spectroscopic measurements.

</details>


### [135] [All-Electron Single-Atom Reference Correction for Absolute Transition Energies in Fixed-Reference PAW-XCH Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09216)
*Yinan Wang,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文评估了VASP中PAW方法计算核心损失谱（core-loss spectrum）绝对跃迁能量时的原子参考修正问题。通过自洽单原子计算构建残余原子参考修正，与独立超软赝势实现对比验证了K、L1、L2,3核心空穴的全电子参考数值一致性，修正后的光谱接近实验绝对能量标度。同元素化学位移由超胞总能量的双重差分决定，且Al和Si的L2,3边近边谱形强烈依赖于PAW对低能3d类未占据态的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度泛函理论计算普遍低估核心损失谱的绝对跃迁能量。VASP中基态和核心激发态使用同一PAW数据集，当核心占据数变化时保留了基态原子参考，忽略了相关的全电子原子参考变化，导致绝对跃迁能量系统性偏差。

Method: 利用自洽单原子计算评估PAW方法中核心占据数变化引起的全电子原子参考变化，从价态参考变化和孤立原子总能响应构建残余原子参考修正；通过与独立超软赝势实现的对比验证数值一致性；分析同元素化学位移中原子参考项的抵消情况。

Result: 残余原子参考修正与独立超软赝势实现在K、L1、L2,3核心空穴上表现出数值一致的全电子参考；修正后的代表性光谱接近实验绝对能量标度。对固定元素、边和核心空穴方案，原子参考项在能量差中抵消，同元素化学位移由超胞总能量的双重差分控制。Al和Si的L2,3边近边谱形严重依赖PAW对低能3d类未占据态的表示。

Conclusion: 通过构建残余原子参考修正可以弥合DFT计算与实验绝对跃迁能量之间的差距；同元素化学位移不受原子参考项影响，但近边谱形对PAW中未占据态的表示敏感，需谨慎处理低能3d类态的赝势描述。

Abstract: The transition energy of a core-loss spectrum comprises a transferable atomic core-reference contribution and a material-dependent response that determines chemical shifts. Density functional theory calculations commonly underestimate absolute transition energies. In VASP, the same PAW dataset is used for the ground and core-excited occupations, retaining the ground-state atomic reference when the core occupation changes and thereby omitting the associated all-electron atomic reference change. We evaluate this change using self-consistent single-atom calculations and construct a residual atomic reference correction from the valence reference change and isolated-atom total-energy response. Comparisons with an independent ultrasoft-pseudopotential implementation demonstrate numerical consistency of the all-electron reference for K, L1, and L2,3 core holes, while representative corrected spectra approach the experimental absolute energy scale. For a fixed element, edge, and core-hole scheme, the atomic reference terms cancel from energy differences, showing that same-element chemical shifts are governed by the double difference of the supercell total energy. At the Al and Si L2,3 edges, the near-edge spectral shape additionally depends strongly on the PAW representation of low-lying 3d-like unoccupied states.

</details>


### [136] [Machine-learning octet $AB$-type binary compounds across chemical space with domain knowledge of the interatomic bond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09257)
*Rohan Kumar,Mariano D. Forti,Aakash A. Naik,Luca M. Ghiringhelli,Thomas Hammerschmidt*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出将化学键领域的显式知识编码为紧束缚键模型的递归特征，用于改进八电子AB型二元化合物在闪锌矿（β-ZnS）与岩盐（NaCl）结构间稳定性（即结构形成能差）的预测。相比仅使用原子特征的以往方法，该"键信息"表示显著提升了核岭回归和符号回归模型的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测八电子AB型二元化合物的结构稳定性是经典的材料信息学问题，其难点在于捕捉4配位（闪锌矿）与6配位（岩盐）结构的相对稳定性，这受电荷转移和原子尺寸差异的调控。以往的结构图和机器学习方法仅使用价电子数、电离势、原子半径等原子级特征，未能显式纳入原子间成键的领域知识。

Method: 通过紧束缚键模型的递归求解，获得局部电子结构的粗粒度表示，从而将化学键信息编码为特征。键参数（成对哈密顿量）取自密度泛函理论二原子分子计算的降维本征态，因此包含了特定A-B对的成键领域知识。将这些键信息特征与核岭回归或符号回归模型结合，并采用序列特征选择，与使用相同从头计算训练集的先前符号回归模型进行比较。

Result: 与以往工作相比，键信息增强的模型在预测AB化合物形成能差方面取得了显著改进，并证明了增加键信息递归特征的数量可以持续提升预测精度。

Conclusion: 显式纳入化学键的领域知识（而非仅依赖原子特征）可以系统性地改进八电子二元化合物结构稳定性的机器学习预测，为材料信息学中结合物理知识模型与数据驱动方法提供了有效范例。

Abstract: The prediction of the structural stability of octet $AB$-type binary compounds is a classical materials informatics problem. The challenge is to capture the relative stability of 4-fold coordinated atoms in zincblende ($β$-ZnS) structure and 6-fold coordinated atoms in rocksalt (NaCl) structure, modulated by charge transfer and atomic-size differences. Previous structure maps and machine-learning approaches used atomic features such as valence-electron count, ionization potential and atomic radii, using either physical intuition or symbolic regression. Here, we demonstrate that explicitly incorporating the domain knowledge of the interatomic bonds can significantly and systematically improve the prediction of $β$-ZnS/NaCl stability. We encode this bonding information through a coarse-grained representation of the local electronic structure obtained by a recursive solution of a tight-binding bond model. The underlying pairwise Hamiltonians are taken from downfolded eigenstates of density-functional theory calculations for diatomic molecules and thereby include domain knowledge of the bond between specific $A-B$ pairs. The benefit of this description is demonstrated with an ensemble of independently trained Kernel Ridge or symbolic regression models combined with sequential feature selection. The obtained models are compared to a previous symbolic-regression model using the same set of \emph{ab initio} calculations for octet binaries as training data. We find a significant improvement in the prediction of the formation energy difference of $AB$ compounds as compared to previous works and demonstrate that an increasing amount of bond-informed recursion features improves the predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [137] [Preserved metallicity and tunable magnetism in Zr-based Janus MXenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09645)
*S. Özcan,B. Biel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算系统研究了Janus ZrMCX2 MXenes（M = Cr, Hf, Nb, Sc, Ti；X = F, Cl, S）的结构、电子、力学和磁学性质。所有组分均具有负形成能和Born稳定性，绝大多数体系保持金属性，多个化合物表现出铁磁/反铁磁基态，三个显示半金属性，部分体系的Néel温度超过室温，且功能化增强了弹性刚度，表明化学不对称性和表面终止可有效调控磁学和力学性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Janus MXenes作为一种化学不对称的二维过渡金属碳化物平台，具有通过打破表面对称性来定制材料性质的潜力。然而目前对其结构稳定性、电子结构和磁性等系统性认识仍然不足，尤其是功能性表面终端的调控作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用第一性原理（密度泛函理论）系统计算方法，对五类过渡金属（Cr, Hf, Nb, Sc, Ti）和三类表面官能团（F, Cl, S）构成的Janus ZrMCX2体系进行了全面的性质表征，包括形成能计算、Born力学稳定性判据、电子结构分析、磁序与磁各向异性能以及Néel温度评估。

Result: 所有Janus结构均具有负形成能和力学稳定性；不同于以往报道的金属-半导体转变，几乎所有体系在强化学不对称下仍保持金属特性。多种化合物具有铁磁或反铁磁基态，三个体系表现出半金属性，若干体系具有显著的磁各向异性能且Néel温度超过室温，同时表面功能化增强了弹性刚度。

Conclusion: 化学不对称性（Janus构型）和表面官能团选择为调控二维MXenes的磁学和力学性质提供了有效手段，同时保持稳定的金属框架。Zr基Janus MXenes被确认为有前景的金属性及自旋相关二维材料候选体系。

Abstract: Janus MXenes provide a chemically asymmetric platform to tailor the properties of two-dimensional transition metal carbides. Here, we present a systematic first-principles study of the structural, electronic, mechanical, and magnetic properties of Janus ZrMCX2 MXenes (M = Cr, Hf, Nb, Sc, Ti; X = F, Cl, S). All compositions exhibit negative formation energies and satisfy the Born stability criteria, supporting the energetic and mechanical stability of the Janus structures. In contrast to the metal-tosemiconductor transitions frequently reported for functionalized MXenes, we find that the metallic character is preserved in nearly all ZrMCX2 systems, despite the strong chemical asymmetry introduced by the Janus configuration. Surface functionalization nevertheless plays a key role in modulating the electronic density near the Fermi level and strongly influences the magnetic and mechanical responses. Several compounds exhibit ferromagnetic or antiferromagnetic ground states, three display halfmetallicity, and sizable magnetic anisotropy energies are obtained, with Neel temperatures exceeding room temperature in selected cases. Functionalization also enhances the elastic stiffness of the Janus structures. These results demonstrate that chemical asymmetry and surface termination provide effective control over magnetic and mechanical properties while maintaining a robust metallic framework, identifying Zr-based Janus MXenes as promising model systems for metallic and spindependent two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [138] [Predictive Simulation of Interphases on Li Metal Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09791)
*Xinyu Li,Jingxuan Ding,Daniel C. Hannah,Yumin Zhang,Qichao Hu,Kang Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合通用可极化力场和通用机器学习力场的仿真方法，能够普遍预测锂金属表面形成的界面相化学特性，无需依赖经验的"爱迪生式"试错方法。该方法成功复现了氟化溶剂促进LiF富集界面相形成、碳酸酯类溶剂产生有机界面相的实验观察，建立了电解质化学与SEI性质之间的分子级关系，为AI驱动的电解质发现奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 界面相是先进电池中最不为人所理解的组成部分，其性质决定了新电池化学能否按设计运行。然而由于缺乏对界面形成过程的原子级认识，从未有过可靠方法预测新电解质体系会产生何种界面相，导致电解质工程依赖冗长的试错方法。

Method: 将可迁移的通用可极化力场与通用机器学习力场相结合，构建了能够跨化学多样性电解质配方模拟界面相化学的仿真框架，直接捕获电解质与锂金属表面的自发界面反应。

Result: 成功复现了实验观察结果：氟化溶剂促进LiF富集型界面相的形成，而传统碳酸酯基电解质则产生更多有机来源的界面相。模拟直接捕获了这些自发的界面反应过程。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了电解质化学、盐浓度、分解路径与SEI性能之间的分子级关系，开辟了普遍化、高通量的界面相预测模拟路径，是AI驱动电解质发现的基础。

Abstract: Interphases remain the least understood components in advanced batteries. Although their properties dictate whether a new battery chemistry could perform as designed, there has never been a reliable way to predict what an interphase could arise from a new electrolyte system due to the absence of atomistic level knowledge about interphasial formation process. In this work, we attempt to develop a simulation method that can universally predict interphasial chemistries formed on Li metal surface, so that the electrolyte engineering would no longer need lengthy Edisonian approaches. By combining a transferable universal polarizable force field and a universal machine learning force field, we simulate interphasial chemistry across chemically diverse electrolyte formulations, and successfully replicate the experimental observation that fluorinated solvents promote the formation of LiF-rich interphases, whereas interphases of more organic origin arise from conventional carbonate-based electrolytes. By directly capturing these spontaneous interfacial reactions behind these interphasial chemistries, our simulations establish molecular-level relationships between electrolyte chemistry, salt concentration, decomposition pathways, and SEI properties, and opens a route toward universal and high-throughput predictive simulation of interphases that is the foundation for AI-driven electrolyte discoveries.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Oxygen Reduction Reaction on Platinum Nanocatalysts Produces Long-Lived, Hysteretic Oxygenated Adsorbates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08050)
*Jaehyeon Kim,Lalith Krishna Samanth Bonagiri,Fujia Zhao,Yingjie Zhang*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过原位拉曼光谱技术，追踪了碱性介质中含活性铂表面的纳米颗粒催化剂上氧还原反应（ORR）所生成的三种关键含氧中间体（吸附态OOH、OH和O2），发现这些吸附物种具有高度长寿命和滞后性，即使在ORR终止后仍能持续存在，揭示了真实催化剂表面吸附结构的复杂性远超单晶模型表面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然单晶催化剂表面的中间物种已被观测到，但工业相关纳米颗粒催化剂上表面含氧物种的性质和演化仍不清楚，这严重限制了人们对真实电催化反应机理的理解。

Method: 采用原位拉曼光谱技术，在Ar与O2饱和电解质条件下进行对比，系统追踪了碱性介质中活性Pt表面纳米颗粒催化剂上含氧吸附物种的形成及随电位变化的演化过程，并考察了表面氧化状态和电解质阳离子（K+ vs Li+）的影响。

Result: 确定了ORR产生的三种关键中间体：吸附态OOH、OH和O2。与"中间体仅在催化反应期间存在"的传统认知相反，这些含氧吸附物种具有高度长寿命且表现出滞后特征，在ORR结束后仍持续存在。这种吸附物保留效应受表面氧化态和电解质阳离子影响较小，可能是由催化剂表面的异质性所促成。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了实际纳米催化剂表面吸附结构的复杂性远超单晶模型表面测量或模拟所捕捉的范围，为理解真实电催化体系中的表面吸附行为提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Aqueous electrocatalysis generates oxygenated intermediates at catalyst surfaces. While intermediate species on single-crystal catalysts have been observed, the nature and evolution of surface oxygenated species on industrially relevant nanoparticle (NP) catalysts remain largely unknown. Here, using in situ Raman spectroscopy, we tracked the formation and potential-dependent evolution of oxygenated adsorbates in alkaline media on NP catalysts with an active platinum (Pt) surface. By comparing spectroscopic features in Ar- vs O2-saturated electrolytes, we determined three key intermediates produced by the oxygen reduction reaction (ORR): adsorbed OOH, OH, and O2. In contrast to the conventional wisdom that intermediates exist only during catalytic reactions, we found these oxygenated adsorbates to be highly long-lived and hysteretic, and to persist even after the termination of ORR. This adsorbate-retention effect exhibits a modest dependence on the surface oxidation state and the electrolyte cations (K+ vs Li+), and is likely facilitated by the heterogeneous nature of the catalyst surface. The results highlight the complexity of surface adsorption structures on realistic catalysts, which often extends beyond that captured by measurements or simulations on model single-crystal surfaces.

</details>


### [140] [Chemical potentials from structure factors: I. Neutral multi-component mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08357)
*Roya Savoj,Xiaoyu Wang,Musahid Ahmed,Bingqing Cheng*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文将S0方法从二元混合物推广到中性多元混合物，结合高斯过程积分与主动学习策略，可高效地在高维组分空间中计算化学势、混合自由能和溶解度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组分混合物的化学势是许多物理和化学现象的基础，但计算具有挑战性。已有S0方法仅适用于二元混合物，缺乏对多组分体系的推广能力。

Method: 首先将统计力学形式推广到高维组分空间，然后引入结合主动学习的高斯过程积分方案，以高效积分化学势并对多样的组分进行采样。

Result: 该方法成功计算了熔融金属合金的混合自由能，以及水-乙醇溶剂中两种对乙酰氨基酚多晶型的溶解度。

Conclusion: 扩展后的S0方法为从原子模拟计算中性体相多元混合物的化学势提供了一条实用且可扩展的路径。

Abstract: The chemical potentials of multi-component mixtures underlie many physical and chemical phenomena, but remain challenging to compute. The S0 method enables the computation of chemical potentials from equilibrium molecular dynamics simulations, by leveraging the thermodynamic relationship between particle number fluctuations and derivatives of chemical potentials, followed by numerical integration along different compositions. Here we generalize the S0 method from two-component mixtures to neutral multi-component mixtures. We first extend the statistical mechanical formalism to high-dimensional compositional space, and then introduce a Gaussian process integration scheme combined with active learning to efficiently integrate chemical potentials and sample diverse compositions. We use this method to compute the mixing free energies of a molten metal alloy, and the solubilities of two paracetamol polymorphs in water-ethanol solvents. The extended S0 method provides a practical and scalable route for computing chemical potentials in neutral bulk multi-component mixtures from atomistic simulations.

</details>


### [141] [Finite-Temperature Spin-Adapted ROKS and TDDFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08454)
*Xiaoyu Zhang*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了有限温度下受限开壳层Kohn-Sham（ROKS）理论及其自旋适配含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）的扩展，通过构造基于高自旋整数ROKS组分的正则系综，在严格保持自旋对称性的前提下统一描述基态和激发态，并成功用于解释双自由基体系的热激活吸收现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子化学中有限温度条件是一个核心研究领域，但现有的密度泛函理论（DFT）在有限温度下尚无法同时一致地描述基态和激发态，同时严格保持与S²算符相关的自旋对称性。这一空缺限制了有限温度下对分子激发性质的理论描述能力。

Method: 作者开发了有限温度ROKS理论，通过使用具有相同自旋量子数|SS⟩的整数高自旋ROKS组分I构造正则系综，每个组分的权重w_I服从玻尔兹曼分布。对于有限温度自旋适配TDDFT，每个整数组分I提供一对普通的零温自旋适配TDDFT矩阵(M_I,N_I)，将这些矩阵按统一的空间轨道顺序排列并求平均。在零温单组分极限下，理论退化为普通的高自旋ROKS和相应的自旋适配TDDFT。

Result: 作者将该理论应用于双自由基体系（diradicaloid），利用计算得到的激发态信息成功解释了变温UV/Vis光谱中观测到的热激活吸收现象，验证了有限温度框架对实际体系热激发行为描述的准确性。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了有限温度下同时保持自旋对称性的ROKS和自旋适配TDDFT理论框架，为有限温度下基态和激发态的电子结构计算提供了统一且严格的方法，并展示了其在解释热激活光谱行为方面的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Finite-temperature conditions constitute a central regime of interest in quantum chemistry. Nonetheless, a consistent incorporation of finite-temperature effects into density functional theory (DFT) for both ground and excited states, while rigorously preserving the spin-symmetry associated with the $\hat{S}^2$ operator, has not yet been achieved. In this work, we develop a finite-temperature extension of restricted open-shell Kohn-Sham (ROKS) theory and spin-adapted time-dependent density functional theory (TDDFT), providing unified frameworks for the description of ground and excited states, respectively. For finite-temperature ROKS, we construct a canonical ensemble by using integer high-spin ROKS components, where each component $I$ has the same spin number $|SS \rangle$ and its weight $w_I$ follows the Boltzmann distribution. For finite-temperature spin-adapted TDDFT, every integer component $I$ supplies an ordinary zero-temperature spin-adapted TDDFT matrix pair $(\Mmat_I,\Nmat_I)$. These matrices are arranged in one unified spatial-orbital order and then averaged. In the zero-temperature single-component limit, the theory reduces to ordinary high-spin ROKS and the corresponding spin-adapted TDDFT. As a numerical application, we apply the theory to a diradicaloid and use the calculated excitations to interpret the thermally activated absorption observed in variable-temperature UV/Vis spectroscopy.

</details>


### [142] [Self-consistent double-hybrid density functional theory via one-body second-order Møller-Plesset perturbation theory and projection-based embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08784)
*Huy Gia Bui,Lan Nguyen Tran*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自洽的单体双杂化密度泛函理论（OBDH），将单体二阶Møller-Plesset（OBMP2）微扰势直接嵌入广义Kohn-Sham框架中，使轨道能够在MP2级动态相关作用下优化，无需有效势或微扰轨道弛豫校正。通过聚簇局域化截断的投影嵌入法（sub-OBDH）降低计算成本，并在多种基准测试中优于标准DFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双杂化泛函在轨道优化后需要额外的微扰校正或优化有效势，计算复杂且缺乏自洽性。作者旨在开发一种无需这些额外步骤、天然自洽的单体双杂化DFT方法，以在保持精度的同时简化计算流程。

Method: 该方法将OBMP2微扰势直接嵌入广义Kohn-Sham框架，能量泛函由半局域交换关联泛函、精确交换和OBMP2相关能组成，并系统推导了有效哈密顿量和自洽场方程。为降低计算成本，对OBMP2分量采用基于投影嵌入和同心局域化截断的sub-OBDH方案。

Result: OBDH和sub-OBDH在双原子势能曲线、自相互作用误差、有机分子二面角扭转以及非共价带电体系相互作用能等基准测试中，均一致优于标准DFT。

Conclusion: OBDH作为一种准确且实用的电子结构计算新方法，在无需优化有效势或微扰轨道弛豫校正的情况下，显著提升了DFT的精度，具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: We present the development of self-consistent one-body double-hybrid (OBDH) density functional theory (DFT). In this approach, the one-body second-order Møller-Plesset (OBMP2) perturbation potential is embedded directly in the generalized Kohn-Sham framework, allowing orbitals to be optimized in the presence of MP2-level dynamic correlation. Unlike existing orbital-optimized double hybrids, OBDH requires neither the optimized effective potential nor perturbative orbital relaxation corrections. The energy functional combines a semilocal exchange-correlation functional, exact exchange, and OBMP2 correlation, from which the effective Hamiltonian and self-consistent-field equations are systematically derived. To reduce computational cost, projector-based embedding with concentric localization truncation is applied to the OBMP2 component, termed sub-OBDH. OBDH and sub-OBDH are benchmarked on diatomic potential energy curves, self-interaction errors, dihedral torsions of organic molecules, and interaction energies in non-covalent charged systems. Across all systems, OBDH consistently outperforms standard DFT, demonstrating its potential for accurate and practical electronic structure calculations.

</details>
